# state.md — What's True Right Now

> **Authority level**: Volatile. This is the only doc that changes weekly.  
> **Audience**: You (to know what's live and what's broken) and Claude Code agents (to avoid building on broken foundations or duplicating what exists).  
> **Maintenance rule**: Update this doc before every merge to main. Use `/ship` or update manually. If a section's "last verified" date is more than 7 days old, treat it as suspect.  
> **Related docs**: system.md covers how the architecture works. intent.md covers what we're building and why. rules.md covers what never changes.

---

## Deployed Features
*Last verified: 2026-07-15*

**Live features at a glance** (audit-only catalog of what's working end-to-end across all surfaces; full ship log below):

- Public marketing landing at `/` (editorial Bloom page); the app lives at `/app`. Contact form → waitlist capture; admin-only "new signups" badges (in-app + admin nav); SEO with generated OG image, robots, sitemap. Middleware now active at `src/middleware.ts`.
- Auth: magic link + Google OAuth, middleware redirect (now enforced via `src/middleware.ts`), session refresh
- Onboarding: `/login` renders the login form directly (entry splash deleted 2026-06-10); first-time onboarding runs post-login via PostLoginOnboarding as a single consent screen (the merged "what this is, and isn't" prose + affirmative provenance + age gate); the old info → persona → seed three-screen sequence was collapsed and the persona-mode pick dropped (2026-06-17); skip for returning users. After intake, a brand-new user lands on **Home** (the front-door launchpad), not the old in-session entry screen — that 3-card "What's on your mind today?" screen was **retired 2026-06-18** (ADR-049); Home's "ways to begin" carry all three modes. (Open follow-up: the just-in-time "Nothing's saved unless you say so" control line lived on those retired cards — a new user still sees the promise on the consent screen, but moving it onto Home for the returning-user "+ New session" case is a flagged, unmade decision.)
- Streaming chat with Jove: SSE, batch rendering, retry on error
- Sliding window: first 2 + last 48 when > 50 messages
- Checkpoint pipeline: user pulls the reflection bar → `/api/checkpoint/compose` → server-side Opus composition → editable overlay → confirm. A **live admin toggle** on the Feature gates page (`composer` default / `conductor` / `compare`) selects who writes the entry: `composer` (`composeManualEntry` re-reads the transcript), `conductor` (`composeEntryAsConductor` — the conductor writes it from full live context), or `compare` (both, shown side by side for the founder to pick). Resolved by `getComposerMode` (admin toggle → `COMPOSER_MODE` env fallback → default). A/B scaffolding — see ship log.
- Checkpoint cards: inline confirm/reject/refine UI
- Manual building: upsert to `manual_entries`, manual tab with markdown rendering
- Bottom nav (mobile, since the 2026-06-17 front-door redesign): 4 tabs — **Home · Manual · Talk · You** — replacing the retired slide-out drawer. Home is the new landing for returning users. (Desktop keeps its sidebar.)
- Session summary: Haiku, fire-and-forget on stale sessions (> 30 min)
- Session history (mobile): the recent-conversations list now lives on the **Home** screen ("Show all" for older threads) — the slide-out drawer was retired in the 2026-06-17 front-door redesign. (Desktop keeps its sidebar session list.) **Regression fixed (2026-06-18):** Guided intake + Upload are reachable for returning users again, and the fix also repaired `startConversation`'s `messages.length > 0` guard, which had silently no-op'd *every* Home start (including "Bring a situation" itself) for anyone with an auto-resumed thread. *(All three modes are now equal "ways to begin" doors via the shared `WaysToBegin` component — rendered as compact horizontal rows on mobile since 2026-06-23, replacing the original one-primary-button-plus-two-secondary-links card.)*
- Desktop shell (viewports >= 1030px): persistent sidebar (collapsible to an icon rail) with **Home · Your Manual (count)** nav rows + a bordered **New session** action (an action, styled distinct from the destination rows) + session-history list + settings / logout (the **Crisis support** footer link was removed 2026-06-24 — crisis lives in Settings → SUPPORT) + a collapsible **Admin tools** disclosure (admin-only; dev-tools panel + admin-page links, collapsed by default since 2026-06-23 so the heavy panel no longer bloats the drawer); room header. **Nav cleanup (2026-06-24, duplicate-pathway audit):** the generic **"Conversation"** nav row was cut (it had no information scent and duplicated the highlighted active-session row + Home's resume hero) — the active session's row in the history list is now the strengthened "back to the conversation" anchor (walnut spine + stronger fill, distinct from hover); the sidebar's quick **Theme** cycle was also removed (Theme lives in Settings → Appearance only). Mobile bottom nav untouched. Room header (2026-07-07): the **wordmark is now centered** (absolutely, so the right-side session meta + feedback never shift it); the running view title moved to the left slot for Manual / Settings / scoped "Going deeper" views and is **dropped entirely on the session view** (the session name no longer sits in the top center). One-view-at-a-time center capped at a 720px reading measure. Since the **2026-06-18 desktop front-door pass (ADR-048)**, desktop has a **native Home** (greeting + slim resume ribbon + a 3-card "ways to begin" triptych — Bring a situation / Guided / Upload — + the 5-section "go deeper" index) and **returning users land on it** (the old `home→session` coercion is gone); the Manual's share sheet renders as a **centered modal** on desktop. Home + Manual reuse the same `useHomeModel` + `LayerIndex` + `WaysToBegin` doors as mobile (no forked components; desktop keeps the 3-across vertical door cards while mobile renders the same doors as compact rows); since 2026-06-23 the 5-section index renders inside a white Home tile. **Mid-width (431–1029px)** is now a **centered phone-width column** (the phone-frame vitrine is retired for signed-in users — `/login` keeps it), and the scoped **"Going deeper · {section}"** context shows in the room header. The `DesktopShell` cutover stays at 1030px.
- Theming: two themes ship — light **"Slate & Brass"** (cool stone ground + graphite ink + aged-brass accent; the **new default** since 2026-06-11) and the unchanged dark **"Hearth"**. The Theme control (light / dark / system) lives in Settings → Appearance (the desktop sidebar's quick Theme cycle was removed 2026-06-24 as a duplicate). All surfaces are token-driven (`--session-*` in `globals.css`), one block per theme; chat, sidebar, Manual, and checkpoint are all hand-polished to the Slate & Brass treatment. The Manual was rebuilt in Home's white-tile language (2026-06-25): each of the five sections is a cream-bright tile (hairline border + soft card shadow + navy "started" / walnut "empty" section emblem) that expands to its entry cards (cream, nested inside the tile), with a mono count line under the title and the share block as its own tile — the old editorial layout (brass Roman-numerals, per-layer hairline rules, the § ornament, the "About this layer" popover) was retired so the Manual and Home read as one product. `MobileManual` is the single source for mobile + the desktop shell; `AdminManualView` reuses the same tiles in readOnly mode; the shared tile shell lives in `SECTION_TILE_STYLE`. The in-chat checkpoint is a calm inline `Plate` preview that sits in the conversation flow (section eyebrow + headline + a one-sentence teaser + "Tap to review →", reusing the historical-checkpoint Plate) — not a glowing spotlight; tapping opens the review overlay for the full entry + Add/Edit/Discard. The overlay is the restored quieter presentation (left-aligned 22px Newsreader headline, no letterpress frame / brass-rule press) at a **480px** reading width, and confirming shows a single full-screen save cover (Adding… → Added). *(The glowing letterpress trigger, the centered Fraunces display headline + brass-rule "press", and the separate inline "Adding to your Manual…" line were all removed 2026-06-25 — see ship log.)* (Known remaining warm spot: the desktop vitrine marketing frame around login/landing still carries hardcoded warm colors.)
- Dev simulate: Desktop sidebar → Admin tools (the `DevToolsPanel`) → "Run live simulation" → drives a fake user through the **real app** live (client-driven, not server-side as of 2026-06-30 / `17a8418`). The fake user goes through the genuine path — `startConversation(mode)` opener, then per-turn replies through `sendMessage`/`sendChipResponse`, tapping the section picker and focus chips when present — so all live affordances render on screen. Intake mode (situation / guided-intake / upload) is selectable per-run; **persona comes from the signed-in account** (the real path — set it in Settings), no per-run override. Stops at the first entry-ready moment (`activeCheckpoint` for auto-propose, `reflectionReady` for the pulled meter), leaving it pending for the user to confirm; the fake user can also emit `[END]`, and there's a 40-turn cap. *(The duplicate inline copy in mobile Settings was removed 2026-06-25; `DevToolsPanel` is the single dev-tools surface. The driver lives in `useChat` (`runLiveSimulation`); the panel dispatches `dev-tools:run-live-simulation`, `MainApp` runs it and broadcasts `dev-tools:live-sim-ended`; the one-fake-turn endpoint is `/api/dev-simulate/turn`. The old server-side `/api/dev-simulate` route + `dev-tools:simulation-event` bridge were deleted.)*
- Dev reset (Settings → "delete user data", dev-only): deletes the user's conversations / messages / manual_entries / feedback, clears localStorage + sessionStorage (sessionStorage added 2026-07-02 — it held `mw_active_conversation` pointing at deleted rows), AND resets the profile's new-user gate (`onboarding_completed_at` → null) so the account presents as a fresh **logged-in** new user — the consent screen replays, and `isNewUser` flips true from zero conversations. Auth row + other profile prefs (persona, phone) untouched. (Does NOT reproduce the anonymous-guest path — that needs a fresh anon session, not a reset of a real account.) *(The `modal_progress` reset was dropped 2026-07-01 with the pattern-forming onboarding modal — see ship log.)*
- Crisis protocol: detects crisis language, provides 988 + Crisis Text Line
- Voice input (Deepgram): auto-scroll during transcription, 3.5-line max height. **Single-pipeline guarantee (2026-07-08, merge `a392b11f`):** `startRecording` latches on `activeRef` so re-entrant taps during the ~1–2s setup are no-ops — fixes the duplicated-line bug where each extra tap spun up its own mic stream + socket + transcriber, all appending finals to the same transcript (a phrase landed 2–3×), and left orphaned live mic streams that stop couldn't reach. Also: the button flips to "stop" instantly on tap (new `"starting"` state), token fetch runs in parallel with `getUserMedia`, and the MediaRecorder starts immediately and buffers chunks until the socket opens so words spoken right after the tap aren't dropped. `ChatInput` ignores toggle taps <400ms apart. Verified in preview against live Deepgram: 3 rapid taps → exactly 1 token fetch + 1 socket; button flips in ~17ms.
- Guest-to-real auth conversion after first checkpoint
- Admin panel: user list (sorted by `last_active`), conversation/message viewer with extraction state, access logging, time markers, and a per-transcript **rubric score panel**. Left nav rail grouped **Operate** (Users / Beta / Feedback / Health) · **Controls** (Tuning · Feature gates) · **Learn** (how-it-works, extraction + schema maps, source docs, agents & skills, vendors). The Beta tab is one filterable/sortable signup list — status `invited` IS beta access. **Tuning** (`/admin/prompt-architecture`) holds everything that shapes Jove's voice + capture copy — the conductor prompt, the composer entry bar, openers, app copy, the intake doors — plus the **session-score trend chart** and the **editable scoring rubric**. **Feature gates** (`/admin/feature-gates`, moved out of Health 2026-07-08) are the four system debug kill-switches — `situation` / `guided_intake` / `upload` / `extraction_brief`, all default-ON, flip one off to isolate the core loop — plus the **composer-mode A/B selector** (Composer / Conductor / Compare; see ship log). **Health** is diagnostics-only (confirm health, API errors, active users, schema).
- Exploration mode: "Explore with Jove" from manual entries
- Transcript recognition: regex detects pasted transcripts (iMessage, email, journal, timestamped chat), Jove asks for context and cross-references manual.
- PWA: web app manifest, icons, standalone display, service worker (offline fallback, stale-while-revalidate for static assets, network-only for API), update prompt.
- Per-user daily message cap: Postgres-backed, default 200 user-authored messages per UTC day (env-overridable). Server-side gate at `/api/chat`; returns 429 with friendly client message and drops the optimistic user bubble. Skipped on server-triggered openers (message:null). Fails open on Supabase query error.
- Per-row OTP brute-force cap: `phone_numbers.otp_attempts` integer column, capped at `OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5` in `src/lib/phone-otp.ts`. Verify route returns 429 `too_many_attempts` once the cap is hit; increments on every wrong code; resets to 0 on successful verify. Send route resets on every fresh issuance so the legitimate "I typed wrong; send me a new code" recovery path is never locked. Layered with Upstash phone-verify rate limiter (currently failing open in prod) for burst protection if ever provisioned.
- Voice persona picker in Settings: "How Jove talks to you" section in `/settings` lets users change persona modes anytime — same multi-select UX as onboarding (General / Autistic / ADHD / Dyslexic), inline, auto-save on toggle with optimistic update + error revert. Backed by `PATCH /api/user/persona-modes` (auth-gated, validated for shape + exclusivity). Default for new accounts is now `{general}` (flipped from `{autistic}` 2026-05-19 — see ship log entry below). Selecting "Dyslexic" also swaps serif font tokens to sans-serif (DM Sans) app-wide via `[data-persona-dyslexic="true"]` driven by `usePersonaDyslexicFont` — FOUC-safe, cross-tab synced, no new font dependency.

**Ship log** (newest first):

- **Landing page: neurodivergent references removed — copy addresses the reader, not a named audience** (2026-07-15, commit `35d56cdb`): All ND references stripped from the public landing (`src/app/page.tsx`), its page/OG/Twitter metadata, and the generated share image (`opengraph-image.tsx` kicker "for neurodivergent adults" → "understand how you operate"). Hero lead + metadata now speak directly ("Mywalnut helps you put words to how you actually work"); "The gap" audience stanza ("built for late-diagnosed autistic adults… where ADHD and dyslexia overlap") → "built for people who have spent years working around that gap"; ND-coded vocabulary swept too ("Masking hides…" → "Keeping it together on the outside hides…"; hero-rotator "spicy" → "messy"). Softer rotator words (scattered / literal / wired / foggy) deliberately kept — everyday adjectives, founder can swap on request. App-internal ND surface (persona modes, dormant voice deltas) untouched. Browser-verified (landing copy + OG image render). BUILD 0 / VITEST 700/700.

- **Module force-delete — delete a module + its entries behind a typed-slug confirm** (2026-07-21, merge of claude/module-force-delete, built 2026-07-15): Plain Delete on /admin/modules still refuses while anything references the module, but the 409 now carries the blast radius and the panel stages a strong confirm — names the conversation + entry counts, warns the entries die for EVERY user, offers Disable as the gentle path, and only arms the destructive button once the founder types the slug back. The forced path deletes the module's manual_entries then the module row; conversations are never deleted (history kept; they fall back to the shared conductor). Verified live pre-merge (409 card with real counts, typed-slug arming, delete). Pure code, no schema changes.

- **MODULES CUTOVER — founder-authored doors that ARE the Manual's sections (ADR-053)** (2026-07-15, branch claude/entry-points-abstraction-8b6ad7): The three fixed entry doors AND the five fixed Manual sections are replaced by one abstraction: the **module** (`modules` table, new admin page `/admin/modules` under Controls). A module = a Home door (name/description/cue/icon + optional one-time intro + optional fixed server-emitted opener) + a Manual section + optionally its **own full Jove prompt** (blank = the live shared conductor; every custom prompt save-guarded by the same `CONDUCTOR_REQUIRED_FRAGMENTS`; "start from current conductor" button; fork-drift labeled). `conversations.mode` = module slug (validated against enabled modules at creation, **no default mode**); `manual_entries.section` = the conversation's module slug, **code-assigned at compose time** (the composer no longer picks among five sections, both A/B arms); tags trimmed to `{strength}`. Home is ONE list (module cards with entry counts + a read-your-manual link; `LayerIndex` deleted); Manual/PDF/admin group via `buildModuleGroups`; disabled modules keep their section while entries remain (disable ≠ delete; hard delete only while unreferenced). **Destructive reset (founder-approved): migration `20260715020000` wiped ALL manual_entries** — everyone starts blank; the founder creates the first modules in admin post-deploy (empty module set = Home shows no ways to begin, by design). Deleted: door registries, the 3 door feature gates (+rows; `extraction_brief` is the only gate left), ~14 door copy/override keys, Intake Doors panel + route, the dead guided section-picker plumbing (`---sections---`/`---start-situation---`, SectionPicker, chip-response path), `upload-copy.ts`, the dual message cap (one 16k cap; paste-wrap rides detection in any conversation). **Situation is SHELVED, not dead** (`situation-copy.ts` dormant — future product on the module structure). ⚠ Stale-reference note: older bullets in this catalog still describe the pre-cutover doors/five-sections (WaysToBegin trio, LayerIndex, "N of 5 started", intake-mode picker copy) — trust this entry + ADR-053 until the next full state audit. Follow-up cuts flagged: `availableOptions` in `/api/dev-simulate/turn` (unreachable), dev-populate fixtures still write legacy section slugs. BUILD 0 / VITEST 58 files green / verified in preview (empty-module Home, admin Modules page, graceful pre-migration API failure).

- **Reflection meter: unreachable "origin" depth rung removed (ladder re-spaced) + eval-tooling upgrades from the first full L-run session review** (2026-07-09, merge `c238d6f3`): The extraction depth ladder had a fifth rung — `origin` (where the pattern started), worth 80% of the bar — that Jove is explicitly forbidden to climb ("No excavating childhood or origins"), so the bar structurally parked at mechanism = 60% and read as stalled (founder report, L-run). **Extractor and conductor are now on the same page:** the ladder tops at mechanism, matching Jove's method — surface 0 / behavior 15 / feeling 40 / **mechanism 75** / 100 marker-only as before. Legacy states carrying `depth:"origin"` normalize to mechanism everywhere a stored depth is read (`normalizeDepth` in extraction.ts — single source for the alias) and self-heal on the next extraction write; pinned by tests. Extraction prompt, admin extraction-map page, and system.md updated to the 4-rung ladder. BUILD 0 / VITEST 708/708. **Same session, eval tooling** (from the first full `/evaluate` + blind applied-psychologist cross-check on a real founder session, conv `f418d61e`, filed as the L-run): `/evaluate` now requires pulling the live `persona_voice_overrides` prompt and scoring against it (the code constant produced false-violation risk); the rubric gains assent-by-trajectory (read the turns around a "yes," not its face), the E1 **landed ≠ resolved** rule (a recognition is keepable while the feeling is still live; a fire after energy fades is late — the L-run's user-forced fire is the anchor), and a productivity qualifier on the asymmetry rule; L-run exemplars + ledger notes 10–13 added, FIXED notes archived; the applied-psychologist agent def corrected after an SE audit (deleted tier system / `detect-checkpoint.ts` / dead alliance-signal references removed — `observation_miss_count`/`pattern_engaged` were deleted from extraction 2026-07-03 and the engine now logs NO alliance signal; added the live-override rule + a rubric-blind clause for independent cross-checks). **Context (DB, not code):** the founder's live conductor override got the "Landed doesn't mean resolved" offer-timing line the same day (validated save + audit history) — the under-firing fix the L-run surfaced. Watch: over-firing (thin/early offers) is the counter-risk; next few real sessions are the test.

- **Post-save fork gets a Jove-voiced tee-up + per-tile sublines** (2026-07-09, commit `21cfd2dd`): The three post-save tiles rendered bare — options with no framing after "Entry has been saved." Added a quiet left-aligned serif lead line above them (*"Where do you want to go from here?"* — reads as Jove's continuation, not a UI label) and a one-line explainer under each tile (Explore this thread more deeply → *stay with what we just found*; Discuss a different situation → *start fresh on something else*; Take a break → *pick it up whenever you want*). Both live in the **client** `PostSaveFork` with the tiles (uses `SelectionTile`'s existing `subtitle`; new token-driven `.mw-postsave-lead`), so they can never drift from the tile labels the way a prompt instruction would — the deliberate follow-through on the `---chips---` decoupling: no prompt/server change, nothing for a live override to desync. BUILD 0 / VITEST 57 files green. **PWA note:** the client bundle is service-worker cached — a hard refresh / reopen is needed to pull the new tile copy + tee-up.

- **Conductor v0.9 folded to code — writing-the-reflection lives in the prompt; consolidated entry spec on both A/B arms** (2026-07-09, commit `41e789ca`): Two coupled changes. **(1) The founder's v0.9 conductor rewrite is now the code floor** (`CONDUCTOR_PROMPT`): goal reframed as a CONNECTION the person draws ("arranging what they already gave you until the link is theirs to make"), the three rules unified under one priority order (feeling first, hand-over second, brevity last), hand-them-the-assembly in "Build it in the open," ongoing-state handling ("I always…" ≠ ask for "the last time"), sideways-interior questioning, rehearsal-breaking scene question, and the close rebuilt as "When there's something worth keeping" + "Saying it's available" (say once it's there, no lean; NOTHING about the reflection ever appears in chat — the never-draft rule moved into "What you never do"). "## Opening" dropped (founder call). Marker contract intact (availability line + `---reflection-ready---` travel together). **(2) The entry-writing spec consolidated** after a two-agent line-economics review (applied-psychologist + prompt-eng): ~560 → **323 words**, 10 → 6 bullets — the fidelity rule ("theirs, not yours") was carried by SIX lines, now one; every validator-backed guard kept (title rules, universal-tone, single-moment hedge, body-words-verbatim, strength-stays-a-strength). The spec now lives in **two deliberate places for the life of the A/B**: `COMPOSER_ENTRY_BAR` (composer mode) and the conductor prompt's new "## Writing the reflection" section (conductor mode — the compose call sends only the mode-flip + machine contract via new `buildEntryMachineContract`, so the model reads the standard once). Byte-identical, script-verified; change one → change both; loser's copy dies at winner-selection. "## Writing the reflection" added to `CONDUCTOR_REQUIRED_FRAGMENTS` so an admin edit can't silently drop conductor-mode's writing standard. Old "THE BAR / caught off guard" depth target retired from the written artifact (psych: a record PRESERVES a recognition, it never manufactures one — that load stays on the conversation). **⚠ Founder sequencing:** the live conductor override still carries the OLD writing section — paste v0.9 into Tuning; do NOT Reset the override until after this deploys (Reset before deploy = prod reverts to v0.8). BUILD 0 / VITEST 706/706 / tsc 0.

- **Retired/stray UI markers stripped before render — the `---chips---` leak killed for good + post-save fork copy** (2026-07-09, commit `a9119361`): The post-save fork went client-owned on 2026-07-08 (`PostSaveFork`) and the `---chips---` marker + its parser were deleted — but Jove's **live conductor-prompt override** (`persona_voice_overrides`, founder-edited) still instructed him to emit `---chips---` + three option lines. With no parser, that block **rendered as raw text** after every save, doubled-up beside the real client-drawn tiles. Verified live (devtest, full paid conversation to a real save): tiles correct, raw marker present. **Why a DB patch wasn't enough:** the override is live-edited; a surgical fix to it was overwritten by the founder's own tuning save within 11 minutes (audit trail in `persona_voice_override_history`). So the durable fix is **code-side** and prompt-agnostic — the render-time floor the SE recommended (two-layer model: prose is founder/DB-owned and may drift; machine contracts like markers are code-owned). New `RETIRED_MARKERS` registry + `stripDefunctMarkers` in `ui-markers.ts` removes any UI marker the product doesn't consume (retired like `---chips---` → cut its trailing payload to end; a stray/hallucinated bare `---word---` line → drop the line) from Jove's text before it's stored or shown. Wired into the **single** existing strip site in `call-persona.ts` (after the active-marker strip), so `cleanContent` (SSE) and the stored row both derive from one cleaned string — a stale live prompt can never leak a dead marker again. Retiring a marker = add it to `RETIRED_MARKERS`. Also: **`PostSaveFork` copy** → *"Discuss a different situation / Explore this thread more deeply / Take a break"* (founder wording). Proven: the exact stored chips message strips to the clean one-line acknowledgment. BUILD 0 / VITEST 57 files green (+5 `ui-markers.test.ts`). **Deferred by design (not blocking):** hard edit-rejection of a prompt that still contains a retired marker, and an admin drift banner — held so live tuning isn't blocked; registry is in place to add them later. **Founder action:** the live conductor-prompt override still carries the now-inert `---chips---` "After a save" block — harmless (stripped) but dead weight; clean it out of the Tuning page next edit.

- **Composer-mode A/B moved to a live admin toggle; `classic` renamed `composer`** (2026-07-08, commit `517586b3`): Follow-up to the `COMPOSER_MODE` ship below — flipping a Vercel env var meant a redeploy per flip, too clunky for iterating the test. The switch now lives on the **Feature gates page** as a Composer / Conductor / Compare selector (`ComposerModePanel` → `GET/PATCH /api/admin/composer-mode`) and takes effect **live, no deploy**. Renamed the mode `classic` → **`composer`** everywhere (value + label), per founder. Stored as a `composer_mode` row in `persona_voice_overrides` — reusing the generic override store's enabled/history/upsert machinery, so **no migration, no new table**; `getComposerMode` resolves admin-toggle → `COMPOSER_MODE` env (still a fallback) → default `composer`. The toggle is **global** (changes who composes for every user; Compare = 2× Opus per pull), stated on the panel. All still test-only scaffolding — the selector, route, `composer-mode.ts`, and the loser composer come out together at winner-selection. Browser-verified as devtest: panel renders on Feature gates, flip persists both ways (API + button), left on the safe `composer` default. BUILD 0 / VITEST 700/700 (new `composer-mode.test.ts` — resolution order + fail-safe) / tsc 0.

- **`COMPOSER_MODE` — conductor composes the entry on pull (A/B vs the classic composer)** (2026-07-08, commit `8fb0f7f6`): Testing a bet — instead of a separate composer re-reading the transcript from outside at save time, have **the conductor write the entry itself** from full live context. New env switch `COMPOSER_MODE` on `/api/checkpoint/compose`: **`classic`** (default, unchanged control), **`conductor`** (`composeEntryAsConductor` in `compose-as-conductor.ts` rebuilds the conductor's exact system prompt via `buildSystemPromptBlocks` — same cached voice + Manual + session context — replays the full session, then appends a final "write the entry now as JSON" instruction; drops the extraction supplements the classic path feeds since the conductor already holds the whole conversation), and **`compare`** (runs both in parallel, returns two candidates without writing a row; the client `CompareOverlay` shows both labeled A·Classic / B·Conductor, the founder picks one, and the pick echoes back via `pick` to materialize the single pending row — 2× Opus per pull, test-only). **One source of truth preserved:** the entry-writing spec (`buildEntrySpecBody`) and all output guards (`finalizeComposedEntry` — section homing, tag closure, headline validate/retry, summary) are now shared by both composers; the classic path is byte-identical (verified by its unchanged test suite). Confirm/write pipeline + review overlay untouched. Fully env-gated and reversible; at winner-selection the loser composer, the flag, `CompareOverlay`, and the two now-dead flags (`anchorApprovedVersion`, `checkpointText`) all come out. Rationale: any handoff to an outside composer is a lossy re-derivation and the likely cause of the "entry reflects the opening label, not the settled material" failure. **Not live-driven** — exercising the new paths needs a full paid conductor conversation that reaches readiness (the founder's actual A/B run). BUILD 0 / VITEST 693/693 (new `compose-as-conductor.test.ts`) / tsc 0.

- **Conductor-prompt fingerprint per conversation — score-vs-prompt attribution** (2026-07-08, commit `e0071f9a`): The Tuning score trend could only mark *when* the conductor prompt changed (by timestamp from `persona_voice_override_history`) — approximate, and blind to prompt changes that ship via code (`CONDUCTOR_PROMPT`) rather than a live admin edit. Now every conversation records the exact prompt that drove it. On the first Jove turn, `call-persona` stamps `conversations.conductor_prompt_sha` — a 12-hex fingerprint of the resolved conductor prompt (`buildSystemPromptBlocks`' tier1 = `override ?? CONDUCTOR_PROMPT`, so **both** live edits and code deploys are captured) — and upserts the prompt text once per version into the new `prompt_snapshots` table (dedup by sha, on-conflict-do-nothing). Mirrors the existing `rubric_sha` pattern; the sha lives on the *conversation* (a property of the session, stamped when it ran) not the score row (unlike `rubric_sha`, which is a property of the scoring run). The trend view (`/api/admin/score-conversation?view=trend`) now joins and returns `conductor_prompt_sha` per score. Edge-safe implementation (Web Crypto, not the node-only `rubricSha`); fingerprints only the conductor prompt, **not** the full assembled system prompt (the Manual/session-context blocks are per-user, per-turn, and user data — so the snapshot table dedups to a few hundred rows lifetime, not one per conversation, and stores no user content). The stamp is **fire-and-forget, self-healing** (a later turn re-stamps if the first write is cut off), gated to first-turn only (`is null`), so a mid-conversation prompt edit keeps the prompt the session started on. **Observational only** — nothing in the product reads `conductor_prompt_sha`; Jove can never be tuned toward it. Migration `20260708130000` (auto-applied on merge; `conductor_prompt_sha` nullable, `prompt_snapshots` RLS deny-all / service-role only). **Forward-only:** pre-merge sessions stay `null`; prompt-version attribution accumulates from ship time. **Follow-on UI not built:** the Tuning chart doesn't yet *draw* prompt-version bands, and there's no snapshot-browser / one-click-revert panel (reverting today = read `prompt_snapshots.text` for a sha, paste into the Tuning override). BUILD 0 / VITEST 689/689 / tsc 0.

- **Conductor: no whole-entry render before save** (2026-07-08, commit `7a5a9407`): Founder call — Jove should not read the complete assembled entry back in chat before the save. Deleted the "Before you draft" and "How to write the entry" prompt sections: under the pull model the composer writes the actual entry when the user taps the bar, so Jove drafting/rendering it in-conversation was duplication *and* the "here's how it might read… [whole entry]" moment the founder disliked (M-run J27/J28/J32; ledger note 8). Jove now works the pieces in the open, lands it, lights the bar — the assembled whole appears fresh in the overlay at pull time (the reveal-at-pull design). The entry-writing quality rules already live in the composer prompt, so this also removed a logic duplication. Composer degrades gracefully (its anchor path handles "no full version said aloud → compose from the conversation"); guarded lines untouched. Also this session: filed the SIM-A exemplar + D3 user-authored-assembly clause, and logged the crisis-injection finding as **safety-correct over-fire — leave it** (a denial isn't a reliable all-clear; negation detection would add false-negative risk to the crisis backstop). - **Honest landed-close copy + retired stale conductor override** (2026-07-08, merge `06e9f926`): The landed-close line Jove is handed said *"That's yours now, in your words. Whenever you want it."* — claiming possession before the user has pulled the reflection from the bar (nothing is saved at that moment; the pull is theirs to do). Reworded to **"That's it, in your words. Yours to keep whenever you want."** — credits authorship without asserting the save, consistent with the surrounding never-announce guardrails. **The bigger fix:** the live `conductor_prompt` override (`persona_voice_overrides`, `enabled=true`) was masking the code constant and was *stale* — it predated the same-day client-owned `PostSaveFork` merge (`42afcc99`) and still instructed Jove to hand-type the retired `---chips---` marker + three option lines, which with the parser gone would have leaked as raw text alongside the real client-drawn chips. Disabled the override in the DB (non-destructive — row + text retained, just inactive; resolver skips `enabled=false` and falls back to `CONDUCTOR_PROMPT`), so code is the live prompt again. - **Conversation scoring in admin** (2026-07-08, merge `2a48865b`): The conductor rubric (`docs/reference/conductor-scoring.md`) is now wired through admin instead of hand-run via `/evaluate`. Four surfaces: **Learn** — the rubric renders under Source docs. **Transcripts** — a "Rubric score" panel on every session: one Opus call applies rubric Part 1 (turns numbered in code, so citations are checkable) and stores the six dimension scores, signals, and verdict in the new `conversation_scores` table (RLS deny-all; rate-limited; migration `20260708120000`, auto-applied). **Tuning** — a "Session scores over time" chart (six dimension rows, fixed 1–5 axis) with a marker at every conductor-prompt edit, plus a batch button that scores unscored completed sessions one call at a time. **Rubric editing** — `scoring_rubric` override key via `/api/admin/scoring-rubric`: the doc is the floor, Reset returns to it, shows in the What's-serving strip. Two guardrails: scoring is **observational only** (nothing in the product reads scores — Jove can never be optimized toward them), and every run stores a `rubric_sha` — scores are only comparable within one rubric version. Browser-verified as devtest; chart verified with injected data (the table didn't exist pre-merge). **⚠ Post-merge check:** run one real "Score session" on a transcript to confirm the full loop. - **Admin time markers — message times, gaps, and true session durations** (2026-07-08, commit `b34d8f4f`): Founder ask ("time between messages, length of the session"). All display-layer — the timestamps already existed on every row. **Transcript view** (`UserProfilePane`): a stats header per session (`7 messages · 4d 22h · started Jul 2, 3:05 PM`), clock time on every message (`JOVE · 3:05 PM`; user messages get a right-aligned meta line), and the gap since the previous message shown when ≥ 60s (`+1h 10m`, `+3d 3h`) — quick back-and-forth stays unlabeled, real pauses and cross-day sitting breaks jump out. **Sessions list**: each row adds duration. **The one non-display change:** duration comes from actual first→last message span computed in `/api/admin/conversations` (added `created_at` to the message query it already ran for counts — no extra query), because `conversations.updated_at` is bumped by later writes (summary generation, status flips) and showed a 139-hour "session" that was really 118h of thread lifespan + 20h of background writes. Note: threads continued via "pick up where you left off" genuinely span days, so list duration reads as thread lifespan; the in-transcript gap markers show where the sittings break. New shared `formatAdminTime`/`formatDuration` in admin-shared.tsx (`formatAdminDate` now reuses the former). Browser-verified as devtest (screenshot in session). BUILD 0 / VITEST 678/678.

- **Post-save fork — one session = one reflection, seeded continuation** (2026-07-08, commit `42afcc99`; Phase B of the reflection-flow redesign): After a save the session's arc is complete; the client offers three ways forward — **Keep working on this** / **Bring something new** / **That's enough for today**. "Keep working" spins a *fresh session seeded with the just-saved entry* (the soft continuation of the hard one-reflection-per-session boundary — designed with the applied-psychologist so the boundary honors the insight rather than shutting the user down after a vulnerable peak). **The fork is now client-owned:** the `---chips---` marker was retired (founder-approved removal from `CONDUCTOR_REQUIRED_FRAGMENTS`) — Jove just acknowledges the save in one line and stops, and the client renders the fork (`PostSaveFork`, reusing the section picker's `SelectionTile`). It's **state-driven** (`postSaveEntry` in useChat), not a message field, so the post-confirm `reconcileThread` (which replaces `messages` from the DB) can't wipe it — the render bug the old model-emitted chips likely had. `keepWorkingFromSave` seeds `startExploration` from the saved entry; "new" reuses the situation door; "break" completes the conversation + goes home. Renders on both mobile + desktop (shared `sessionContent`). **Removed the whole chip cascade** (parse block, event field, `ChatMessage.chips`, render, `QuickReplyChips`) — net −180 lines. **Bonus fix:** re-added `renderExplorationContextBlock`, which had been deleted in the legacy-voice retirement and *silently unseeded the live "Explore with Jove" feature* — tapping an entry to explore was opening a cold generic opener; now it (and the new seed) open from the entry. Modernized `ExplorationContext` (dropped the stale numeric `layerId`; keyed on section name). SE change-review: ship. - **First-entry orientation made deterministic (v0.8.3)** (2026-07-08, commit `b9b7d3d3`): The one-time "how to capture" explainer (bar is full → tap Build Manual entry → you approve every word) was a ~150-word prompt block the model phrased in its own words and self-timed to the landing turn — non-deterministic, riding the prompt on *every* pre-landing turn for a one-turn payload, and the exact surface where a hallucinated-save slip (the v0.7 class) could re-enter, since it had the model describe the save mechanic. Now it's a **fixed sentence the server appends to Jove's landing message** the first time readiness lands for an empty-Manual web user — same mechanism as the crisis-resources append. Deterministic (identical every time), fires exactly once, and the model never touches the save wording. `FIRST_ENTRY_EDUCATION` is now the fixed appended text (still admin-editable via `first_entry_education` on the Tuning page); appended at the `---reflection-ready---` detection point in call-persona.ts, gated by the unit-tested `shouldAppendFirstEntryEducation`. **Dead code removed:** the `firstEntryEducation` prompt option + injection (system-prompt.ts) and its every-turn computation in `buildPromptOptionsFromContext`, plus their tests. (Live landing-turn append covered by the unit gate + mirrors the proven crisis-append path; not driven end-to-end.)

- **Three-way capture action — save / close / that's not it** (2026-07-08, commit `9ef0d63e`; Phase A of the reflection-flow redesign): The checkpoint overlay's three actions are reframed as graded signals that double as feedback on how well Jove read the user (applied-psychologist pass — the psych ranked this the highest-leverage piece for whether the user feels *seen*, because it's where a masking user either says "no" freely or complies with a wrong entry). **"Add to my Manual"** (it landed) unchanged; **"Jove, let's rework together" → "Close — the words are off"** (right thread, wrong words → Jove folds the correction); **"Discard" → "That's not it"** (wrong read → Jove drops the draft and re-angles from a different door in their material; twice on the same thread → stop reaching for the entry, back to plain talk). The primary/subordinate button hierarchy is preserved (Save is the filled primary, the other two are quiet links) so the overlay doesn't read as a rating scale that flips the user into evaluating the AI. The synthetic replies Jove replays (`CHECKPOINT_ACTIONS.naturalReply`) were realigned to the new framing and kept flat/affect-free (no fabricated recognition — the `"That resonates."` red line holds). The far-off posture rides the conductor's existing "not a yes" line — no new section (zero-sum discipline): don't defend, don't interrogate, don't apologize through it, re-angle. The **2-strike cap is soft** (conductor guidance, not a hard code gate) — a hard gate would need per-thread reject-count state the code has no thread-identity for, and the pull model already lets the user walk away (SE-confirmed the right call). Also **removed dead `postRejection` plumbing** — it was threaded confirm route → callPersona → buildSystemPromptBlocks but never emitted any text (the pinned POST-REJECTION line died with the tier system, ADR-052); cut the flag across three files + its orphan `system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap`. Net −8 lines. SE change-review: ship. *(Stale reference docs — `prompt-constructor-delta.md`, `master.md` — still describe `postRejection` as live; tracked separately.)* BUILD 0 / VITEST 683/683 / tsc 0.

- **Reflection bar is depth-only — removed the post-save recharge** (2026-07-08, commit `a0285238`): The bar's fill was `min(depth, post-save-cooldown)` — it drained to 0 after a save and rebuilt over N messages. That recharge was the source of the meter's unexplainable behavior (a bar that moved for reasons tied to an invisible message count, capped by a `min()` of two different concepts). Since a session builds toward a *single* reflection, there's no in-session refill to pace, so the cooldown was cut entirely: `reflectionMeterFill` is now depth-only (`REFLECTION_DEPTH_PCT[depth]`), and its dead params (`turnsSinceCheckpoint`, `gatePassed`, `cooldownTurns`) + the `REFLECTION_RECHARGE_TURNS` constant went with it. Readiness (fill → 100 + the pull affordance) remains Jove's `---reflection-ready---` landed marker alone, applied by `resolveReflectionMeter`. The bar now answers exactly one question — *how deep are we* — and never resets. No migration (the admin dial + `checkpoint_tuning` tables were already removed on main 2026-07-07). Net −51 lines; meter tests rewritten for the depth-only signature. First of a staged reflection-flow redesign (founder brainstorm: one session = one reflection, a three-way capture action save/close/far-off, and a post-save fork — later phases pending an applied-psych pass on early-capture + copy). BUILD 0 / VITEST 683/683 / tsc 0.

- **Reflection meter no longer resets on a discarded/reworked pull** (2026-07-08, commit `98a4831c`): Pulling a Manual entry writes an `is_checkpoint` message row immediately (compose route), but the meter's "last checkpoint" lookup in `loadConversationContext` counted *any* `is_checkpoint` row regardless of status — so a pull the user then **Discarded** or sent to **"Jove, let's rework together"** (`rejected`/`refined`) still reset the meter as if they'd saved: `turnsSinceCheckpoint` zeroed and the `reflectionLanded` scope moved past Jove's landed marker, wiping the user's reflection progress for a save that never happened (surfaced after a reload / on the restore path — the client already latched the pill correctly, `MobileSession` onClose documents the intent). Fix is a one-condition filter: the lookup now requires `checkpoint_meta->>status = 'confirmed'`, so only a real save resets the meter. Both meter values (`turnsSinceCheckpoint` + `reflectionLanded`) derive from that single query, so the one filter fixes both with no drift; the reload-restore route (`/api/checkpoint/meter`) and the live SSE path (`call-persona`) inherit it. The same jsonb operator is already live in the confirm route's concurrency guard. Senior-eng change-review: ship (correct, single source of truth, no historical-data regression — confirmed saves have carried `status: "confirmed"` since 2026-04-17). +1 e2e regression test in `db-contract.e2e.test.ts` (real Postgres — the filter is DB-enforced, so a mocked client would only test the mock; runs under the e2e CI job). Fill pace and the capture-progress curve untouched. BUILD 0 / VITEST 697/697 / tsc 0.

- **Ready state: the inversion — the band flips, the wordmark follows** (2026-07-07, merge `eb1f19d7`): Founder follow-up to the same-day pill ship — the flat walnut deep-field bloom "looked wrong" in prod. Ran an 8-way treatment competition in the mockup artifact (gradients, a pattern, a different color family, an inversion); founder picked **the inversion**: at ready the band flips to the theme's OPPOSITE pole — espresso with a cream wordmark in light mode, warm parchment with a dark wordmark in dark mode — a book spine while the entry waits. Mechanism: `--mw-deepfield` re-recipe'd per theme + new `--mw-band-*` tokens, and the header text flips via a custom-property remap scoped to `.mw-rh-topbar` (`--session-ink`/`--session-walnut`/etc. → band values) — the remap pierces the TopBar/RoomHeader inline `var()` styles, so **zero component changes**; the bar's fill gradient and the pill are siblings and keep real theme tokens (paper pill on espresso, flipped-dark pill on parchment). The flip rides the same 1.5s ease as the band fade; reduced-motion instant. 2 files (+52/−24). Verified live: both themes × mobile + desktop. BUILD 0 / VITEST 697/697 / tsc 0.

- **End-to-end drivers audit — dead acknowledgment cut, truthful schema-map, one Tuning name, live-driver strip** (2026-07-07, commit `d7e87f5`): Founder-ordered audit for "conflicting drivers" (the class that bit us when a live prompt override masked the save-gate fix). Two parallel auditors, every finding independently verified. **Cut:** the composer's `acknowledgment` field — generated and parsed on EVERY save, consumed by nothing, and its prompt instruction carried the banned push-era "I want to put that down" phrasing (behavior-preserving deletion, saves tokens per compose). **Corrected:** the admin schema-map described three retired push-model dials (`min_scenes`/`failsafe_turn`/`depth_floor`) as live — only `cooldown_turns` is read (reflection-meter recharge); the founder's prod row holds inert values in the other three. The `persona_deltas` gate is documented as INERT (nothing reads `personaModes` — gate kept per the settled ND-personas decision, toggling it changes nothing). rules.md's 96-line legacy voice section (still naming `voice-scaffold.ts`/`LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT` as live) collapsed to the stub its own banner promised. Every remaining "Jove's Prompt" page reference renamed "Tuning" (how-it-works ×3, schema-map, extraction-map, system.md, CLAUDE.md, evaluate skill, code comments); dead `voice-scaffold.ts` references cleaned from the overbuild-check ratchet meter (its `cat` was erroring) and the applied-psychologist agent; home admin nav section renamed "Controls" (two identical "Tuning" labels). **New:** a "What's serving right now" strip on the Tuning page — all five override keys with shipped-code/OVERRIDDEN status at a glance, so the override-masking failure class is visible before it bites (rides the existing GET, no new calls). **Audited CLEAN (for the record):** reflection-meter readiness has exactly ONE source (the `---reflection-ready---` marker; live SSE and reload paths share `resolveReflectionMeter`); every extraction field has a live consumer; env switches are messaging-scoped and documented; prod has zero enabled overrides. **Open product call (not touched):** the refinement-ceiling overlay branch + `deferred` action are unreachable in the pull model (every pulled checkpoint starts at `refinement_count 0`; the only increment lands on a terminal row) — remove or restore multi-refine is a founder decision. Browser-verified as devtest. BUILD 0 / VITEST 697/697 / tsc 0.

- **Conductor v0.8/v0.8.1 (founder polish) + the admin Tuning page + override reconciliation** (2026-07-07, commits `af04fae` + `989a072` + `cee2246`): Six founder-directed prompt changes: explicit goal paragraph (the full usable shape, Jove's discover-and-flesh-out job, no absolutes); "Build it in the open" reshaped to CENTRAL PIECES ONLY (segment checks, examples-not-a-checklist; "keep a working version alive" and "the save is a formality" deleted); formula trim (stuck-section scripts cut to principles; the stock interior question removed — it had reproduced verbatim in every transcript; new red line: example phrasings are register, not scripts); recognition opens the door, doesn't close the entry (a beat or two testing an unseen edge); the composer's plain-not-literary register line ported into "How to write the entry" (knowing duplication, founder-approved); opener de-scripted; full em-dash sweep of the template except the crisis clause (punctuation register bleeds into chat). Live probe of v0.8: no stock phrases, zero whole re-renders, marker rode a naturally-varied close line, v0.7 save gate held (no fake save on wrap-up; real signal → Kept + chips). **Tuning page**: /admin/prompt-architecture is now "Tuning" — the conversation (Jove's prompt editor, unchanged) and the entry (the composer's prompt shown read-only, rendered from the new exported `buildComposerSystemPrompt()` so the display can't drift, with the entry-bar field moved here from the Voice editor; one edit surface per key). **Override reconciliation**: a live `conductor_prompt` admin override (the founder's same-morning edit, based on a pre-v0.7 prompt) was serving — meaning the v0.7 save-gate fix wasn't live; its two unabsorbed founder decisions were folded into the constant as v0.8.1 (illuminate rule gains "can guide, or occasionally suggest, if it furthers the goal"; post-save acknowledgment example is now "Entry has been saved in your Manual."), then the override was reset via the Tuning page — verified disabled; the code constant serves. Browser-verified as devtest. BUILD 0 / VITEST 697/697 / tsc 0.

- **Conductor v0.7 — the hallucinated-save red line closed** (2026-07-07, commit `c253353`): The live-probe red line (PR3, same-day ship below) fixed in the prompt. Two changes to `CONDUCTOR_PROMPT`: (1) the close line and `---reflection-ready---` travel together — never one without the other (the missed-fire half); (2) **"After a save" is now gated on the real save signal** — the section opens only when the confirm route's synthetic "I saved that to my Manual." reply appears; approval/wrap-up phrases ("that's mine," "I'm good for today") are named as non-saves; without the signal, no "kept," no chips, a warm one-line close instead. A softer trigger-definition-only wording **failed under sampling variance** (replay reproduced the fake save 1 of 2 runs); the explicit gate replayed **4/4 clean** on the exact PR3 trigger and still produces Kept+chips on the real signal (probe 4, fresh regression conversation). New guard test pins the prompt to `CHECKPOINT_ACTIONS.confirmed.naturalReply` so the two wordings can't drift. No live `conductor_prompt` admin override existed at the 2026-07-06 check, so the code constant is the live prompt. BUILD 0 / VITEST 697/697 (+1 guard).

- **Conversation scoring rubric shipped; /evaluate rewired; first live conductor probes** (2026-07-07, commits `0261f38` + `a56542e`): The conductor-tuning initiative's scoring framework is now a repo doc: `docs/reference/conductor-scoring.md` — core-first (Part 1 alone is the whole rubric: two hard rules, three mechanical signals, six 1–5 dimensions with turn-cited anchors; Part 2 holds expansion modules — landed-signal timing, pull/assembly quality, rupture-repair), every anchor tied to a filed exemplar (purpose-run + Kevin-run + live probes), hardened by an independent behavioral-psych review (ranked recognition evidence — corrections > fresh words > charge/"huh"; multi-route interior for the ~50%-alexithymic user base; same-root no-triple-counting; rupture-repair metric). `/evaluate` rewired to `conductor-prompt.ts` + the rubric; the stale 314-line `quality-framework.md` (graded the deleted tier/rebuilt world) **deleted**. **First live probes of the v0.6 conductor** (3 synthetic-user runs, production model/params; transcripts in `docs/reference/conductor-probe-transcripts-2026-07-07.md`): 2 clean `---reflection-ready---` fires, ok-not-yes catches live, direct pivots honored ("Fair.", no third pass) — and two confirmed drifts: whole-working-version re-rendered 3× in five turns (the segments-vs-whole gap, ledger note 2 now evidence-backed), and **a red-line failure: probe 3 skipped the marker on the close message, then hallucinated a save — "Kept, as you said it." + `---chips---` with no save event** (the model treated chat agreement as the save; the real signal is the confirm route's synthetic system message, which the prompt never names). Filed as ledger note 5; prompt fix queued as its own session. Docs + agent-tooling only, no runtime change; BUILD 0 / VITEST 696/696.

- **Never assume gender — three prompt surfaces hardened** (2026-07-06, commit `9d19f7c`): No prompt anywhere told any model call not to assume gender, and two of the exposed surfaces compound (a guessed pronoun in a stored summary gets re-read by Jove every future turn/session). Three one-line additions: (1) conductor prompt, new "What you never do" red line — never assume gender, theirs or anyone's they mention, names or "they" until told; (2) the session-summary prompt (`generate-summary.ts`) — refer to the user as "they"; (3) the compose call's compressed entry-summary rule (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`) — third person means "they" for the user, never a gendered pronoun. Ruled out as non-issues: entry bodies (the user's verbatim words), extraction (internal-only), the group/Linq prompt (no-further-investment). Verified no live `conductor_prompt` admin override exists, so the code constant is the live prompt. Passes the scaffolding test (pronoun assumption from names/roles is a real frontier-model failure, a red line — not taste). Pre-existing gendered pronouns in already-stored summaries are untouched. BUILD 0 / VITEST 696/696.
- **The narrative docs catch up to the pull model** (2026-07-06, commit `f2ce566`): `system.md` fully rewritten around "Jove talks; the user saves" — pipeline, extraction (6 fields, two consumers), capture lifecycle, three-block prompt assembly, invariants (leads with **"Jove never saves"**), SSE protocol, feature gates, text-dark status; also fixed two false claims (admin routes are not read-only; agent sessions CAN browser-verify via devtest). `CLAUDE.md`'s "Prompt Structure" section — which still taught agents the deleted three-tier/rebuilt voice as live — replaced with the conductor reality. `decisions.md` gains **ADR-052** (the pull-model migration: why push died, what shipped, and the one-way-door consequence — no voice switch; revert = git revert + rebuild). With this, the whole doc set + the admin doc shelf (`/admin/docs` renders these files) is current. Remaining from the trust-the-foundation arc: the founder prod smoke test (send a real message, watch a reflection land + save). Docs-only; BUILD 0 / VITEST 0 (696/696).
- **Admin map pages tell the pull-model story** (2026-07-06, commit `53b7aa9`): The in-app admin documentation stopped lying about the deleted push model. **how-it-works** rewritten as a six-stage walkthrough of the real loop: conductor prompt + Manual/session context assemble while extraction fires alongside → Jove streams (never proposes a save; marks landed moments with the hidden `---reflection-ready---` signal) → the reflection meter is the user's capture surface → tap → compose → editable overlay → confirm → database closes the loop. **schema-map**: `is_checkpoint` / `source_message_id` / `persona_voice_overrides` descriptions rewritten for user-pulled capture + the new `conductor_prompt` key. Nav labels renamed "Jove's prompt architecture" → "Jove's Prompt" everywhere. These pages are the founder's learning surface for the system — kept deliberately plain-language. BUILD 0 / VITEST 0 (696/696) / tsc 0; how-it-works visually verified.
- **Always-true conductor plumbing + dead checkpoint SSE surface collapsed** (2026-07-06, commit `7a4b9ac`): Three dead layers left by the pull-model migration removed. (1) `conductorActive` (always true) cut from `ConversationContext`; compose route passes `anchorApprovedVersion: true`; **`LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT` / `VoiceVariant` deleted entirely** (zero readers) along with the `voiceVariant` prompt option. (2) `resolveConversationMode` collapsed to parse-only — its per-mode-gate fallback had been unreachable since the promotion (the conductor always honored the requested mode), so live behavior is byte-identical; **the mode gates stay** — their real job is hiding home-screen doors via `/api/onboarding-status`. (3) The checkpoint SSE surface: `checkpoint` (always null since the push-path deletion) and `composing` (never sent) removed from `MessageCompleteEvent`, the three null emissions cut from call-persona, the dead client branch + `composingCheckpoint` state thread (useChat → MainApp → MobileSession) deleted; `checkpoint_proposed` analytics moved to `composeCheckpoint` (under the pull model "proposed" = the user pulled a reflection into view). Verified with explicit exit codes (a zsh `PIPESTATUS` casing bug had been masking exit codes in earlier checks — builds now asserted via `$?`): tsc 0, build 0, vitest 696/696, plus a **live browser turn as devtest** — message streamed and rendered on the slimmed protocol, zero console errors.
- **Jove's Prompt — the conductor is live-editable as one document** (2026-07-06, commit `e0b6e16`): Founder decision: keep non-code voice control, but as ONE editable interface, not per-section machinery. The whole `CONDUCTOR_PROMPT` is now editable from admin via a new `conductor_prompt` key in the existing `persona_voice_overrides` system; `buildSystemPromptBlocks` resolves `tier1 = override ?? CONDUCTOR_PROMPT` (byte-identical when no override; an edit re-primes the prompt cache next turn). **Save guard**: an edit that drops a non-negotiable line — the 988/741741 crisis lines, `---reflection-ready---`, `---chips---` — is rejected at the API with a plain-language error naming what went missing; `CONDUCTOR_REQUIRED_FRAGMENTS` + `validateConductorPromptEdit` live in dependency-free `conductor-prompt.ts` so the admin display and the enforcement share one source of truth (tests pin that the shipped prompt passes its own guard). **The dead prompt-architecture viewer is gone** (103KB page + its API route + `prompt-sections.ts`) — replaced by one compact "Jove's Prompt" page: what the model receives in order (prompt → compressed Manual → session context), live section outline, the whole document in one editor, protected lines explained, Save/Reset. The inert `rebuilt_character` Character override is retired (it had been dead for live traffic since the 2026-07-02 promotion); **`voice-scaffold.ts` deleted** (the ND persona-delta files import nothing from it — they remain untouched per the settled keep); migration `20260706020000` deletes the orphaned row (history rows kept — audit log). `VoiceEditorPanel` keeps the small operational fields and links to the page. Browser-verified as devtest: page renders (2,412 words / 14 sections), the save guard rejects live with a 400 naming all four protected lines (no write), the Voice editor excludes the conductor field. tsc 0, build clean, 705/705 vitest (+6 guard tests). ⚠ One junk `beta_feedback` row from devtest was accidentally submitted during browser verification (the feedback widget's Send was clicked with test text) — harmless, delete from the admin Feedback panel when convenient.
- **The rebuilt/legacy rollback voice worlds retired — conductor is the sole 1:1 voice** (2026-07-06, commit `60c4ee0`): Post-Wave-3 loose-ends audit, the "rollback" bucket. The conductor has been the live voice for all users since the 2026-07-02 promotion, and Wave-3 ship 2 already deleted the Jove-pushed checkpoint machinery the rebuilt/legacy voices fell back to — so those two dormant voice worlds no longer restored a working capture path. Keeping them was a rollback to nothing, and their Tier-1/2/3 machinery was clouding the voice core. **Removed:** `composeTier2` + the entire Tier-3 system (`buildTier3`, `TIER_3_BLOCKS`, `deriveTier3Flags`, `Tier3Flags`), `TIER_1`, `REBUILT_LIMITS` / `REBUILT_MECHANICS` / `MECHANICS_PARTS`, the five base voice arrays, and the dead render helpers (`renderTranscriptContextBlock`, `renderExplorationContextBlock`, `renderPastedContentGuidance`). `VoiceVariant` narrowed to `"conductor"`. `system-prompt.ts` 1131→282 lines; **net −4,398 lines** across src + tests. **Kept (verified):** the `CONDUCTOR_PROMPT` path is **byte-identical** to the old conductor branch — no cache invalidation, no live prompt change; `buildGroupPrompt` (Linq facilitator) untouched; `REBUILT_CHARACTER` retained (still the admin Character-override default in `voice-overrides.ts`); the four ND persona-delta files (`voice-{autistic,adhd,dyslexic,general}.ts`) preserved untouched (settled keep — now dormant/unimported). tsc 0, build clean, vitest 699/699. **Senior-engineer verdict: SHIP** (byte-identity of the conductor block + zero live imports of any deleted symbol are the load-bearing checks). **Follow-ups noted (not blockers):** (1) the admin **prompt-architecture viewer** is now semantically dead — its `SECTION_DEFS` regexes match the retired TIER 1/2/3 headers, so it renders empty/zero-token sections; wants its own cleanup pass. (2) The admin **Character voice-override editor** is inert for live Jove (has been since the 2026-07-02 promotion — the conductor ignores `voiceOverrides.character`); the field still saves but changes nothing users see — a product decision to confirm (keep as future hook, or remove). (3) `conductorActive` is now always-true (a flag pending collapse). (4) `voice-scaffold.ts` still holds now-dead-except-tests scaffold consts (`BANNED_PHRASES`, `PACING_RULE`, etc.) — safe to delete in the follow-up.
- **1:1 text/SMS gated OFF (silent) pending a rebuild** (2026-07-06, commit `beece51`): The conductor is a pull-model voice — the user saves from the on-screen reflection meter, which text/SMS has no surface for — so the 1:1 text path was a broken half-limbo (conductor talking, no capture, prompt pointing at a nonexistent reflection bar). Gated OFF: `routeInboundMessage` checks `TEXT_MESSAGING_ENABLED` right after the CTIA keyword block; when it's not `"true"`, a normal inbound is **dropped silently** — the webhook still 200s, no user lookup, no Jove turn, no send. STOP/START/HELP stay live above the gate (CTIA never gated). Env var, not a feature_gate — a coarse channel switch that should default OFF and needs no per-inbound DB read (matches the `MESSAGING_PROVIDER` env pattern, ADR-035). Flip `TEXT_MESSAGING_ENABLED=true` in Vercel to re-enable once text is rebuilt. New `message-router.test.ts` pins gate-off-drops / gate-on-proceeds / STOP-still-honored. **⚠ On deploy, 1:1 text/SMS stops responding (founder call — the broken experience is off until rebuilt).** tsc 0, 1066 vitest green, build clean.
- **Dead-config cleanup: cut the `checkpoints` gate + `computeInheritedRefinementCount`** (2026-07-06, commit `cb2e318`): Post-Wave-3 loose-ends audit, bucket C. Removed the `checkpoints` feature gate — it gated Jove's auto-propose (deleted in Wave 3); no live code reads `gates.checkpoints` anymore (capture is the user-pulled meter). Dropped from `FeatureGates` / `FEATURE_GATE_KEYS` / the doc comment / the admin panel row / the tests; migration `20260706010000` drops the orphaned row. Also deleted `computeInheritedRefinementCount` — the pull path composes fresh at 0 (`buildCheckpointMeta(composed, 0)`), so its chain-inheritance had no caller (`refinement_count` itself stays live: compose sets 0, confirm increments). Deferred (bucket C item 3): the always-null `checkpoint` SSE field + its dead useChat branch — lowest value, client-entangled, left as a tidy-up. tsc 0, 1063 vitest green, build clean.
- **The `reflection_meter` gate removed + admin extraction displays refreshed** (2026-07-06, commit `d07303e`): Post-Wave-3 follow-up. The conductor pull model is the only capture path, so the `reflection_meter` feature gate no longer fit — the meter is unconditional on web (`reflectionMeterEnabled = surface === "web"`), and its one remaining reader (a fail-closed check in `/api/checkpoint/compose`) would have **rejected the save by default** (gate defaults OFF; prod worked only because it was manually enabled). Removed the gate entirely: from `FeatureGates` (interface / defaults / key map), the compose + meter routes, the Feature Gates admin panel, and the client (useChat / MobileSession / ReflectionHeader); migration `20260706000000` drops the orphaned row (unknown keys are ignored, so it's cleanup — the only behavior change is un-blocking compose in a default env). Also refreshed the admin extraction surfaces after the Wave-3 schema trim: `ExtractionPanel` stopped rendering the deleted gate scorecard + `gate_eval`, and the `extraction-map` doc dropped the deleted-field descriptors (`checkpoint_gate` scorecard, `pattern_engaged`, `layers.signal`) and the now-unused `"gate"` job. tsc 0, 1070 vitest green, build clean. Closes the post-Wave-3 loose-ends bucket-C/D items (the `checkpoints`-gate cut + `computeInheritedRefinementCount` remain for a later pass).
- **Extraction schema trimmed to what the pull model reads (Wave 3, ship 3)** (2026-07-03, commit `9af646a`): The push-path deletion left extraction fields that now feed nothing live. Removed from `ExtractionState`: `pattern_engaged` (was a push-gate input), `layers` / `LayerSignal` (was `deriveCheckpointApproaching`-only), and the `checkpoint_gate` scorecard — `concrete_examples`, `has_mechanism`, `has_charged_language`, `has_behavior_driver_link`, `strongest_layer`. **Kept `checkpoint_gate.distinct_contexts`** — the save-time composer reads it for the headline over-claim softener. Also removed the orphaned `validateComposedEntry` + `SOMATIC_WORD_PATTERNS` (no prod caller) and the dead gate-scorecard debug log in call-persona. **Trimmed the extraction PROMPT to match:** dropped the SECTION SIGNALS and PATTERN ENGAGEMENT sections, reframed CHECKPOINT GATE as an informational DISTINCT CONTEXTS note (not a gate anymore), pruned the JSON schema + CRITICAL RULES — so the extractor stops generating the dead fields (output tokens) and stops carrying them in the recurrent fed-back state. Net −397 lines. tsc 0, 1070 vitest green, build clean. **Follow-up chip filed:** the admin `ExtractionPanel` + `extraction-map` doc still display the removed fields, and the `reflection_meter` feature gate defaults OFF while the pull model is now the only capture path (`compose/route.ts` would reject by default — prod works only because the gate was manually enabled). That's a product/config decision (default it ON, or remove the vestigial gate) for a separate pass. **This completes the Wave 3 arc** (promote → delete push → trim schema): the codebase is now cleanly and only the conductor pull model.
- **The Jove-pushed checkpoint path is deleted (Wave 3, ship 2 of 2)** (2026-07-03, commit `5619000`): With the conductor promoted to the live voice, capture is pure-PULL (tap the meter → `/api/checkpoint/compose` → confirm), so the entire Jove-pushed checkpoint machinery was unreachable dead code. Removed it: **persona-pipeline** — `deriveProposalFlags`, `validateMaterialQuality`, `applyCheckpointGates`, `deriveCheckpointApproaching`, `CheckpointGateResult`, the `checkpointsEnabled`/`checkpointApproaching`/`priorCheckpointSuppressed` context fields, and `resolveReflectionMeter`'s non-conductor branch (it's pure-pull now: `reflectionMeterEnabled = surface === "web"`). **call-persona** — the whole streaming-handler checkpoint block (detection, gate/strip, split-delivery, inline composition, the `checkpoint` SSE field — now always `null`), plus `stripCheckpointFromText`/`splitCheckpointLeadIn`/`detectCheckpointInResponse`. **persona-bridge (SMS)** — its detection/gate/compose block; text now saves the reply and returns (no capture until the text rebuild). **system-prompt** — the CHECKPOINTS / first-checkpoint / POST-SUPPRESSION Tier-3 blocks + their `deriveTier3Flags` gating. **detect-checkpoint.ts deleted**; the three inert checkpoint dials (min-scenes / failsafe-turn / depth-floor) + their panel rows removed (**cooldownTurns KEPT** — meter recharge). **Kept intact:** the pull composer (compose/confirm routes + `composeManualEntry`), the reflection meter + `---reflection-ready---` marker, crisis/988 (three layers), the legacy/rebuilt voice as rollback. **Net −3,497 lines.** tsc 0, 1081 vitest green (−115 tests for deleted behavior), build clean. **Reviewed:** senior-engineer verdict SHIP — live loop + SMS path verified intact, no dangling refs, SSE well-formed, no caller left reading an absent field. **⚠ This closes the rollback window:** `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT="rebuilt"` no longer restores a working capture path (the push path it fell back to is gone) — rollback is now `git revert` of this commit, not a one-line flip. **Ship 3 (deferred, low-risk):** the now-unread extraction fields (`checkpoint_gate` scorecard beyond `distinct_contexts`, `pattern_engaged`, `layers[].signal`), the orphaned `validateComposedEntry`, and stale admin-doc comments — a Wave-1-style schema/doc cleanup. **Owed founder smoke test:** send a normal message in prod as a real user, watch a reflection land + the meter fill (the one thing static analysis can't prove — though this commit only *removes* an already-unreachable branch, so a normal turn emits exactly what it did before).
- **The deep-field reflection header comes to desktop — one shared surface** (2026-07-03, commit `b33bd56`): The 2026-07-02 deep-field header was mobile-only; on desktop the RoomHeader (the actual header) showed nothing — MobileSession's ReflectionHeader rendered only a wordmark-less band in the conversation column below it. This brings the **same** treatment to the desktop RoomHeader on the session view: it colours at ready, the meter is its base rule, the message-you-clear blooms below, and the **session title rides in the coloured row unchanged**; non-session views are untouched. **Architecture (senior-engineer-reviewed):** a new `useReflection` hook (MainApp) is the single source for the reflection UI state — composing, the one-time education (SSR-guarded localStorage), `showEducation` (derived once), `onBuild` — consumed by both headers, so the decision logic is never duplicated. `ReflectionHeader` now wraps the header row as `children` (TopBar on mobile, the RoomHeader row on desktop) — one component, one treatment. The whole-header tap overlay is **mobile-only** (on desktop it would swallow the FEEDBACK button); desktop gets an explicit **"Build reflection"** button in the header's right cluster. `MobileSession` is `forwardRef` + `useImperativeHandle(composeAndOpenOverlay)` so the hook drives the ONE `CheckpointOverlay` imperatively from either shell — the overlay + review core stay **byte-for-byte local and single-writer** (chosen over lifting the overlay state, which would have entangled the checkpoint path; the SE approved the imperative handle as "strictly better"). Composing is one flag in the hook, passed down to drive both the header and the overlay's loading. **Verified live on mobile end-to-end:** onBuild → imperative handle → compose (a real entry) → review overlay → education dismissed → standing colour band. tsc + eslint clean, 1245 vitest green, build clean. **⚠ Owed founder spot-check: the desktop shell (≥1030px) can't be previewed in-env (it renders the mobile column even at 1400px) — the RoomHeader treatment is verified by build/types/tests + the shared components proven on mobile, but not yet by eye on a real desktop.**
- **The conductor is promoted to the live voice (Wave 3, ship 1 of 2)** (2026-07-03, commit `32577cc`): `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT` flips `"rebuilt"` → `"conductor"` — every user now gets the pull-model voice, not just admins. `conductorActive` is now derived as `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT === "conductor"`, so this one constant turns the whole pull model on and is the single rollback lever (set it back to `"rebuilt"` to restore rebuilt+push in one place). **Web:** conductor voice + reflection meter (user pulls; Jove never triggers saves). **Text/SMS:** conductor voice, but the meter is web-only — so **text capture is intentionally dark** until the text rebuild (founder call: "cut text over now," accept the interim; the conductor's "reflection bar" line is a known wart on SMS meanwhile). **Experiment gating removed (founder: "hard-commit, no switch"):** the `conductor` feature gate, the `isAdmin` scoping, and the `isAdmin` threading through call-persona / chat / meter / compose are all gone; the meter-restore route's gate short-circuit is gone (ctx.reflectionMeterEnabled is the sole authority). Migration `20260702010000` drops the orphaned conductor gate row. Legacy stays as the deeper rollback. **Rollback note:** the rebuilt+push fallback is intact until ship 2 removes the push path — so the LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT="rebuilt" escape hatch works *until then*. tsc 0 errors, 1196 vitest green, build clean. **⚠ Ship 2 (delete the now-dead push machinery — detection, gate/scorecard, inert dials) is gated on founder prod-verifying the conductor holds for real users, because ship 2 removes the rebuilt+push rollback net.**
- **Extraction-map admin doc reframed — the per-turn brief is gone (Wave 1 follow-up)** (2026-07-03, commit `5fa14ed`): Closes the follow-up chip filed in the Wave 1 entry below (the stale `formatExtractionForPersona` reader refs on the `/admin/extraction-map` page). The drift ran deeper than reader strings: the page was built around "one call, two jobs — **brief + gate**," but the per-turn brief no longer exists — nothing renders extraction into Jove's live turns (`system-prompt.ts:1125` confirms the retirement). **Reframed to the true shape:** extraction feeds the checkpoint **gate** and the save-time **composer** (`composeManualEntry`), and nothing else. Renamed the "Briefs Jove" job → **"Composes the entry"**; re-tagged `sage_brief` / `current_thread` / `distinct_contexts` to the composer and corrected `strongest_layer` (a gate input, not a composer one); rewrote all 13 stale reader refs to their real call sites, verified against `extraction.ts` + `persona-pipeline.ts`; flagged `has_charged_language` as a prune candidate (Lock 1 replaced it — no production reader, only a dev log + admin panel). **Removed dead grouping:** the empty `signal` job (phantom "pattern snippet") and the permanently-0 "Jove's brief" / "Modals" / "SSE payload" destination rows — the matrix now shows the two real destinations. Doc-accuracy only, no behavior change; net −28 lines. tsc clean, lint clean, 1208 vitest green, build clean; browser-verified as admin.
- **Strip-to-baseline experiment retired; conductor re-homed on its own gate (Wave 2)** (2026-07-02, commit `b168e9f`): The experiment is concluded (founder call), so the whole harness came out — `baseline-experiment.ts`, the `/api/admin/baseline-experiment` route, `BaselineExperimentPanel` + its admin mount, the `"baseline"` voice branch in `system-prompt.ts`, the `baseline` VoiceVariant member, and all `baselineActive` / `baselineForces` / `baselineGateOpen` threading across persona-pipeline / call-persona / meter route / chat route. **The conductor survives on its own switch:** a new `conductor` key in the existing `feature_gates` table (defaults OFF, fails closed — same posture as `reflection_meter`), still admin-scoped in code (`conductorActive = isAdmin && gates.conductor`), rendered as a toggle in the **Feature Gates** admin panel. No new table — reuses the feature-gates infra + its per-turn read (removal-first). `baselineGateOpen` dropped entirely: under the conductor the pushed path is off and the meter ignores the gate verdict, so it had no observable effect; crisis still blocks via `validateMaterialQuality`'s own crisis check. The MECHANICS carve stays as internal `REBUILT_MECHANICS` structure (byte-identical, snapshot-guarded) — only its baseline framing was removed. Migration `20260702000000` seeds the conductor gate OFF and drops `baseline_experiment_gates` (auto-applies on merge). Conductor guard tests extracted to `conductor-prompt.test.ts`. **⚠ Founder action:** to resume conductor testing, flip **"Conductor voice (experiment)"** ON in the Feature Gates panel (the old Baseline panel is gone; the toggle state didn't carry over). Net −923 lines. tsc 0 errors, 1196 vitest green, build clean. **Wave 3 (push-path removal) remains staged — gated on conductor promotion + text/SMS moving off the pushed model.**
- **Extraction cleanup — the per-turn brief + 6 dead feeder fields removed (Wave 1)** (2026-07-02): The first cut from the 5-agent Jove-variables audit.
