The per-turn extraction brief (`formatExtractionForPersona`) was injected only into the LEGACY voice — verified that neither live voice (rebuilt, conductor) receives it (`system-prompt.ts`) — so the six fields whose ONLY reader was that brief were computed every turn for no one. **Removed:** `formatExtractionForPersona` + `SIGNAL_LABEL`; the whole `extractionContext` plumbing (BuildPromptOptions, both prompt builders, persona-pipeline, the admin prompt-viewer samples); `ExtractionState.mode` / `observation_miss_count` / `user_named_cost` / `user_named_stance` and `LayerSignal.material` / `.examples`; the extraction-PROMPT sections that produced them (mode rec, observation-miss, readiness signals) + their JSON-schema keys, so the extractor stops generating them (output-token savings); six now-dead field blocks in the admin extraction-map page. **Kept (still consumed live):** depth, clinical_flag, language_bank, current_thread, sage_brief, checkpoint_gate, pattern_engaged — `sage_brief` and the JOVE BRIEF prompt section stay because they feed the save-time composer. **Tradeoff accepted (founder call):** a rollback to the dormant legacy voice would now run without its per-turn brief; legacy is itself scheduled for Phase-3b teardown, and it's reversible in git. Net −908 lines. Follow-up chip filed for the remaining stale `formatExtractionForPersona` reader refs in the extraction-map doc. tsc 0 errors, 1208 vitest green (−37 tests for the deleted brief/fields), build clean. Wave 2 (baseline-experiment teardown) and Wave 3 (push-path removal) remain staged.
- **The reflection strip becomes the header itself — deep-field, a message you clear** (2026-07-02, commit `76a5b7f`): The stacked meter + ready-strip + one-time explainer (three surfaces floating under the header, founder-flagged "cluttered, bolted-on") is replaced by one cohesive header band. **Presentation only — the readiness logic is untouched:** marker-driven ready (`---reflection-ready---`), `pattern_engaged`, the depth-fill curve and full-bar-⇔-ready all still live server-side; the bar shows the server fill as-is (honors the resolver — gate-ready already fills to 100, so it reads full there). New `ReflectionHeader` **composes the shared `TopBar`** (untouched — it's used in 9 places) with the deep field painted behind it, the 3px meter as the header's base rule, and the message below the bar. At ready the header blooms into the **deep field** (walnut over each theme's own base — flat, so a tall band has no seam; Slate & Brass + Hearth both tuned) and the bar's glow settles to a calm brass rule. **First session only:** a message grows below the bar you dismiss — "*Your reflection is ready.* Nothing enters your Manual unless you build it — build it when you're ready, or keep talking." + a quiet "Got it" — reusing the existing `mw_reflection_intro_seen` flag; once cleared (or after building, which also marks it seen), colour alone carries the standing invitation. Tapping the header composes on demand — **not gated** by Got it. **Removal-first:** ~174 lines of strip/explainer markup out of `MobileSession`; new component +118, `.mw-rh-*` + per-theme `--mw-deepfield` in `globals.css` (design-token guard green). Feature-gated to `reflection_meter` — the plain header is unchanged, no regression off the pull-model path. Landed via a 17-mockup, multi-round design competition (variant C, "the deep field"). Verified: tsc + eslint clean, 1245 vitest green, build clean, and live-checked in both themes in the running app. **Deferred (fast-follow):** the saved-receipt + bar-drain (frame 6) needs a "just saved" signal from the confirm flow — no receipt today, so nothing regresses.
- **Admin: checkpoint dials marked "push-model only"** (2026-07-02, commit `86edaf7`): Fallout from the 5-agent Jove-variables audit — three of the four checkpoint-tuning dials (min scenes, failsafe turn, depth floor) have zero effect under the conductor arm (readiness there is Jove's `---reflection-ready---` marker, not the eagerness gates), yet the panel presented all four as live, so an admin tuning them while testing the conductor would reasonably think the panel was broken. Labeling fix, not a removal (they still govern the older push model on text/SMS): a per-dial "push-model only" badge + one intro sentence; `cooldownTurns` stays unmarked because it still paces the conductor bar's recharge. Token-only styling (design-token guard green). 1245 vitest green, build clean. Part of the audit's "Wave 0" — the one fix worth doing during conductor testing; the actual variable deletions (dead extraction fields, baseline teardown, push-path removal) remain staged, not started.
- **Jove's landed line publishes ready — the meter stops guessing** (2026-07-02, commit `157405b`): The stop-thrashing resolution to "the strip fires too early." Every extraction-side readiness proxy fired early in live runs — depth alone (mom-run), depth + `pattern_engaged` (the Guerneville run, where the strip appeared mid-exploration with no working version in existence) — because the analyst infers readiness secondhand, one turn behind, while Jove already JUDGES it firsthand: the conductor's "When it's landed" sequence (working version built → more-to-mine check → landed markers → "That's yours now, in your words"). This ship wires that existing judgment to the meter and deletes the guesswork. **Mechanism (reuses the proven marker rails):** the landed message ends with `---reflection-ready---` (one clause added to the existing landed paragraph — the only prompt change); call-persona strips it like `---sections---`/`---chips---` and tags the row `metadata.reflection_landed` (same pattern as `checkpoint_suppressed`); `loadConversationContext` reads it back scoped since the last checkpoint, so a save resets readiness with zero new state; both meter paths (live SSE + reload restore) get it through the one shared resolver. **Deletions:** the same-morning `pattern_engaged` ready condition and the `CONDUCTOR_STRIP_FILL` depth threshold — ready no longer reads the analyst at all; the analyst only animates the depth-journey fill (caps at 80; snaps to 100 exactly when the strip appears — full bar ⇔ strip visible holds). **Invariant intact:** Jove still cannot save; the marker opens the door, the user walks through it. **Known risk, accepted:** marker compliance (chips precedent) — miss direction is late/never, visible, and fixable in exactly one prompt paragraph. This was the escalation named in the mom-run entry ("marker-published working version comes off the shelf"), now taken. Guard test pins the clause; 1245 vitest green, build clean. **Founder gate: live conductor run — strip should appear at the moment Jove says the reflection is theirs, not before.**
- **Full bar ⇔ strip visible — the meter tells one story** (2026-07-02, commit `2c2838d`): First live sighting of the pattern_engaged gate surfaced a display contradiction the founder rejected on sight: the bar at 60% while the strip said "ready when you are." Not parallel systems — ONE resolver computes both fill and ready from the same scorecard — but its two outputs told different halves of the story on screen. Fix in the shared resolver (so live SSE, reload-restore, and any future surface agree by construction): when ready, fill snaps to **100**; pre-ready fill stays depth-only and caps at **80** (`REFLECTION_DEPTH_PCT.origin`), so a full bar can never appear without the strip. **Deliberately reverses the 2026-07-02 latch removal** ("false 100%-at-ready"): that call was correct when ready meant depth-alone (a weak bot-side signal — 100 was a false claim); ready now requires the user to have worked the pattern, so full-at-ready is honest and the contradiction was the greater harm. Test suite pins the new contract (ready → `{fill:100}`; origin-unengaged → `{fill:80, ready:false}`). 1244 vitest green, build clean.
- **Ready strip requires an engaged pattern, not depth alone** (2026-07-02, commit `6c84441`): The founder's "entries showing as ready too early" report, root-caused past the curve work: the strip's trigger read ONLY `extraction.depth` — a bot-side, monotonic material high-water mark that flips the moment mechanism material is *touched* once, knowing nothing about whether the user is in it. Same class of error as the killed watcher ("fires at material peak, not user landing"), inherited by the strip in passive form. Psych pressure-test (founder-commissioned, depth-vs-balk) confirmed the split — bar fill stays depth (honest journey), strip ready needs a **user-side** signal — and rejected the first design ("user approved the working version") as re-importing the documented leading-yes failure one layer up, mis-firing earliest for masking/fawn-assent ND users. **Fix, removal-first (founder call against a new extraction signal):** ready = fill ≥ `CONDUCTOR_STRIP_FILL` **AND `extraction.pattern_engaged`** — the signal that has existed all along (pattern named + user elaborated / added an example / named the cost / corrected it; sticky once true; user naming it themselves counts immediately). One `&&`; no new field, no prompt change, no migration. Anti-stuck: `pattern_engaged`'s engagement definition is deliberately broad (a user who never corrects but keeps working the thread passes), and the `failsafeTurn` override pattern exists in the gate code if soak shows under-firing. **Escalation documented, not built:** if entries still save thin, the psych's stricter gate (working-version-exists + correction-or-fresh-restatement, biased to stay closed when unsure) is next — evidence first. The admin tuning-knobs proposal from the same morning was parked: it tuned the wrong signal. 1244 vitest green, build clean. **Founder gate: test-and-review in prod before finalizing.**
- **Locked-box cleanup: door-intro pre-warm removed; watchdog comment corrected** (2026-07-02, commit `079c8c0`): The founder's post-mortem question — "this is a small thing we heavily engineered" — turned into a deletion pass over what accreted around the locked-box bug. **Removed: the pre-warm** (`preStartRef` + stashed promise + dismiss fallback + its dedicated double-tap guard in `MainApp`). It started the conversation *behind* the one-time door-intro modal to save a few seconds of typing-indicator wait — value: at most three times per user, ever (once per door) — and it was precisely the "start a conversation inside a hidden view" choreography that helped the 0px-composer bug hide for weeks. Dismiss now starts the conversation in the open; `useChat`'s in-flight `startGuard` covers double-fires (the `createStartGuard` unit tests still stand — that guard protects all start paths, not just the pre-warm). **Corrected, not removed: the SSE stall watchdog's comment** — it cited the 2026-07-01 locked-composer incident as its motivation, now known to be the 0px textarea, not a hung stream; the watchdog stays as cheap generic insurance against silently-dead mobile connections, with its history set straight so nobody re-learns the wrong lesson. **Kept (earning their keep):** header id-capture, sessionStorage clears, the 0px guard. **Browser-verified on a true first-time door** (devtest's `door_intros_seen` reset by hand): modal shows with ZERO `/api/chat` calls behind it, dismiss fires exactly one, opener arrives ~3s, composer clickable/typable. Net: the whole bug saga now ends with less code in `MainApp` than it started with. 1244 vitest green, build clean.
- **The bar waits: back-loaded fill curve + the false 100%-at-ready latch removed** (2026-07-02): Founder call — visible fill creates "I should action this" pressure, and early fill was part of why premature pulls produced thin entries. **(1) `REFLECTION_DEPTH_PCT` back-loaded** (surface 0 / behavior 8 / feeling 28 / mechanism 60 / origin 80, was 6/30/58/85/92): the bar barely moves through storytelling, stirs at feelings, does its real rising only once the WHY is on the table. `CONDUCTOR_STRIP_FILL` tracks the new mechanism value (60) — same semantics, strip still appears at mechanism depth. **(2) The client's `ready ? 100 : fill` latch removed** (`MobileSession.tsx`): under the pull model "ready" means the strip is available, NOT that the reflection is complete — snapping the bar to full was a false completion claim and the literal source of the action-pressure; for the gate-driven model the latch was redundant (gate-ready already returns fill 100 server-side), so removal changes nothing for normal users. Meter tests updated to the new curve. 1244 vitest green, build clean.

- **The "locked box" had a SECOND root cause: a 0px composer textarea — found and fixed** (2026-07-02, commit `d6f0044`): The founder re-tested after the `dcf23e8` id-capture fix and the locked-input symptom persisted — with the decisive new detail that the box *looked* highlighted but clicks didn't land until refresh. Live DOM inspection on the repro found the actual mechanism: the composer `<textarea>` was **0 pixels tall**. Causal chain: `MobileLayout` keeps inactive views mounted with `display:none`; `ChatInput`'s auto-resize `useLayoutEffect` runs on mount, measures `scrollHeight` inside the hidden subtree (always 0), writes `height:0px` — and only re-runs on input changes, so entering the chat from Home kept the 0px textarea inside a normal-looking pill (its own `min-height:56px`; the placeholder is a separate absolutely-positioned span). Clicks hit the wrapper div, never the input; refresh remounted the view visible → measured correctly → "fixed." This also corrects the previous entry's diagnosis: the composer was **never disabled-locked** during the opener (`ChatInput` deliberately allows type-ahead; only send is held) — the "lock" was always this geometry bug, which is why four logic-layer fixes couldn't touch it. **Fix (one guard):** when `scrollHeight` reads 0 (unmeasurable/hidden), clear the inline height and bail — CSS `rows=1` governs once visible; the next measurable input change resumes auto-resize. **Browser-verified on the full repro** (delete data → onboarding → situation chat from Home): textarea 26px, `elementFromPoint` hits the textarea, click focuses, typing works, opener on screen — no refresh. The `dcf23e8` id-capture + sessionStorage fixes remain correct and verified for the opener-vanishes-on-refresh half. 1244 vitest green, build clean.
- **Post-save chips: open question no longer overrides them** (2026-07-02): Second consecutive live miss with the same shape — Jove had left a question open before the save, then acknowledged in one line and RESUMED the question instead of offering the three path chips (2-for-2 → confirmed bug, not noise). One clause in the conductor's "After a save": *offer the chips even when you left a question open — the open question IS the "Keep this thread going" path; ask it if they choose it.* Continuity and the chips stop competing. Guard test pins the clause. **Also on record from the same run (the second mom conversation):** the early-pull fallback WORKED — the entry came back organized around one pattern with the open end honestly held ("I don't know yet if she doesn't want it or she can't do it… I'm not sure it's the same for me"), not a chronological retelling, headline in the user's own word ("bids"). One authorship wobble logged: Jove's fantasy-rebuttal ("that's not fantasy behavior, that's a real want") was folded into the entry as the user's self-talk without the user saying it back — same class as the Kevin-card over-claim, mild form; a built working version would have prevented it (the working-version-never-starts watch item now has a third data point). 1244 vitest green, build clean.

- **Recurring "input locked / opener vanishes" bug — root-caused and fixed at the right layer** (2026-07-02, merge `dcf23e8`): The 4x-recurring bug (delete user data → new situation chat → composer reads frozen → refresh → Jove's opener gone) was never a hang or stale client state — senior-eng audit traced both symptoms to ONE architectural fact: `startConversation`/`startExploration` learned the conversation id only from `message_complete`, so the id (and the `mw_active_conversation` sessionStorage bookmark downstream of it) committed **only after the opener stream fully closed**. A refresh during a long opener — exactly what a user does when the box reads frozen — left the client with no id; the server-saved opener was orphaned and the reload dropped it. The four prior fixes each patched a layer below the defect (onboarding replay, start-guard, pre-warm latency, SSE stall watchdog — the watchdog never fires on a healthy-but-slow stream). **Fix (removal-first, no new machinery):** both start paths now capture `X-Conversation-Id` from the response header the moment the fetch resolves — the same pattern `sendMessage` has used since the 2026-05-25 ghost-conversation fix; one source of truth, fragile ordering deleted. Plus the independent hygiene gap: `handleDeleteData` and `handleDeleteAccount` cleared localStorage but **not sessionStorage**, leaving `mw_active_conversation` pointing at deleted rows — both now clear it. **Composer deliberately untouched** (founder call): the lock during an active stream is correct on every turn; with the id committed early, refreshing through a slow opener is harmless. **Browser-verified end-to-end on the exact repro** (dev-reset → fresh onboarding → situation chat → refresh mid-stream with an artificially throttled opener): id in sessionStorage while the opener was still streaming, opener intact after reload, composer typable, exactly 1 conversation row (no orphans). No unit test added — pinning the header-before-stream ordering needs hook-render tooling the repo doesn't have (one-assertion infra rejected under the Complexity Gate); the browser repro is the regression check. 1244 vitest green, build clean.
- **Early-pull hardening: strip at mechanism depth + anchor fallback** (2026-07-02): The mom-run incident — the founder tapped the ready strip mid-descent (an open question literally on the table), and the save bypassed every conversational safeguard at once (no working version had been built, no completeness check, no landing) → the entry was a faithful-words-but-chronological recap: **the thin-entry failure back through the new door.** Diagnosis on record: the voice didn't regress — v0.6 moved the save door from Jove's gated offer to a strip tappable from "feeling" depth, and all the full-shape machinery lives later in the arc than the strip's invitation. Two small fixes, no prompt bloat: **(1) `CONDUCTOR_STRIP_FILL` 58 → 85** — the strip now appears at *mechanism* depth ("why it fires"), the earliest point an entry can hold its shape (one constant; regression test updated: feeling → strip hidden). **(2) The anchor's early-pull fallback** — one sentence in the composer's anchor block: if NO working version was ever said aloud, ignore the reproduction rule and follow the normal entry rules — one pattern worked down, "never a chronological retelling of the session" (closes the silent-degradation hole the anchor instruction shipped with). **Watch items logged, deliberately NOT prompt lines (soak governance):** (a) post-save chips did not fire on their first live outing (run confirmed on post-Step-4 code — model skipped the ---chips--- instruction and resumed the open thread); (b) the working version started late/never by turn ~14 — the v0.6 cadence guard may over-suppress *starting* the build, not just re-checking; (c) the either/or forced-choice tic ("which one is louder") — recurring, half-defensible, founder declined a new rule over bloat concerns; zero-sum revision of the existing either/or bullet is the path if it persists. **Escalation named:** if early pulls keep producing thin entries at the higher threshold, the marker-published working version comes off the shelf (the system would then KNOW when something buildable exists). The ready-state semantics finding feeds the founder's 5-option strip/bar design exploration. 1244 vitest green, build clean.

- **After-save paths — pull-model Step 4 of 5** (2026-07-02): The post-save moment, founder-approved via an interactive HTML demo (artifact) before build. Prompt-only, conductor "After a save" section: acknowledge in one line, no ceremony — then end the turn with the **existing `---chips---` marker** and the three paths: **Start somewhere new · Keep this thread going · Take a break**. Chips parsing verified mode-agnostic in `call-persona.ts` (runs on every turn), so the options render with this week's "handoff" tile design — nothing new built, per founder instruction. Rules: keep-going must pick up a SPECIFIC loose end from the conversation (never a generic "what else?"); break closes warmly with **no reminder promise** (the real reminder is its own follow-up feature); fresh start opens simply. The rest of Step 4 was verify-not-build: the entry card in the thread rides the existing `is_checkpoint` row + `reconcileThread`; the bar reset after save is automatic (`turnsSinceCheckpoint=0` → fill 0 under the depth-only conductor meter). **Voice impact is scoped to the post-save turn only** — everything pre-save (conversation, working version, strip, tap fidelity) is untouched, so the founder's parallel testing of Steps 1–3 stays clean. 1244 vitest green (+1 chips guard test), build clean. **Founder's end-to-end gate now covers the full loop:** conversation → landed → strip tap → overlay body = approved words (the Step 3 human diff) → confirm → card in thread → one-line acknowledgment + three chips → each path behaves.

- **Strip teaches itself + composer verbatim anchor — pull-model Steps 2.1 + 3 of 5** (2026-07-02): Two founder-approved pieces in one ship. **(2.1) The ready-strip explainer popup is gone.** `ReflectionIntroModal` (the "HOW THIS WORKS" interruption that fired the first time the strip appeared — founder: "does not feel like the right experience") is deleted; the strip now teaches itself in place: on its FIRST appearance it carries one extra line — "Nothing enters your Manual unless you build it. Tap when you're ready — or just keep talking." — with a "Got it" that clears it forever (same per-device localStorage flag the modal used, so users who saw the old popup aren't re-taught). One fewer component; the training happens where the attention should live. Bar milestones confirmed as-is (founder call: the 5 depth-stage jumps stay; no cosmetic creep). **(3) The composer anchor.** On the conductor pull path (`anchorApprovedVersion: ctx.conductorActive` from the compose route), the composer's job for the BODY changes from authoring to REPRODUCING: locate the MOST RECENT working version the user approved in the transcript (their last "yes, that's it" — or their own rewrite; latest corrections always beat earlier drafts) and carry it near-verbatim — never re-author, never upgrade register, never reintroduce corrected-away phrasing, never deepen past what they approved. Clerical outputs (section/title/tags/summary/acknowledgment) unchanged. Normal pull path untouched (flag off). 1243 vitest green (+2 anchor-framing tests; −ReflectionIntroModal). **THE GATE ON THIS STEP IS HUMAN (senior-eng requirement):** hand-run 5+ conductor conversations to a real landing, tap the strip, and diff the overlay body against the transcript's last approved working version — instruction-following here is behavioral, not testable. If drift shows, the deterministic-marker capture is the named escalation.

- **Meter rewire under the conductor — pull-model Step 2 of 5** (2026-07-02): The founder caught the tell live: the progress bar appeared **only after a browser reload**, never while chatting. Root cause — the two meter paths disagreed: the live SSE emit is experiment-aware (conductor forced `reflectionMeterEnabled=false` → no bar), while the reload-restore route (`/api/checkpoint/meter`) never learned the caller was admin (3-arg `loadConversationContext`) → resolved as a normal user with the global `reflection_meter` gate ON → served the bar with **gate-driven** fill. Fix, per the senior-eng design: **one shared resolution** — new `resolveReflectionMeter` in `persona-pipeline.ts` consumed by BOTH the live emit and the restore route (crisis/no-extraction → null lives inside it, one rule). Two regimes: normal users unchanged (gate verdict drives fill+ready); **under the conductor the open gate is NEVER fed into the meter** — fill is depth-only (can't claim 100/complete) and `ready` means only "strip visible" past `CONDUCTOR_STRIP_FILL` (58 = depth "feeling"; one constant, the invitational threshold). Conductor resolution flipped: `reflectionMeterEnabled=true` / `proposalsEnabled=false` (v0.6 has no push trigger, so detection was dead weight); the baseline force-ladder keeps push. The restore route + compose route now thread `isAdmin`; the restore route's global-gate short-circuit exempts admins. Strip copy → founder-approved "Your reflection, in your words — ready when you are." 1241 vitest green (+4 `resolveReflectionMeter` tests incl. the strip-lit-on-empty-chat regression), build clean. **Known interim:** tapping the strip composes WITHOUT the verbatim anchor until Step 3 — the body may not match the conversation's approved working version yet. Side effect: the live simulator now stops when the strip appears (its `reflectionReady` stop) — earlier than before under the conductor.

- **Conductor v0.6 — Jove stops triggering saves; the pull-model redesign, Step 1 of 5** (2026-07-02): First step of the senior-eng-reviewed reflection UX (the founder's decided contract: bar fills during conversation → passive ready strip → tap pulls full context → editable overlay → confirm → entry in thread → three post-save paths). **Prompt-only this step.** Jove no longer triggers saves AT ALL — the entire save contract (exact phrase + near-miss bans, v0.5.1) is removed from the live template and kept as a commented revert block in `conductor-prompt.ts` (founder-requested soft-removal). "When to offer the entry" + "Before you offer" merged into **"When it's landed"**: recognition/completeness logic survives; the close is a one-time acknowledgment — "That's yours now, in your words — whenever you want it" — never a save offer, never announce-language; the ask-how line references the reflection bar deliberately (web-scoped: `conductorActive` requires `isAdmin`, which the SMS path never sets — revisit at the text port). Plus the two founder-flagged polish guards: **check-in cadence** (resonance checks only when something CHANGED — the over-fire risk) and **opener variance** (the "Ok, so…" tic — never start two turns the same way). Guard tests inverted: the save phrase must now be ABSENT from the conductor render. 1237 vitest green, build clean. **Expected interim state: conductor runs cannot save until Steps 2–3 land** (the strip doesn't render under the conductor yet — a senior-eng factual catch: the experiment forces the meter off). Steps ahead: (2) meter rewire — render under conductor, depth-only fill, threshold visibility, no server "ready" claim; (3) composer anchor — most-recent-approved-version instruction on tap→compose, gated by a HUMAN transcript-vs-overlay fidelity diff; (4) post-save chips + entry-in-thread verify; (5) promote + deletion pass (SMS-dependent push machinery held until SMS capture is sunset or ported).
- **Selection tiles redesigned — "the handoff": chips as unprinted user bubbles** (2026-07-02, merge `20bfc0f`): The in-chat selection chips (guided-intake section picker + `---chips---` focus picks) were founder-flagged as "not interesting or distinct enough"; a 17-mockup, 3-round design competition (5 broad directions → 5 experimental → 3 refinements of the winner, staged in a live-comparison artifact) landed on **"the handoff"**: each tile wears the user's own sent-bubble shape — the real `Bubble` USER_STYLE geometry (bubble radius + 5px top-right tail), right-aligned — as a **blind emboss**: unprinted paper relief at rest (parchment fill, inset relief shadows, letterpress titles in light), Jove's navy blooming from the tail corner on hover, and the user-bubble fill "printing" on press. The concept is product-true: tapping a chip seeds the user's side of the conversation, so the chip is literally a reply not yet said, and choosing it converts Jove's offer (navy) into the user's words (user-bubble fill). **On select the chosen tile prints while its siblings vanish** (founder-requested), then `onSelect` fires after a 260ms beat (0ms under reduced motion) — that staging lives ONCE in the new `SelectionTileGroup` (`SelectionTile.tsx`); both pickers render through it (net duplication down: their copied group-wrapper markup is gone). Chevron dropped; staggered press-in entrance; dark (Hearth) gets night-tuned relief values; **no new tokens** (parchment / user-bg / persona tints all pre-existing). Verified live in preview on both themes with all states staged against the compiled CSS — which caught a real bug pre-ship (the entrance animation's `fill-mode: both` pinned `opacity:1` and silently defeated the vanish class; staging classes now clear the animation). Build clean, **1237** vitest green (one source-shape assertion updated to the group API — `value: layer.name` — same intent, LAYERS stays the single source). **Owed 30-second confirm (not run in-session — devtest's drifted state wouldn't fire the Guided door, a known account issue):** start a Guided session in prod → tap a section → the tile should print peach, the other four should fade, then Jove replies. Design artifacts: the 17-option comparison page (private artifact, session-scoped).

- **Conductor v0.5.1 — the save trigger is the ONE exception, near-misses banned by name** (2026-07-01): The overwork-run incident: v0.5 executed a *perfect* close — say-it-back-in-your-own-words landing, working version as speech with resonance checks, the completeness check surfacing NEW material (the weed→next-day-anxiety loop), offer made ("Do you want to keep that in your Manual, the way it reads?"), user said yes — and then the model performed the save with the banned near-miss **"I want to put that down," twice**; the detector regex (requires "in your Manual") never fired and **nothing saved**. Root cause: v0.5 banned announce-language ("Never say you're putting something down") and required announce-shaped trigger language (the exact save sentence) without drawing the line between them — the two sound alike and the model blended them into the banned form at execution. Fix (prompt-only, conductor): the contract now names the exact sentence as **the ONE exception to the never-announce rule**, bans the near-misses by name ("I want to put that down," "let me put that down," "let me write this up" — "all fail silently and leave you claiming an action that never happened"), and adds a self-repair line (if you notice you said one, say the exact sentence now). Guard tests unchanged and green (exact-phrase + verbatim-clause + never-announce assertions all hold against the new text); 1237 vitest, build clean. **Also on record from the run:** the v0.5 watch item (over-digging past a genuine "yes") did NOT materialize — the model honored real landings and used "yes" streaks correctly after resonance checks.

- **Conductor v0.5 — research-hardened close + VERBATIM save** (2026-07-01): The deep-research pass (105 agents, adversarially verified; findings in the 2026-07-01 session) plus the purpose-run debrief ("really enjoyed the conversation, first time — the conclusion was not great") landed as five prompt changes + one code change. **Prompt (conductor only):** (1) "Build it in the open" revised — the working version is SAID as plain speech, never a formatted block; every check is a resonance check on the user's own fresh words; **"ok" is not a yes** (ask which word they'd change). (2) New "How you know there's more — and when it's landed" — completeness read from the user's MANNER: groping/fresh-image/present-tense = more underneath; exact-words/charge-easing/own-fresh-restatement = landed; fluent polish = the rehearsed version, go underneath. (3) Stall repertoire in "When people get stuck" — every move threaded from their last words (never a topic-reset hypothetical), exception questions for stalls, user-AUTHORED hypotheticals, ask for the feeling directly (it doesn't volunteer), scaling fit-check only near the close. (4) Save contract reshaped to offer-don't-announce — "putting that down" is a banned false-action claim; on yes, the exact phrase + **the approved entry text travels IN the save message**. (5) New "After a save" — the save is real; never deny it or re-render it (fixes the purpose-run incident where Jove said "I didn't save anything yet" after a real save). **Code — the verbatim save:** under `conductorActive`, the entry body = `extractProposalProse(saveMessage)` UNTOUCHED (new helper in detect-checkpoint.ts, same transition-boundary contract as the detector/stripper); the composer keeps section/tags/headline/summary but can no longer re-author the body (the purpose-run card had re-written the approved draft back into the register the user rejected). Explicit fallback: <40 chars of prose after the phrase (user rushed "just save it") → composer body, dev-logged. **1233 vitest green** (+4: verbatim-helper unit tests + v0.5 guard tests incl. the exact-phrase + verbatim-clause contract), build clean. **Known first-run watch item (stated at ship):** the "ok is not landed" rule can overcorrect into won't-close/interrogation fatigue — the tell to watch is whether Jove honors a genuine "yes, that's exactly it" or digs past it.
- **Checkpoint follow-up no longer denies the save it just made** (2026-07-01, commit `9ac3659`): Founder-reported — after confirming an entry, the "Saved to Work and career" card showed correctly, then Jove's *next* (post-confirm) message contradicted it: "I didn't save anything yet — I was about to propose it. Let me actually put it in front of you." **Root cause.** On a successful checkpoint the card (`checkpoint_meta`) is the entry's surface, so the message row's own text drops the transition line. The success path in `call-persona.ts` reused `stripCheckpointFromText`, which is built for the *suppression* case: when the model's lead-in doesn't stand on its own, it substitutes the grounding fallback **"Tell me what's going on for you right now."** That fallback (a) rendered as a stray line inside the saved card, and (b) poisoned the post-confirm turn's replayed history — the entry text lives only in `checkpoint_meta` (never fed back to the model), so Jove's own transcript read "asked a grounding question, then the user says they saved it," incoherent enough to override the pinned "Saved." and make it deny the save. **Fix.** New `stripCheckpointForCard` helper — same `findCheckpointTransition` contract, but returns `""` (never the fallback) when there's no standalone lead-in; the card + acknowledgment bubble already carry the turn. One call-site swap in the composition-success branch. **Test.** New `stripCheckpointForCard` describe block (4 cases) including a regression guard pinning the exact leak and contrasting the two helpers side by side. **Still latent (flagged, not fixed):** the structural root — the post-confirm turn never sees the saved entry text. Removing the fallback removes the trigger; if the denial ever recurs *without* the stray line, the follow-up work is to feed the saved entry into the post-confirm turn. Build clean, 1233 vitest green. Server-side pipeline logic behind an authed flow (not browser-verifiable in preview).
- **Composer input legibility + softer focus** (2026-07-01, merge `626d09a`): Two small UX fixes to the chat composer (`ChatInput.tsx`), founder-reported. **(1) Typed text is now legible.** The textarea rendered the user's typed words as *italic Newsreader at 14.5px* — hard to read while composing, and sub-16px triggers iOS Safari's focus-zoom. Switched to **upright, 16px** (italic now stays only on the ghost placeholder, so a prompt reads distinctly from real text), and the caret uses the ink token instead of a faint 34%-opacity navy tint (you can see where you're typing). **(2) The "blue line" on focus is gone.** It was a global `textarea:focus-visible` rule drawing a hard 2px persona-navy `border-bottom` — a straight line inside the rounded pill. The composer is now opted out of that rule (`textarea.mw-composer-input`), and the focus signal moved to the pill itself: persona border + a soft 3px ring on focus (standard container pattern, keeps a visible focus state for a11y). Other textareas keep the underline. Verified live in the mobile preview (upright readable text + soft pill focus). Style-only, no logic/schema/flag change; build clean, 1229 vitest green. *(Open call, deferred: the focus ring still uses the app's navy accent — can be neutralized to a warm/ink tone if the objection was the color, not the line.)*

- **Conductor v0.4 — the entry is built in the open, approved in pieces** (2026-07-01): Two founder-approved prompt additions to the conductor variant, from the Kevin-run debrief (the run passed the timing bar — user-authored reveal + two real corrections before the offer — but closed too fast and the saved entry froze the pre-correction frame). **(1) "Build it in the open":** once the pattern takes shape, Jove keeps a one-to-two-sentence working version alive in the user's words, folds each correction in, and briefly says the updated version back ("still right, or off?") — so by the offer the user has already approved every piece; the save is a formality, never a reveal. **(2) "Before you offer":** no generic "anything else?" close — a case-specific push (one concrete direction: another context, an unfinished thread) OR "should I put together a draft of what we've been building?", the user picks; offer only those two paths. Prompt-only, conductor variant only (admin-scoped, live voice untouched); guard tests (crisis clause, save contract, zero cross-domain strings) still green against the v0.4 text. 1240 vitest, build clean. **Flagged next (not in this ship):** the composer constraint — under conductor the saved entry body should be the last approved working version near-verbatim (composer structures, never re-authors); needs a design pass on how approved text travels conversation→save, best done after v0.4 transcripts exist.

- **`emerging_pattern_snippet` pruned end-to-end + `profiles.modal_progress` column dropped** (2026-07-01, commit `830f01f`): The follow-up to the Modal 2 removal below, resolving both of that ship's flagged leftovers. **(a) The write-only extraction field is gone.** After Modal 2 and its SSE feed chain were deleted, the extractor still *generated and stored* `emerging_pattern_snippet` every turn even though nothing read it — pure per-turn prompt attention + output tokens. Removed end-to-end from the extraction layer (`extraction.ts`): the prompt's "EMERGING PATTERN SNIPPET" section + its JSON-schema line (renumbering the two sections below it), the `parseSnippet` helper + `SNIPPET_MAX_WORDS` + the call in `mergeExtractionState`, and the `ExtractionState` field + its `defaultState` default. Tests: deleted the whole `extraction-parser.test.ts` (every test in it existed only to exercise `parseSnippet`), removed the "does not leak" test from `extraction.test.ts`, and stripped the leftover fixture line from three other suites. Admin: removed the field record from the extraction-map page. Old stored values in existing `extraction_state` JSON are harmless (ignored on read). **(b) The dead column is dropped.** `profiles.modal_progress` had zero readers/writers after the modal-ladder removal (GET `/api/modal-progress` selects only `created_at`; door-intro gating moved to `door_intros_seen`) — dropped via migration `20260701000000_drop_profiles_modal_progress.sql` (auto-applies to prod on merge) and removed from the schema-map admin listing. Docs: updated the extraction-state shape in `system.md` + `master.md`. Net **+13/−228** across 11 files. Build clean, **1229** vitest green (−11 vs the prior count: the deleted parser file + the leak test). **Process:** base branch was 2 commits behind `origin/main` — the parallel session that removed Modal 2 (`7b209c1`) hadn't merged when this task was scoped; fast-forwarded onto it first so the field was genuinely write-only before pruning, then merged clean.

- **Hung-stream lock fixed: SSE stall watchdog + early conversation-id capture** (2026-07-01): The reported bug — opening a Situation conversation showed Jove's opener but the composer stayed locked; a refresh unlocked it but the opener "disappeared." **Root cause (repro'd live on a dev server; the server side was proven healthy — the SSE body was complete and well-formed): `parseSSEStream`'s `reader.read()` had NO timeout.** When a connection hangs after delivering `message_complete` but before the close (network/proxy quirk on prod), the read promise never resolves → `isStreaming` stays true forever (composer locked), and `startConversation` never reaches its post-stream code → the client never records the new conversation id → on refresh the opener conversation is orphaned (saved in the DB, unknown to the client). **Fix, two small client pieces:** (1) a **stall watchdog** in `parseSSEStream` (60s with zero bytes → cancel the reader and resolve `{stalled}`; generous because first-token latency and the checkpoint compose gap are real silences) — a stall after a completed message recovers silently (the turn rendered; only the close was lost), a stall before any message surfaces the standard "Connection lost. Try again."; (2) **conversation id captured at `message_complete` time** (fill-only, never overwrites) instead of only after stream end, so even a refresh mid-hang can't orphan a server-created conversation. Protects every streaming turn (openers, normal sends, post-confirm), not just the incident path. 1250 vitest green (+3 stall tests incl. the exact hung-after-complete shape), build clean. *(Watch item: the same live repro measured a ~40s opener generation on a cold dev server — if prod openers run long, that lock-while-streaming window is by design but worth revisiting.)*
- **Pattern-forming "halfway there" onboarding modal removed + its whole feed chain** (2026-07-01, commit `7b209c1`): The `PatternFormingModal` ("Modal 2") — a one-time onboarding popup that interrupted a live conversation mid-session once the extraction layer detected a forming pattern — was deleted along with everything that existed **only** to feed it. **Removal-first, end to end:** the component + its test; the three SSE trigger fields the server pushed to the client every turn purely to gate this popup (`emergingPatternSnippet` / `hasLayerEmergingOrBeyond` / `concreteExamples`) — stripped from `call-persona.ts`, `sse-parser.ts`, `useChat.ts`, `MainApp.tsx`, `MobileSession.tsx`; the **`modal_progress` ladder** whose sole job was sequencing this popup — the `POST /api/modal-progress` endpoint and every place that advanced it (the door-intros first-intro bump, the silent first-checkpoint auto-advance in `MobileSession`, the dev-reset reset); and the `modal_2_shown` analytics event. **Kept:** `GET /api/modal-progress` — two of its fields (`signup_at_ms`, `is_anonymous`) still feed door-intro analytics and anonymous-user modal suppression. Net **−525/+45 lines** across 18 files. **Two flagged leftovers — both since RESOLVED in `830f01f` (see top of ship log):** (a) the extractor still *generated and stored* `emerging_pattern_snippet` every turn even though nothing read it now — a per-turn prompt-attention + token cost, flagged as a prune candidate in the extraction-map admin page; (b) the `profiles.modal_progress` column is now fully unused (no reader/writer) — flagged as a drop candidate in the schema-map admin page; a drop migration was deferred pending founder go. Build clean, **1237** vitest green (−1: the `modal_2_shown` analytics assertion). *(Live in-browser verification not run — the popup only fired several turns into a real conversation, and the worktree session can't start the dev server per CLAUDE.md; verified by build + full suite. A follow-up session was spun off to do the extraction-field prune + column-drop call.)*
- **Conductor voice variant (admin-only experiment, default OFF)** (2026-07-01): The founder's self-contained "conductor" prompt (v0.3 — "don't leave a live moment" + "hand over the connection") wired as a third experiment variant behind the existing `baseline_experiment_gates` table (new `conductor` key + panel switch; no new table/route). **Safety:** the prompt carries REBUILT_LIMITS #2's crisis language VERBATIM ("The one exception — crisis" section, 988 + Crisis Text Line + non-negotiable-trigger examples) — locked by test — and the pipeline crisis layers (phrase detector + 988 append, gate crisis block) are variant-independent. **Structure:** the conductor branch reads NO force toggles and appends NO Tier-3/REBUILT_MECHANICS, so the cross-domain "anywhere else" instructions it would fight are structurally impossible to leak (guard tests: crisis present, save contract present, zero cross-domain strings). Carries the minimal save contract so entries really save; known watch-item: the server composer re-writes after confirm and may drift from words settled in conversation — to be flagged in transcripts, not pre-fixed. Precedence conductor > baseline when both switches are on; same admin triple-scoping; gate open under conductor (crisis still blocks); Situation-mode runs (conductor-composed-with-guided-intake is the logged FINAL arm of the ladder). 1247 vitest green (+5), build clean. Teardown rides with the baseline experiment (conductor-prompt.ts header).

- **Baseline experiment follow-ups: empty-system-block fix + plain-language feature-gate copy** (2026-06-30, commits `9f3e543`, `7e5a95a`): Two fixes after the strip-to-baseline harness went live. **(1) Empty system block → "Connection lost."** With Baseline mode on and all forces off, a fresh turn produces a completely empty `dynamic` prompt tail (no Tier 3, no Manual, no session context — the point of the strip), and the web path still pushed `{ type:"text", text:"" }` into the Anthropic `system` array, which **400s** (`"system: text content blocks must be non-empty"`) and surfaced as the chat "Connection lost" card. Fix: **drop empty text blocks** in the `systemBlocks` assembly (`call-persona.ts`) — a no-op for rebuilt/legacy (their `dynamic` is always populated) and it lets the stripped baseline through. Confirmed against the real Anthropic API (empty → 400, filtered → 200); guarded by a source test that keeps the filter from being removed. Also covers the mid-ladder cases (a fresh turn's tail stays empty until the user is returning or the Tier-3/intake force is on). **(2) Feature-gate copy rewritten for legibility.** After the gates confused live use — `situation` OFF silently reroutes new chats to Guided; `reflection_meter` ON silently turns OFF Jove's auto-propose (they're mutually exclusive web capture models) — the admin Feature Gates panel now shows, per switch, its **default**, a one-line "what it controls," and plain ON-vs-OFF with the interaction gotchas called out (⚠), plus a **global-scope** header and a "· changed" marker on any gate off its default. Copy-only, no logic. Build clean, **1240** vitest green (+ the empty-block source guard). *(Deployed via Vercel on push; the migration table `baseline_experiment_gates` had already applied after a CI re-run cleared a transient "Supabase CLI rate limit" setup failure — not a migration error.)* **Known interaction (not yet coded):** the baseline experiment is admin-scoped but still reads the GLOBAL `reflection_meter` / `situation` gates, so running the pilot cleanly currently requires flipping those global product switches (reflection_meter OFF so Jove pushes; situation ON so the pilot runs in Situation mode). A scoped fix — baseline forces the push model + honors the requested mode for the admin's conversation only — was offered but not built pending founder go.

- **Confirmed reflection now reliably appears in the chat thread — post-confirm DB reconcile** (2026-06-30, commit `ce5b90f`, merge `7d4c9be`): The reported bug — after a user **builds a reflection** (the user-pulled Reflection meter → review overlay → confirm), the entry reliably saved to the Manual but the chat thread didn't reliably update; it only showed correctly after a reload/conversation switch. **Root cause (verified by reading + two subagent reviews): the live thread relied on the SSE stream as its ONLY source of truth after a confirm, while everything is already persisted server-side.** Two gaps, one cause. (1) In the user-pulled path the saved-reflection card **never entered the live thread** — `composeReflection` writes an `is_checkpoint` message row to the DB (`/api/checkpoint/compose`, "so a history reload renders it") but the client only opened the review overlay; it never appended the card to the client `messages` array, so the card appeared only on reload (`loadConversation`). (2) Jove's post-confirm follow-up is **stream-only and fragile**: the confirm follow-up is a full LLM turn streamed back; on a mid-stream interruption the catch just logged, and on the retry path (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` abort or a network blip → retry) the second attempt hits the server's idempotent branch and returns **JSON, not SSE** — the client then only called `loadManual()` and returned, never reconciling the thread. The follow-up assistant message *was* persisted (`call-persona.ts`), so it existed in the DB but never showed until a reload. Net: the Manual re-synced after a confirm; the thread never did. **Fix (removal-first — make the thread re-read the DB like the Manual already does):** a narrow `reconcileThread(conversationId)` in `useChat` that re-reads the conversation's messages and replaces the client list — **nothing else** (it deliberately does NOT touch the reflection meter or Manual, so it can't race the "meter starts over after save" reset — the trap in reusing `loadConversation`). Wired into `confirmCheckpoint` at BOTH settle points: as the **last writer** in a `finally` after `streamFromResponse` (covers success + interruption) and on the idempotent-JSON path. Because the re-read runs last and replaces messages by matching DB id, the authoritative DB view wins with no flicker/dup. Also collapsed the duplicated fetch-and-map into one `fetchThreadFromDb` helper now shared by `loadConversation`, `switchConversation`, and `reconcileThread` (removes the parallel copy the fix would otherwise have added). **Timeout non-fix (record correction):** the senior-eng review flagged the 30s client `AbortController` as a total-duration abort killing healthy long replies — a closer read showed it is **already** effectively time-to-headers: `callPersona` returns its `ReadableStream` synchronously, the route flushes headers immediately, so `await fetch` resolves fast and the timer is cleared before body streaming. It does NOT cap healthy streams, so no timeout change was made (and the reconcile covers the rare slow-headers→retry case anyway). **Reviews:** Explore-agent trace + senior-engineer change-review = ship (right architecture, not a hack; required the narrow reconcile over `loadConversation`, last-writer ordering, and keeping meter/Manual out — all followed). **Scope:** 1 file (`useChat.ts`, +64/−37). No schema/flag/route/model change. Build clean, **1238** vitest green (15 useChat + 12 confirm-route among them). **Owed 30-second confirm (not run in-session — needs the `reflection_meter` gate ON + a deep enough conversation):** build a reflection on a throttled connection → confirm → the saved card + Jove's "Saved…" reply should appear in the thread live, no reload.

- **Guided intake: tapping an option no longer kills desktop typing (the "text input not clickable after the second selection" fix)** (2026-06-30, commit `61d538b`, merge `4ddaf4e`): The reported bug — in guided intake on desktop, after tapping a second option tile the text box goes dead (you can't type; a page refresh clears it). **Diagnosis (reproduced live in-browser, devtest, ≥1030px): a focus problem, not a pointer/overlay one.** Confirmed the composer textarea is *never* given `disabled`/`readOnly` (only the send/mic buttons are), is not covered by any overlay (the `CheckpointOverlay` returns null when closed), and accepts focus and wins the hit-test across its full width in every reproduced state — so "not clickable" is really "the caret isn't in the box, so keystrokes land nowhere." **Root cause:** a real mouse click on a `SelectionTile` moves DOM focus onto the button; the tile then **unmounts** (the picker/chips hide while Jove replies, gated on `!isLoading && !isStreaming`), so focus falls to `<body>`. The prior desktop fix (commit `0357b4f`) chased the lost caret back at turn-end via a `requestAnimationFrame(() => textarea.focus())` keyed on the `disabled` edge — a deferred focus racing other focus-affecting work (modal siblings, re-renders) in the same frame, which it loses unpredictably. That's why the first tap felt fine and later taps didn't, and why chasing focus was the wrong shape. **Fix — stop the focus from leaving instead of chasing it:** `SelectionTile` now suppresses its own focus-steal with `onMouseDown={(e) => e.preventDefault()}` (the standard pattern for controls that act without grabbing focus). The caret never leaves the composer on a tap, so the dead-input state can't arise. One line on the shared control covers **both** surfaces — the `---sections---` picker (`SectionPicker`) and the `---chips---` focus picks (`QuickReplyChips`) both render `SelectionTile`. Mouse-path only: keyboard Enter/Space activation and the press/focus-visible states are unaffected; `click` still fires on iOS. **Removal-minded but not removed:** the `ChatInput` rAF focus-restore is **kept** — it still does its reliable job (returning the caret after a *typed* message's turn completes, where there's no unmounting button to race) — with its now-stale comment corrected so it no longer claims to cover the selection case. **Senior-engineer subagent change-review = ship** (high confidence the `preventDefault` eliminates the root cause; endorsed keeping the rAF for the typed-turn case and confirmed the accessibility/touch safety — on touch it keeps focus *off* the composer, i.e. no unwanted keyboard pop). **Scope:** 2 files — `SelectionTile.tsx` (+9, the guard + comment), `ChatInput.tsx` (comment only). No logic/schema/flag/model change. Build clean, **1238** vitest green (no new tests — there is no component-test harness for these mobile files; count unchanged from the prior ship). **Live real-mouse verification NOT completed in-session** — the preview environment's browser annotation extension intercepts coordinate clicks, and the test account's state drifted (57 sessions) so the Guided door stopped reliably rendering the section picker; verified by build + full suite + the reproduced diagnosis. **Owed 30-second confirm:** desktop → open Guided intake → pick a section → pick a focus option (the second tap) → start typing *without* clicking; text should land in the box (and the tile's press animation should still show). **Process note:** branch was 1 commit behind `origin/main` (`9f3e543`, the baseline empty-system-block fix — no file overlap); rebased clean, merged `--no-ff`, fast-forward-free push.

- **Extraction field guards + strip-to-baseline experiment harness (admin-only, default OFF)** (2026-06-30, commit `508043b`): Three coordinated changes. **(1) Extraction correctness — two fields made monotonic to match the count guard.** The merge already forces `concrete_examples`/`distinct_contexts` to a high-water max because Sonnet re-reads from a smaller window and returns lower counts; **`depth` and `language_bank` had no such guard.** `depth` now never drops below the deepest level reached (max by `DEPTH_LEVELS` index); `language_bank` now **accumulates** (union by phrase, charge held at its high-water mark, layers unioned) instead of being replaced wholesale — so a charged phrase captured earlier can't silently drop out and fail the gate's `builtOnCharged` check. Growth is bounded: high/medium phrases kept unbounded (the gated material), low-charge capped at 20 (oldest evicted) so the bank fed back into extraction input each turn can't balloon over long conversations. **(2) `REBUILT_MECHANICS` carved into `MECHANICS_PARTS`** that reassemble **byte-identical** (guarded by `voice-mechanics-carve.test.ts` against a frozen snapshot) — the live rebuilt voice is provably unchanged; the carve only enables per-rung add-back in the experiment below. **(3) Strip-to-baseline experiment** (TEMPORARY, admin-only, OFF by default): a `baseline_experiment_gates` table + `BaselineExperimentPanel` (admin → Debug → System gates) with a master switch + six force toggles (flag-don't-grab / seam rule / mechanics deepening / character shaping / tier3-intake / re-add gate) to climb the add-back ladder by hand. The "baseline" voice variant strips Jove to a neutral identity + safety/author LIMITS + a bare save contract, measuring how close raw model judgment lands a save at a real seam. **Triple-gated so a stripped Jove can never reach a real user:** admin-only writes (`requireAdmin`), applied only when the conversation's user is an admin (`baselineActive = isAdmin && enabled`, computed in `/api/chat`), and the switch table is read only for admins. **Crisis/988 safety is NOT behind any toggle** — it stays on three independent layers (`handleCrisisDetection`, the gate's preserved crisis block, the LIMITS #2 clause) even fully stripped, locked by a test. Reads fail closed to OFF. **Teardown condition documented in four places** (migration, admin-panel banner, `baseline-experiment.ts`, this entry): when the experiment concludes, delete the table, route, panel, threading, and module. Build clean, **1238** vitest green (+11 new: carve byte-identical guard, baseline reader/crisis/leak proofs). Migration `20260630000000` is additive + default-off + admin-scoped → no prod behavior change on apply. *(Live in-browser run is the next step — flip Baseline mode on in admin, start a Situation conversation, talk to stripped Jove; not auto-run.)* **Process note:** branch started on a stale base and origin/main moved twice mid-ship (reflection-meter `ef4e481`, checkpoint-dials `3bec707`); a rebase broke mid-conflict and was recovered via reflog reset, then integrated by merge — one conflict in the `loadConversationContext` signature (took upstream's `surface` param and added `isAdmin` alongside).

- **Checkpoint dials relabeled in plain admin language** (2026-06-30, commit `3bec707`): The four Checkpoint-dial names and help texts in the admin Tuning panel leaked engine jargon ("Min concrete scenes," "Cooldown (turns since last)," "Engagement failsafe turn," "Depth floor") that an admin shouldn't need the firing internals to parse. **Copy-only rename, no logic touched:** the labels/help live in the single-source `CHECKPOINT_TUNING_FIELDS` map (`checkpoint-tuning.ts`), so editing there flows to both the panel and the API — **Examples before proposing** / **Messages between entries** / **Propose-anyway after (messages)** / **Minimum depth**, each with a help line that leads with what the knob does then which way is more eager. Two fixes beyond the labels, both in `CheckpointTuningPanel.tsx`: (1) the **depth dropdown** now shows a plain gloss for each stored value (`surface → "what happened"` … `origin → "where it comes from"`) so the non-obvious "surface = no depth required" reading is legible (stored enum values unchanged); (2) the **locked quality-floor gates** were relabeled out of internals — "Pattern engaged / has-mechanism / behavior-driver link" → **"Real pattern, with a 'why'"**, "Charged-language requirement" → **"Built on the user's own strong words"**, "Crisis block" → **"Never during a crisis"** — and the intro + save/reset notices swapped "next turn" → "next user message." Triggered by a founder readability review that first traced all four dials end-to-end (Explore agent + direct read): confirmed every dial is genuinely wired through `validateMaterialQuality` + `applyCheckpointGates` (and the reflection meter), fails open to `CHECKPOINT_TUNING_DEFAULTS`, and the three floors are truly un-lowerable from admin — *nothing broken or unhooked*; the only substantive note was a config one (live `depth_floor` was set to `surface`, which disables the depth gate). Build clean, **1227** vitest green (pre-commit hook; copy change touched no test assertions — the `concrete scenes N/N` strings the tests match are dev-log gate reasons, not the renamed labels). *(Live in-browser verification not run — admin copy change, worktree session with preview unavailable per CLAUDE.md; verified by build + full suite. One look at Tuning → Checkpoint dials is the 30-second confirm.)* **Process note:** the edits first landed in the shared main checkout by a worktree path-translation slip (Explore returned main-repo paths, used verbatim); main was exactly in sync with `origin/main` (0/0) and the worktree branch was empty, so they were committed directly on the up-to-date main and pushed via fast-forward rather than relocated — re-validated build + suite against that tree before committing.

- **Reflection meter becomes the web capture model; Jove-pushed bloom card removed (the "rejected checkpoint keeps re-proposing" fix)** (2026-06-30, commit `ef4e481`): The reported bug — after the user taps "Not yet" on a proposed checkpoint, Jove re-proposes the same entry every message. **Root cause:** a UI rejection is recorded on the message row (`checkpoint_meta.status`) but never fed back into the next turn's prompt — the system has a circuit-breaker for *gate*-suppressed re-proposals (`priorCheckpointSuppressed` → POST-SUPPRESSION) but **no equivalent for a user rejection**, and the extraction brief keeps whispering "there's a real piece here" because a button-tap rejection never reaches the extraction layer (it only listens to words). **Fix = adopt the model the bug can't exist in, not bolt on rejection-memory:** switch the **web** surface from Jove-pushed proposals to the **user-pulled reflection meter** (the fill bar + persistent "A reflection's ready" top strip; the user pulls on their own clock → on-demand compose → review overlay). With no push there's nothing to re-propose. The reflection meter was already built behind the `reflection_meter` gate (default OFF) — this makes it the intended web model and **simplifies the UI to match:** removed the composer-locking **"bloom" card** + all its Not-yet / fly-up / `reflectionDeferred` defer machinery (~130 lines + 2 dead keyframes), promoted the existing **top strip** to appear whenever the meter is ready (non-locking — the composer stays open; the strip persists across reload via the latch+restore path, and a confirmed save clears it). A one-time **`ReflectionIntroModal`** (localStorage, per-device) explains the meter the first time the strip appears; **`PatternFormingModal` is suppressed under the meter** (its copy promised a pushed card the pull model never delivers). **THE BLOCKER (senior-eng switchover audit):** a *global* gate flip would silently **freeze Manual capture on SMS/text** — that channel has no meter and no pull/compose surface, so Jove-pushed checkpoints are its *only* capture path. Scoped the meter **web-only** via a new pure `deriveProposalFlags(gates, surface)` helper (text passes `surface:"text"` → meter forced off, proposals stay on); locked with **4 unit tests** asserting the SMS-capture invariant. **Removal-first:** also deleted the now-dead `reflectionPreview`/`preview` gist plumbing end-to-end (server emit → SSE type → hook state/return → MainApp thread → the strip render) — the only consumer was the bloom; a quick attempt to surface it on the strip was **reverted** when live verification showed it rendered extraction's third-person `current_thread` ("user pulled back without the friend knowing"), off-voice for a user-facing surface. Net **−78 lines** this commit. **Verified live in-browser** (devtest, `reflection_meter` temporarily flipped on in the dev DB then reverted; real Opus): bloom gone, strip shows at ready (clean single line), the explainer modal fired once with the exact approved copy in the real walnut design, dismiss set the seen-flag and it did **not** re-fire on reload or across threads, **composer was enabled** while ready (lock removed), reload **restored** the strip, the pull → "Building…" → review overlay worked end-to-end, and the meter **reset to 0** after a checkpoint was created (cooldown). Build clean, **1227** vitest green (+4 `deriveProposalFlags`). **Still behind the `reflection_meter` gate (default OFF) — flip it in admin to enable; text/SMS is unaffected either way.** **Phased cleanup (per the audit):** the Jove-pushed pipeline stays — it's the rollback lever AND the live SMS capture path, so it is **not** deletable until the meter soaks in prod and SMS gets a pull path or is sunset. **Process note:** branch was 2 commits behind `origin/main` (the dev-simulator ship `17a8418`/`4c3d1f8`); rebased onto current live — one conflict in the `loadConversationContext` signature (upstream had just removed `personaModesOverride`) resolved by taking their signature and adding only the new `surface` param, then fast-forwarded.

- **Dev simulator rebuilt to drive the REAL guided-intake path live (the "list items never showed" fix)** (2026-06-30, commit `17a8418`): The admin simulator couldn't show guided intake's tappable list items (the `---sections---` picker, the `---chips---` focus picks). **Root cause was architectural, two-fold:** (1) it ran the entire Jove↔fake-user exchange **server-side** in `/api/dev-simulate` and then re-rendered the conversation **from the DB** — but chips/sections are **live-only affordances** (delivered on the `message_complete` SSE event, attached to React message state, stripped out of stored `content`, never persisted), so a DB replay has nothing to render; (2) the loop generated a fake **user** message on turn 1, so Jove never hit the `message===null` opener turn that emits `---sections---`. The simulator literally could not show list items no matter what Jove did. **Fix (replace, not patch):** a **client-driven** simulator — a fake user drives the genuine app (`startConversation` → `/api/chat` → `sendMessage`, the same paths a person hits), so the opener, section cards, focus chips, and the `[selected from options]` tap all render live. New admin endpoint `/api/dev-simulate/turn` returns one fake-user line per turn (thin wrapper over the existing `generateSimulatedUserMessage`; new `snapToOption` forces the reply onto a real option when affordances are present so the chip send registers as a genuine tap). The driver is an **effect-driven state machine in `useChat`** (reads fresh render state, not refs, to avoid stale-closure races between turns); `simBusy` is state, not a ref, so clearing it re-fires the loop. **Stops at the entry-ready moment under BOTH models — `activeCheckpoint` (auto-propose) OR `reflectionReady` (user-pulled meter)** — leaving it pending for the user to confirm. The `reflectionReady` stop was **added during live verification**: the test account runs the pulled meter where Jove never proposes, so the original checkpoint-only stop ran past the natural end and pushed Jove into a repeat-question loop. **Removal-first:** net **−208 lines**; deleted the old server route + test; dropped the now-dead `personaModesOverride` plumbing from `call-persona` + `persona-pipeline` (the live sim uses the **signed-in account's** persona — the genuine path — set it in Settings to test a neurotype). **Verified live in-browser** (devtest account, real Opus/Haiku): section picker rendered **5 tiles** with exact section names, the fake user tapped through, Jove deepened a real episodic scene, and the run ended cleanly at the reflection-ready handoff with **no repeat-question loop**. Build clean, **1223** vitest green (+6 `snapToOption`, −the deleted route tests). **Two flagged follow-ups (NOT done):** (a) **focus chips (`---chips---`) reliability** — the section picker is confirmed working end-to-end, but in the test runs Jove went straight from the section into a direct episodic question rather than offering a focus-chip sub-list; the OPEN-THE-SECTION prompt *describes* offering moments but doesn't force the `---chips---` marker the way the opener forces `---sections---` (a one-line prompt change if we want chips firing reliably — now observable live in the rebuilt sim); (b) **minor** — `reflectionReady` propagates ~1 turn late, so the sim may run one extra turn before stopping (harmless for a watched run; left rather than over-engineer a settle-delay). **Process note:** branch was even with `origin/main` (7ce122c); committed and **fast-forwarded** onto main — no rebase needed.

- **Guided intake: focus chips cue a moment to retrieve, not a theme to confirm (the "saves too fast/thin" fix)** (2026-06-26, commit `ac0f0ff`): Guided intake's documented failure — it builds entries too fast on thin material (single recalled scene + a leading "yes"), flagged in the `21a0ea9` entry below when a "propose sooner" change was **backed out** for aiming the wrong way. An applied-psychologist review reframed the mechanism: the failure is **confabulation, not thinness, and its root is the focus-pick, not the checkpoint gate.** The focus chips handed the user a *theme/category* ("how change lands," "getting overloaded") — a category cue, which the autobiographical-memory literature (Williams overgeneral-memory line; specificity ≠ detail) shows biases toward overgeneral, made-to-fit recall = the leading-yes. **You can't gate your way out:** the gate counts structure and a confabulated scene has structure too, so a stricter bar would punish the genuine single-vivid-scene recognition the product exists for while a compliant user just generates the extra scene — the symmetric reason the earlier propose-*sooner* change failed. **Fix is upstream in the flow — three edits to existing prompt text in the guided-intake Tier-3 block (`system-prompt.ts`), no new gate / flag / model call (anti-overbuild):** (1) **focus chips cue a specific moment to RETRIEVE** ("the last time a sound made you leave a room"), not a theme to confirm, teed with "something that actually happened, not a topic" + an always-present "something else" out — an event cue retrieves a real episode where a category cue invites a summary; (2) a **provenance test** in MISSING-PIECE STEERING — don't propose until the user reached for one specific the focus chip didn't hand them ("a category dressed as a scene, not a scene"); (3) one sentence calibrating Jove that intake material is **recalled-not-live — treat as cool until the scene heats up**, overriding the base character's "they brought you a live situation" premise for this mode. The provenance test is **distinct from MECHANICS' never-said test** (HARD BLOCK on duplicated decision logic): never-said checks the *entry* against the user's words (is it a discovery); provenance checks the *scene* against the menu (is it retrieved) — a different point in the pipeline, no second copy. **Scope:** one file, `system-prompt.ts` (+ the 3 regenerated tier3 snapshots). Build clean, **1223** vitest green. *(Live in-browser verification not run — prompt text, not browser-observable; worktree session.)* **NOT soak-validated — confidence is high on the memory science, medium on whether this wording pulls real episodes at scale; the flagged next step is a before/after read on 5–10 live guided-intake transcripts before trusting it.** Separate untouched findings from the same review: the persona deltas and the extraction brief are **dormant in the live rebuilt voice** (an autistic user gets the generic voice; extraction only gates saves, never reaches Jove's turn) — bigger "turn-it-back-on" calls, not part of this change. **Process note:** branch was 11 commits behind `origin/main` (none touched `system-prompt.ts`); rebased via stash onto current live — clean — and fast-forwarded the code commit onto main. `origin/main` advanced again mid-ship (a parallel session's two state.md doc commits, `ff4dc5c`/`7afa45b`); the code commit landed cleanly between them and this state.md entry sits on top.

- **Reflection-pull compose route now fails retryably (502), not generically (500), when the composer throws** (2026-06-26, commit `e7aff95`): The user-pulled Reflection compose path (`/api/checkpoint/compose`, behind the `reflection_meter` gate) called `composeManualEntry` **without a try/catch**. Inside the composer (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`) the `JSON.parse` of Opus's output is **unguarded**, so if Opus ever returns non-JSON the throw propagated all the way up and the route returned a generic **500** — the route's `if (!composed)` guard only caught the `null` return, never a thrown error, so the client never got the intended **retryable `502 compose_failed`** that keeps the meter full + strip so the user can re-tap. The web (auto-pushed) path already handled this correctly — `call-persona.ts` wraps its `composeManualEntry` call, sets `composedEntry = null`, and skips the checkpoint gracefully. **Fix (parity, not a new mechanism):** wrap the compose route's call in try/catch and treat a throw **exactly like the existing `null` return** — fall through to the already-present 502 guard. **Security:** logs the error **TYPE and conversation id only** (`err instanceof Error ? err.name : typeof err`), never the error message — a `JSON.parse` `SyntaxError` can embed a snippet of the model's (user-derived) output, and the Security Rules in CLAUDE.md forbid logging user content (stricter than the web path, which logs the full `err`). **Robustness/UX, not a correctness bug:** no data loss either way today — the entry simply isn't written; this just converts a dead-end 500 into a re-tappable 502. **Resolves the known follow-up** flagged in the `de462c6` (THE BAR admin-editable) entry below. Scope: one file, `compose/route.ts`, +28/−10. Flagged by senior-eng review as pre-existing and out of scope for the THE-BAR change. Build clean, **1223** vitest green (no tests added — the count is the post-rebase total incl. the 4 from `6013079`). *(Live in-browser verification not run — a server error-path robustness fix isn't browser-observable; verified by build + full suite.)* **Process note:** `origin/main` advanced to `6013079` (parallel double-start-race ship) mid-session; rebased onto it — clean replay, single non-overlapping file — and fast-forwarded.

- **Same-tick double-start race closed on the remaining start paths** (2026-06-26, commit `6013079`): The three conversation-start functions in `useChat` (`startConversation` / `startExploration` / `startNewSession`) guarded re-entry with `if (isLoading || isStreaming)` — React **state**, which isn't visible within the same render tick, so two calls fired in one tick (a **sub-frame double-tap** on a start control) both read a stale `false` and both proceeded, creating **two conversations / two `POST /api/chat`**. The guided-intake **door** path was already hardened with a synchronous `preStartRef` guard in `MainApp` (commit `f96ecf0`); this generalizes that protection to the start paths that aren't behind the intro modal. **Fix (one place, removal-first not applicable — React state can't be read fresh in-tick):** a synchronous in-flight latch (`createStartGuard`) shared by all three — acquire before the await, release in `finally`. `startNewSession` defers its release by a microtask (it has no network await to anchor the hold, and a synchronous release would clear the lock before a same-tick second call runs; its double-fire is benign anyway — no conversation created). The `isLoading || isStreaming` state guard **stays** — it still covers the post-render, cross-operation in-flight case (e.g. a start tapped mid message-stream) the latch doesn't. **Low-severity** — the window is sub-frame and usually covered by the in-flight UI; flagged by senior-eng review as the kind of race that's easy to wave off. The latch is **exported and unit-tested directly** — vitest runs node-env without jsdom (deliberate convention), so the hook can't be rendered; the test wires the real latch through a faithful acquire→await→release harness and asserts two synchronous starts issue **exactly one fetch** (+ release-isn't-a-deadlock + releases-on-throw). Scope: `useChat.ts` (+ `useChat.test.ts`, +4 tests). Build clean, **1223** vitest green, tsc clean (only pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` generated-file noise). *(Live in-browser verification not run — a sub-frame double-tap race isn't browser-observable; verified by the build + the four direct race tests.)* **Process note:** the worktree base was 6 commits behind `origin/main` (a parallel reflection-meter ship touched `useChat.ts` in different regions); rebased onto current live — clean 3-way replay, no conflicts — and fast-forwarded.

- **The composer's entry-voice standard (THE BAR) is now admin-editable** (2026-06-26, commit `de462c6`): The composer (`composeManualEntry`, Opus — it writes every Manual entry) had its depth/quality standard — "THE BAR," what makes an entry land — hardcoded inline. Extracted it to an exported `COMPOSER_ENTRY_BAR` constant and made it live-editable via the existing voice-overrides system (new key `composer_entry_bar`, surfaces in the **Voice editor** next to Character — the entry-writing equivalent of CHARACTER). **Reuse, no new system:** same `persona_voice_overrides` table + `OverrideFieldEditor` + `getVoiceOverrides`/`saveOverride`/`resetOverride`; the persona-voice route accepts it (a core voice key, not a door opener) and never logs the text. The resolved override threads into the composer as `entryBarOverride` through its live callers — web (`call-persona`, via a hoisted var mirroring `postConfirmLineOverride`) and the reflection-pull `compose` route (via `ctx.voiceOverrides`); the deprecated Linq path fails open to the default. The composer resolves `entryBar = override?.trim() ? override : COMPOSER_ENTRY_BAR`, so a missing/disabled/blank override or a DB error all fall back to the shipped text — **double-guarded fail-open** (`getVoiceOverrides` returns `{}` on error/blank/disabled; the resolver re-checks `.trim()`). With no override the composer system prompt is **byte-identical** to before (senior-eng verified char-for-char — 914-byte BAR exact, incl. the two-space `Recap:`/`Deeper:` indents). **Deliberately LOCKED — the override can't reach them:** the output JSON schema, the five section slugs + assignment, the length validators, and the no-clinical-names safety rule, all code-literal. Scope: `confirm-checkpoint.ts` (extract + interpolate), `voice-overrides.ts` (registry field), `call-persona.ts` + `compose/route.ts` (thread), `VoiceEditorPanel.tsx` (taller field). **Senior-eng change-review = ship.** Build clean, **1219** vitest (+4: default / override-substitution / blank-fallback / locked-structure-preserved). **No schema change** (reuses `persona_voice_overrides`). *(Live in-browser verification not run — worktree session, preview unavailable; the override path is identical to the sibling voice fields already live in prod.
