One real save+reset in the Voice editor is the recommended 60-second confirm.)* Rebased onto two parallel composer/voice "plain-spoken register" pushes (`81d95ca`, `fb737b8`) — the BAR's `Deeper:` exemplar conflict was resolved in their favor so the default tracks live, then fast-forwarded. **Known follow-up (pre-existing, flagged by review):** the reflection-pull compose route doesn't wrap `composeManualEntry` in try/catch like the web path does, so a composer JSON-parse throw would surface as a generic 500 instead of the intended retryable 502 — tracked separately as a task; no data loss either way.

- **Jove's checkpoint-DRAFTING register pulled plain (the upstream half of the entry-register fix)** (2026-06-26, commit `fb737b8`): The composer fix below (`81d95ca`) flagged its own follow-up: in the live auto-pushed path the composer *polishes Jove's drafted checkpoint message and faithfully preserves its register* (`call-persona.ts` → `composeManualEntry({checkpointText: conversationalText})`), so if Jove **drafts** the proposal in a literary/systems register, the final entry stays elevated no matter how good the composer prompt is. This change pulls the drafting voice plain. **Single live lever:** Jove's checkpoint-proposal prose is drafted entirely in `REBUILT_MECHANICS` (`voice-scaffold.ts`) — the Tier-3 `checkpoints`/`first-checkpoint` blocks that *also* draft proposals are **excluded** in the rebuilt (live) voice (`REBUILT_TIER3_EXCLUSIONS`), so MECHANICS is the one place to touch and there's no competing copy. **Fix (demonstrations beat instructions, zero new rule-text):** MECHANICS already owned a register cue for the draft — the vague adverb *"plainly."* I made it concrete by hanging a **flat contrast exemplar** off it at proposal altitude — *("when someone pushes back I go quiet and agree just to make it stop — my jaw locks, the words won't come, even when I don't agree," not "under pressure I default to agreement, the body foreclosing on speech")* — plain spoken-with-a-body-word vs. clinical/literary. **No second copy of the composer's "said, not written" frame** was added (HARD BLOCK on duplicated conversational rules): the composer keeps its frame for the final entry; MECHANICS keeps its own pre-existing one-word cue, now demonstrated. The exemplar's "right" side also models the very next clause (carry a body word in), so it does double duty. The em-dash was again **left unconstrained** (matches `81d95ca`). **Empirically tested** old-vs-new via a throwaway offline probe (`scripts/draft-register-probe.ts` — real Opus through the real rebuilt prompt assembly, no DB; 4 fixed seed conversations each ending at a proposal seam across work/relationships/sensory/ADHD domains; deleted after, per Complexity Gate). Baseline drafted intermittently elevated — *"one channel runs the interaction, the other watches how you're landing through their eyes," "You run a version of yourself in rooms with other people," "the bill comes due in the body."* After the exemplar those are **gone** across every seed and sample; drafts are first-person, spoken, and carry the user's own body words ("chest goes tight," "empties the tank," "where it dies"). **Tested three cue phrasings:** a longer cue ("said the way they'd say it out loud to a friend") worked but echoed the composer's "out loud to someone they trust" too closely; a terse anchor ("said, not written up") let mild systems vocab creep back; **"plainly" + the exemplar alone was both the plainest and the cleanest** — confirming (again) the composer-fix finding that the flat *exemplar* is load-bearing, not the frame. **Scope:** one file, `voice-scaffold.ts`, 1 changed line (the proposal sentence). Build clean, **1215** vitest green. *(Live in-browser verification not run — prompt text, not browser-observable; worktree session.)* **Process note:** `origin/main` advanced twice mid-session (composer `81d95ca`, then guided-intake `f96ecf0`); the one-line change was rebased onto current live and fast-forwarded so nothing was reverted.

- **Guided-intake opener pre-warms behind the intro modal — the first-screen "thinking" wait is gone** (2026-06-26, commit `f96ecf0`): Opening the guided-intake door for the first time showed the one-time "BEFORE YOU BEGIN" intro card (`DoorIntroModal`), and the conversation only started *on dismiss* — so Jove's model-generated tee-up streamed afterward, reading as a ~5s dead "thinking" wait on an otherwise-empty screen. **Why not just emit a fixed opener (the obvious fix):** the rebuilt guided-intake flow *intentionally* generates the tee-up live ("generate one short tee-up in your own voice — don't reproduce fixed wording"; `door-intros.ts` notes guided-intake has no `openerKey` because its opener is generated, unlike situation/upload which server-emit fixed strings) — so an initial "emit a fixed `GUIDED_INTAKE_OPENER` verbatim" approach was **abandoned** once the current code was loaded (that constant had already been deleted in the doors rework). **Fix (overlap, not replace):** `handleStartConversation` now kicks off `startConversation(mode)` the moment the intro modal opens, stashing the `Promise<boolean>` in a ref; the opener generates *behind* the modal during reading time, and `handleDoorIntroDismiss` just reveals the (already-streaming or ready) session instead of starting then. Applies to all three doors' first-time flow; the guided tee-up is the big win (situation/upload already server-emit instantly). **Safe by construction:** the modal has no back-out path (every dismiss — Got it / Escape / backdrop — already committed to starting), so pre-starting can't orphan a conversation; a synchronous ref guard at the top of `handleStartConversation` closes the same-tick double-tap window that `useChat`'s React-state in-flight guard can't catch; and dismiss falls back to a real start *only* if the pre-start was skipped by that guard (resolves false) — a successful pre-start resolves true, so it never double-starts. The always-mounted (display-toggled) session panel means the streamed opener renders behind the modal and is ready on reveal. **Scope:** one file, `MainApp.tsx`, +33/−1. Senior-eng change-review = **ship** (traced double-start / dead-end / orphan / promise-rejection / view-state — all safe; its one finding, a sub-frame door double-tap, is closed by the added ref guard). Build clean, **1215** vitest green. *(Live in-browser verification not run — worktree session, preview unavailable per CLAUDE.md; verified by build + full suite + adversarial review. The felt result is confirmable on the next first-time guided-intake load.)* **Process note:** the worktree base was stale and an in-progress edit was wiped by a worktree reset mid-session; the change was re-applied on current `origin/main`, and a parallel composer-fix push (`81d95ca`) landed during the work — rebased onto it and fast-forwarded so nothing was reverted.

- **Composer entry register pulled toward plain spoken language** (2026-06-26): Manual entries read literary/highfalutin — "the doing"/"the watching" nominalizations, em-dash dramatic pauses, therapist-and-self-help cadence — instead of how a person actually talks about themselves. **Root cause (not a missing rule):** the composer's single load-bearing exemplar (the "Deeper" example every other instruction points at) was itself written in an elevated, systems-y register ("wins the resources," "fires the monitoring"), and demonstrations beat instructions. **Fix (removal-first, net *shorter*):** rewrote the "Deeper" exemplar flat and spoken; replaced the enumerated register bans with one higher-order frame — *"the entry is the user saying this out loud about themselves, not a writer composing a passage about them, and not a therapist or self-help book describing them; if it sounds written instead of said, rewrite it the way they'd say it"*; cut a disliked title fail-example; trimmed two redundant "go deeper than they could've said" restatements (the idea was stated 3×). The em-dash was deliberately **left unconstrained** — a per-tic ban is over-constraining and the founder doesn't mind it. **Empirically tested** old vs new prompt on plain seeds via a throwaway offline composer harness (real Opus, no DB; deleted after, per Complexity Gate): the new prompt reliably drops the nominalizations and clinical vocabulary; a pure higher-order frame *alone* could not, which is why the flat exemplar is load-bearing. **Key finding (next work):** in the live auto-pushed path the composer *polishes Jove's drafted checkpoint message* (`call-persona.ts` → `composeManualEntry({checkpointText})`) and faithfully preserves its register — so the remaining register leverage is upstream, in **Jove's checkpoint drafting** (the voice prompt), flagged as separate next work. **Scope:** one file, `confirm-checkpoint.ts`, 4 changed lines; mocked compose test green. *(Live in-browser verification not run — prompt text, not browser-observable; worktree session.)* **Process note:** the worktree was 7 commits behind `origin/main`; the single-file change was forwarded onto current live and shipped via fast-forward so the intervening shipped work (app-copy removal, admin/onboarding changes, kill-parking migration) was not reverted.

- **Onboarding + Home copy is now admin-editable** (2026-06-26, commit `522ec05`): The new-user-facing copy — the three "ways to begin" entry doors (title / description / button), the Home welcome tile, the Manual-index heading + subheadings, and the post-login Seed/consent screen — became live admin overrides, edited from a new **"Onboarding & Home copy"** card in the Tuning → TUNE group. **Reuse-first, no parallel system:** the same `persona_voice_overrides` table + `OverrideFieldEditor` + `readOverrideRows`/`saveOverride`/`resetOverride` helpers the Intake-doors / Voice panels already use. The strings were lifted out of the components into a new **pure** registry `src/lib/persona/app-copy.ts` (`APP_COPY_FIELDS` → label/group/default; `resolveAppCopy` → the nested `AppCopy`; `APP_COPY_DEFAULTS`). Resolution folds into the **existing `/api/onboarding-status`** response (no new round-trip); `MainApp` holds the resolved copy in state and threads it to the Home views + onboarding, every consuming component defaulting to `APP_COPY_DEFAULTS`. **Fails open to the shipped defaults** on any missing/disabled/blank row or DB error — an empty overrides table renders byte-identical to before. New `/api/admin/app-copy` (GET grouped fields, PATCH save/reset; requireAdmin + service-role + key-validated against the registry, never logs text). The registry is client-safe (a type-only `OverrideRow` import + the `PERSONA_NAME` constant), so no server code reaches the client bundle, and app-copy keys can't leak into Jove's prompt (`getVoiceOverrides` skips any key not in its own registry). Senior-eng change-review = **ship**; its two flagged items fixed in the same commit (four section `aria-label`s now track their editable headings; the welcome default's stale "five **layers**" → "five **sections**" per the live noun). **No schema change** (reuses `persona_voice_overrides`). Build clean, **1215** vitest green (+7 resolver tests incl. a fail-open contract + an every-key-wired guard; `onboarding-copy.test.ts` repointed to the registry). *(Live in-browser verification not run — worktree session, preview unavailable; the override path is identical to two sibling panels already live in prod. One real save+reset in the admin panel is the recommended 60-second confirm.)* Rebased onto a parallel kill-parking + doc-audit push and fast-forwarded. **Follow-up (same day):** the panel groups the ~20 fields into four **collapsible sections** (collapsed by default — it's a large panel), each header naming **where** the copy appears and **when/who** sees it, plus an "N edited" badge so overridden sections are visible at a glance.

- **Doc-audit freshness pass — ~31 drifted claims fixed across docs + admin "how it works" pages** (2026-06-26, commit `d8d8d25`): Ran `/doc-audit` (read-only multi-agent freshness audit, 13 surfaces, ~450 claims verified against code). **Highest-impact drift: the Sonnet→Opus model-tier labels** — Jove's conversational turn and the entry composer both run on `claude-opus-4-7`, not Sonnet (only extraction is Sonnet; summaries are Haiku) — stale across `CLAUDE.md`, `system.md`, admin how-it-works, the vendor registry, and prompt-architecture. Also: rewrote `system.md`'s stale **Onboarding Flow + Storage Keys** (the seed-text `sessionStorage` handoff is gone; live keys are `mw_*`; completion lives in `profiles.onboarding_completed_at`), fixed the inbound-text webhook routes, and dropped the nonexistent `next_prompt`/`nextPrompt` field; **admin/schema-map** gained the **5 missing config tables** (`feature_gates`, `persona_voice_overrides` +history, `checkpoint_tuning` +history) under a new "config" family and filled omitted columns (table count 15→20, now auto-derived); **admin/extraction-map** reframed `distinct_contexts` from a phantom hard gate to a strengthening signal and collapsed the retired first/subsequent two-bar gate to **one bar for every checkpoint**; plus `rules.md` color tokens, the prompt-architecture token figure (~7,000→~3,000 under the live rebuilt voice), the `wrap.md` decision-log note, and the docs ADR count. The companion current-state fix in this entry: the Deployed-Features "Checkpoint pipeline" line said "Haiku classifier → Sonnet composition" — corrected to "deterministic regex detector → Opus composition." Each fix independently verified against code — notably one audit agent's token-count claim was **wrong** (it measured the legacy variant) and was caught by direct measurement of the live rebuilt prompt; also confirmed the live path passes `voiceVariant: LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT`, so prod genuinely runs rebuilt (no code bug). Build clean, **1208** vitest green, tsc clean. *(Read-only audit + doc/admin-display edits — no runtime logic changed. Admin explainer pages are data-driven and typecheck-validated; not separately browser-verified.)* *(Caveat: the audit fanned out on a worktree base ~100 commits behind `origin/main`, so a few "ADR-051 doesn't exist / held group is live" findings were stale-base artifacts — ADR-051 and the parking-kill are in fact live on main; those were dropped, not applied.)*

- **Killed parking — the unexpected "6th category" is gone; every entry homes on one of the five** (2026-06-25, commit `fe097a4`, ADR-051): The structure migration (ADR-050) parked self-to-self patterns at `section = NULL` and gathered them into a **held group** — the proto-face of a deferred "inner-world" sixth section. But the held group was given full *section* chrome and pushed into the same array every surface treats as "the five," and only **1 of 5 renderers** checked the `isHeld` flag — so it leaked as a visible **sixth category** ("How you relate to yourself", a copy stub) into the Home "Your manual" index, the **"N of 5 started"** counter (could read "6 of 5"), the Manual page tile, and the PDF export. Jeff flagged it ("why six?"). Found the cause (commit `3275fdd`, the Step-3 read-path cutover) and, per Jeff's call, **killed parking entirely** rather than just quieting the held group. **(1)** The composer (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`) no longer has a "park as null" option — it always picks one of the five; an off-spec/missing pick defaults to `relationships` (logged) instead of orphaning a confirmed entry. **(2)** The held-group concept is deleted **at the source** — `buildLayers` now returns exactly five sections, so the Home index/counter/pips, mobile Manual, admin manual view, and PDF are all fixed by one change; removed `HELD_SECTION`/`HELD_GROUP_LABEL`/`HELD_GROUP_ABOUT`/`sectionForEntry`/`isHeld` and the dead consumer branches (**net −45 LOC**). **(3)** The **4 existing parked rows refiled** via migration `20260625000001` so they don't orphan when the held UI goes away: *the caregiver trap* + *the permission loop* → **Relationships** (romantic — both are partner-dynamic patterns that fail the survives-solitude test, arguably mis-parked); *The Room Inside You* → **Work and career** (inner critic about capability); *Exposure Freeze with a Running Verdict* → **Sensory and burnout** (a body freeze — the "body wins" spine rule). Destinations read from each entry's actual content, confirmed by Jeff. `layer` left frozen; the `section` column stays nullable as legacy provenance (NOT NULL hardening deferred). Rewrote `section.test.ts` to assert the invariant directly (exactly five sections, **never** a sixth group, a stray null entry is dropped not resurrected). **Verified:** build clean, full **1208** vitest suite green, tsc clean (only pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` generated-file noise). *(Live in-browser verification not run: the four refiled rows belong to three prod users and the migration only applies on merge, so the test account can't display them yet, and dev-populate never produced a 6th category to begin with — the unit tests exercise the exact invariant more directly. The refile becomes verifiable on prod once the migration runs at merge.)* **Reverses** the held-group / deferred-sixth-section sub-decision in the 2026-06-24 restructure entry below.

- **Admin Tuning tab reorganized into TUNE vs DEBUG groups** (2026-06-25, commit `287c0c7`): The Tuning tab was a flat stack of four panels with no hierarchy or orientation — "just a list," unclear what each does. Reorganized into two labeled groups under a page intro: **TUNE — Jove's behavior** (the everyday soak dials: Voice editor, Checkpoint dials, Intake doors) leads; **DEBUG — System gates** (the Feature-gate kill-switches) sits last. Each group carries a one-line "what this is / when you'd touch it" description; the now-redundant Feature-gates intro paragraph was trimmed (the group header carries the default-ON / debug-scaffolding framing). Pure presentational change in `admin/page.tsx` — a small reusable `TuningGroup` header + a `TuningSection` wrapper, mirroring the existing local `UsersSection` pattern — with **no behavior, API, or schema changes**; the four panels and their endpoints are untouched. Build clean, **1208** vitest tests green (pre-commit hook). *(Live in-browser verification not run — session started from a worktree where the preview/dev-server is unreliable per CLAUDE.md; a faithful linen/walnut mockup was shown to the founder instead.)* **Process note:** the edits first landed in the shared main checkout by a worktree path-translation slip; they were re-applied cleanly in the worktree and shipped via fast-forward push, leaving the main checkout's other in-progress work untouched.

- **Jove no longer assumes gender for people the user mentions** (2026-06-25, commit `ce7e902`): Jove was gendering unnamed third parties (a partner, a coworker) the user brought up. Root cause was **not** a missing rule — it was the prompt's own example content. Nine example lines across `voice-scaffold.ts` (incl. one in the live `REBUILT_MECHANICS` block), `voice-autistic.ts`, and `voice-adhd.ts` demonstrated picking she/he for an *unnamed* person, and demonstrations beat instructions — so they quietly undercut the Tier 1 "mirror exact language" rule. **Fix (removal-first, zero net prompt length, no new rule):** replaced each assumed pronoun with the role noun ("your partner") or singular "they"; named people (Sarah, Sam, Tom) left as-is since the user supplied the name. The one remaining gendered pronoun in the voice files ("his sign-off") is a code comment about Jeff, not prompt text. Three tests that pinned the old strings were updated. The existing user-gender rule (`system-prompt.ts:400`, first-message block only) was left untouched — if soak shows gendering still slips through, the flagged next step is to *broaden that one rule* to cover anyone the user mentions rather than add a second. Build clean; **1208** vitest tests green. *(Live in-browser verification not run — this is prompt text, not browser-observable; session also started from a worktree.)*

- **Per-door intros + per-door opener editing + chip-typing & refresh fixes** (2026-06-25): A fit-and-finish pass bundling five founder-requested items. **(1) Per-door intros.** The single global "How this works" modal (`ChatWindowModal`, gated on `modal_progress < 1`) is retired; each intake door now shows its OWN one-time "how this works" card the first time that door is opened (situation / guided-intake / upload), tracked per-user in a new `profiles.door_intros_seen text[]` column. The gate moved to the single door-tap choke point `handleStartConversation` in `MainApp`; the modal (`DoorIntroModal`, now presentational) renders from admin-editable copy. First door-intro dismissal advances `modal_progress` to ≥1 so the later pattern-forming modal chain still fires. **(2) Admin "Intake doors" panel** (Tuning tab, first): one card per door with editable **intro title + body** (all three) and **opening message** (situation + upload — guided-intake's opener is a generated tee-up, shown as a note, not editable). Stored as rows in the existing `persona_voice_overrides` table (no new table) via a new `/api/door-intros` (client copy + seen) and `/api/admin/intake-doors` (admin edit) route; the two door openers were pulled out of the generic Voice editor so each key has one edit surface. `upload_opener` is newly admin-editable (resolved in `call-persona`'s server-emit). Shared `OverrideFieldEditor` + `readOverrideRows`/`saveOverride`/`resetOverride` helpers de-dupe the field markup + upsert/audit logic across the Voice and Intake-doors panels. **(3) Chip typing.** When option chips or the section picker are on screen, the composer placeholder invites *"Or type something else…"* and (desktop only) focus returns to the input the moment it re-enables — fixing the bug where typing silently did nothing after selecting an option until a click/refresh. **(4) Refresh stays put.** A refresh mid-session no longer bounces to Home: `MainApp` persists/restores the active view (`mw_active_view`) and `useChat` persists/restores the exact conversation (`mw_active_conversation`, preferred over most-recent). **(5) Chip lag (debug).** Findings only: the perceived lag is model-generation under a *buffered* reveal (typing indicator until done), which is load-bearing — the raw stream carries `---chips---`/`---sections---`/checkpoint markers stripped server-side before display. Post-stream work is light (sync strips + one DB insert). The only real shortening is live token-streaming with client-side marker stripping (a separate, flash-risky effort) — recommended as its own opt-in, not bundled here. **Verified:** `next build` clean, full **vitest** suite green (**1205** tests incl. new `door-intros` + `DoorIntroModal` coverage), tsc clean (only pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` generated-file noise). *(Live in-browser verification was NOT run — this session started from a worktree, where the preview/dev-server is unreliable per CLAUDE.md; flagged honestly. The door-intro end-to-end needs the `door_intros_seen` migration, which auto-applies on merge.)*

- **Suppressed-checkpoint dead turn fixed (the "Okay." bug)** (2026-06-25, commit `21a0ea9`): A real guided-intake transcript showed Jove reply with a bare **"Okay."**, the user respond **"?"**, and Jove recover with "Sorry — I went quiet on you." Root cause: when the material-quality gate **suppresses** a checkpoint, `stripCheckpointFromText` removes the transition line + entry prose and keeps whatever preceded it — but it kept **any** non-empty preamble, so a filler ack ("Okay.") in front of the stripped proposal became the whole message, with **no handoff** (violates Tier 1 R4). Fix is pure code (no prompt change): a shared `leadInHandsOff` helper — a lead-in stands alone only if it asks a question or is a substantive directive/reflection (≥4 words); a bare ack falls back to the existing neutral grounding line ("Tell me what's going on for you right now."). Applied to **both** the suppression strip and `splitCheckpointLeadIn` (the pass-path early lead-in), since a bare ack shipped early then a failed composition strands the identical dead turn — one helper closes both. Regression tests added for each path. Build clean; 1202 tests green; tsc clean. *(Sibling note: an earlier "propose sooner" depth change in the same session was **backed out** — real transcripts showed guided intake builds entries if anything too fast/thin, not too slowly, so it was aimed the wrong way.)*
- **Dev populate tool: real composer output (titled) via frozen fixtures, not hand-written content** (2026-06-25, branch claude/hardcore-knuth-c26b78): The admin "populate manual" tool kept its own hand-authored `SECTION_CONTENT` narratives and inserted them with `name: null` — a parallel copy of composition output that drifted from how real entries are actually written, and titleless. Reviewed with the senior-engineer agent. **Fix:** the five samples are now the output of the real `composeManualEntry` (the same Opus path the live checkpoint uses), captured once offline through a throwaway script and frozen in `src/app/api/dev-populate/fixtures.ts` — each carries a composed title, body, section, tags, summary, and key_words. The route just inserts them: **zero model calls per click, instant, deterministic** (no live composition — that's what the Simulate tool is for). Single source of truth for entry composition; the fixtures are a captured cache, not parallel logic, with a regenerate recipe in the file header. **Also:** the re-populate cleanup switched from `name IS NULL` to `source_message_id IS NULL` (real + Simulate entries always carry a source_message_id from the `confirm_checkpoint_write` RPC; direct-inserted seeds never do — so it still wipes only prior seeds, never real test data — and `name IS NULL` would have stopped matching once seeds gained titles). Explicit insert column list so a schema change fails loudly. Build clean; route stays on edge (no model calls).

- **Manual read-path was dropping `section` → every entry fell into the held "junk drawer" group** (2026-06-25, branch claude/hardcore-knuth-c26b78): After the layers→sections cutover, `buildLayers` groups entries by `sectionForEntry(e) = e.section ?? HELD_SECTION`, but the three Manual read/return routes still SELECTed only `id, layer, name, content, …` — never `section`. So every entry reached the client `section`-undefined, fell back to `HELD_SECTION`, and rendered in the held "patterns about your relationship with yourself" group regardless of its real section. Affected all users' Manual display (and the admin populate tool, which is how it surfaced — populated entries land with `layer:null` + a correct `section`, so they were the most visible casualty). **Fix:** added `section` to the SELECT in `src/app/api/manual/route.ts` (user Manual), `src/app/api/admin/manual/route.ts` (admin view), and `src/app/api/manual/[id]/route.ts` (PATCH return — without it, editing an entry bounced it to held). `tags` left out — no UI reads it. Regression test in `route.test.ts` now asserts the SELECT includes `section`. Build clean; manual route + section + manual-context suites pass.

- **Checkpoint cleanup: inline preview tee-up + restored popup + single save screen** (2026-06-25, commit `f9c7f2b`): Three founder-requested fixes to the checkpoint experience, each confirmed against interactive HTML previews before building. **(1) Tee-up.** The in-chat checkpoint is no longer the glowing letterpress trigger (centered display headline + brass rule + heavy amber halo, headline-only). It's a calm inline `Plate` preview that sits in the conversation flow — section eyebrow + headline + a one-sentence teaser of the entry + "Tap to review →" — reusing the same `Plate` component as historical checkpoints (no fork). Tapping still opens the review popup. **(2) Popup.** Restored the pre-2026-06-12 presentation: left-aligned **22px Newsreader** headline (was centered 30px Fraunces display), dropped the letterpress inset frame and the brass-rule "press" animation, and widened the plate **400 → 480px** for a more comfortable reading measure. **(3) Save.** Collapsed the two save indicators into one — removed the inline "Adding to your Manual…" line below the entry; the full-screen cover is now the single save screen, shown from confirm (Adding…) through success (Added). Net deletion overall (removed the press/brass-rule state + logic and the dead `.checkpoint-trigger-frame` / `.checkpoint-trigger-plate` CSS); the teaser is one small `checkpointTeaser` helper. **Verified:** build clean, full **vitest** suite green (incl. the 6 `CheckpointOverlay` contract tests pinning auto-close / Escape / scroll-lock), tsc clean (only pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` generated-file noise); the visual was confirmed against the founder-approved interactive previews. *(A live in-browser checkpoint screenshot was not run — triggering a real checkpoint needs a full conversation flow; flagged honestly.)* Rebased on `origin/main`, clean replay. Supersedes the "letterpress Plate … confirming plays a press" checkpoint description in the Theming line above.

- **Section 2 renamed: "Work and money" → "Work and career"** (2026-06-25, commit `1fa8983`): Founder copy request. Reframed the second Manual section away from money/earning toward career, in the single source of truth `src/lib/manual/layers.ts` (every surface imports from it): title (**Work and career**), tagline ("How you hold up where you earn." → "How you hold up **at work — and what fits**."), description (dropped "where you earn"; added "the kind of work that fits how you're built"), the `earning and stakes` dimension → `fit and direction`, the example (financial pressure → a tight deadline), and the empty-state status. The genuinely ND-relevant dimensions stayed (masking at work, operating under pressure, what you can absorb, the line you won't cross). The **`work-money` slug is unchanged** — it's the frozen DB key, so existing entries keep their home and **no migration** is needed. Also updated the two surfaces that hardcode this copy instead of importing it: the public landing-page section index (`src/app/page.tsx`) and the checkpoint-composition section gloss (`src/lib/persona/confirm-checkpoint.ts`). Copy-only — no schema/API/decision-logic. **Verified in-browser as devtest:** the Manual renders **WORK AND CAREER** with the new empty-state copy ("Nothing about how you operate at work yet."); no "Work and money / where you earn" left anywhere in `src/`. Build clean, **1199 tests** pass; rebased on `origin/main`, clean fast-forward push. Supersedes the "Work and money" section name in the 2026-06-24 restructure entry below.

- **Settings: Crisis support to the bottom + inline Dev Tools removed** (2026-06-25, commit `59aee10`): Two small declutters to the mobile Settings page from one founder request. **(1)** The **SUPPORT / Crisis support** row moved from mid-list to the **bottom** — section order is now Account → How Jove talks to you → Appearance → Text Jove → Support — so the page reads top-down from account basics to the safety backstop. It stays an always-visible row (not collapsed). **(2)** Removed the admin-only inline **Dev Tools** accordion (Simulate user / Populate manual) from `MobileSettings` entirely, along with its now-dead state, handlers, and the `onSimulationEvent` / `onPopulateComplete` props threaded through `MainApp`. This was a **duplicate** of the desktop sidebar's `DevToolsPanel` (still live under **Admin tools**, dispatching the same `dev-tools:*` window events) — so dev simulate/populate are unchanged there; only the redundant mobile copy is gone. The in-app **"Open admin dashboard →"** link that lived inside that section is gone with it on mobile (admins still reach `/admin` via the desktop sidebar or the URL — flagged to Jeff, left out to match "remove dev tools"). No new component, no decision logic; net deletion. **Verified in-browser as devtest (admin):** Settings shows the five sections with Crisis support last and **no Dev Tools** even for an admin; console clean. Build clean, **1199 tests** pass, tsc clean (only pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` generated-file noise); branched off `origin/main`, clean fast-forward push. Supersedes the "five sections … Dev tools" description in the 2026-06-24 collapsible-sections entry below.

- **Home doors lead with Guided** (2026-06-24, commit `d35576f`): Reordered the shared `WaysToBegin` DOORS so **Guided** is the first entry door, ahead of "Bring a situation" (then Upload). Presentational only — mobile + desktop inherit the order; door availability + mode resolution unchanged.

- **Guided-intake prompt: drop the last stale old-section example** (2026-06-24, commit `6ee845a`): A small follow-up to the restructure below. The guided-intake `OPEN THE SECTION` block still illustrated the drill-down with "(e.g. inside how they process things: …)" — an old pattern-type layer name that no longer exists post-cutover (an unlisted S1 hardcoded literal the Step-3 pass missed). Neutralized it to a taxonomy-agnostic example ("concrete focuses like …") so it tracks any future taxonomy. The `SectionPicker` reads `LAYERS` directly, so it already auto-follows the new sections — no other guided-intake change was needed. tier3 snapshot regenerated; 1199 tests, build clean; fast-forward push.
- **Manual restructured: pattern-type layers → life-area SECTIONS + tags (Steps 1–3 live)** (2026-06-24, commit `179f300`): The Manual's organizing structure changed. The five **pattern-type layers** (My Strengths / Some of My Patterns / How I Process Things / What Helps / How I Show Up with People) are replaced by five **life-area sections** — **Relationships · Work and money · Routines and structure · Sensory and burnout · Interests and flow** — plus a **closed tag set** (`strength` on any section; `romantic`/`family`/`friends` only inside Relationships, DB-CHECK-enforced incl. the COALESCE-hardened null-section guard). Each entry has exactly one section (its home); tags are an optional lens, never primary nav. Shipped as three gated migrations against live beta data (21 entries / 12 users): **Step 1** (`20260624000002`) added `section`/`tags` columns + hardened CHECKs; **Step 2** (`20260624000003`, applied to prod by hand then recorded in-repo here) backfilled the 21 via a propose→human-review-every-row→apply pass — **17 homed, 4 held**; **Step 3** (`20260624000004`) is the code cutover. The old integer `layer` column is **FROZEN provenance** (now nullable; existing rows untouched — never the structural key again; code keeps the `layer`/`LAYERS` identifier as a documented divergence from the product noun "section"). **Self-to-self patterns are PARKED:** the backfill surfaced a recurring inner-pattern territory (inner critic, self-verdict, self-permission) that doesn't fit a life area; those entries get `section = NULL` and render in a **held group** ("How you relate to yourself" — a **COPY STUB**, Jeff's voice pass is a fast-follow). A **sixth `inner-world` section is DEFERRED** pending beta evidence; its boundary (the **survives-solitude test** — a pattern homes to Inner world only if it runs with nobody else in the room) is on file (plan §2 Rule C) but not built. Composition now emits `section` (or null to park, high-bar/bias-to-home) + `tags`; the `checkpoint_meta` carrier, RPC (`p_section`/`p_tags`), SSE payload, and analytics all switched off `layer`. No-write-back guard (`sectionForEntry` is pure; parked rows stay NULL) is unit-tested. Plan + disambiguation rules: `docs/reference/structure-migration-plan.md`. **Verified:** build + **1199 tests** green; live as devtest (Home + Manual render the five sections with new emblems/empty-copy + the held group with its 4 parked entries; no console errors); **post-ship prod read-back confirmed data unchanged** (13 relationships · 1 each work/sensory/routines/interests · 4 parked · 1 strength tag). **Open follow-ups:** the held-group label copy (stub), tag-filter UI (not built — additive lens deferred), and a live new-checkpoint→section smoke test. The legacy plan's "five layers" language across this doc is now superseded by this entry.
- **Settings collapsible sections + crisis off the desktop sidebar** (2026-06-24, commit `4781ef4`): Two declutters from one request. **(1) Desktop sidebar:** removed the **Crisis support** footer link — crisis already lives in **Settings → SUPPORT** as an always-visible row that opens the dedicated `MobileCrisis` page, and the in-conversation crisis protocol is the real-time safety net. The sidebar footer is now just **Settings + Log out** (Theme was already cut the same day in `198f97f`). Retired the `onNavigateToCrisis` plumbing through `DesktopShell` → `MainApp` and the now-dead `IC_HEART` icon. **(2) Settings page:** the five sections (Account / How Jove talks to you / Appearance / Text Jove / Dev tools) are now **tap-to-expand accordions, collapsed by default**, via a MobileSettings-local `CollapsibleSection` (rotating chevron, `aria-expanded`, children stay mounted and hide via `display:none` so phone-link / dev-tools form state survives a collapse). The **SUPPORT / Crisis support** row stays **always-visible** — a safety resource shouldn't hide behind a tap. No new shared component, no decision logic. Mobile bottom nav untouched (crisis was already a Settings row there). **Verified in-browser as devtest:** desktop sidebar down to Settings + Log out; Settings sections collapsed-by-default with the crisis row visible; expanding a section reveals its rows. Build clean, 1193 tests pass, tsc clean; branched off `origin/main`, clean fast-forward push.
- **Desktop sidebar nav cleanup — cut the redundant "Conversation" row + Theme cycle** (2026-06-24, commit `198f97f`): Output of a duplicate-pathway navigation audit (mobile-behavior + frontend-design skills; founder flagged the "Conversation" drawer button as not fitting). **Desktop sidebar only; mobile bottom nav untouched.** Removed the generic **"Conversation"** nav row (expanded + collapsed rail) — it carried no information scent and duplicated *both* the highlighted active-session row directly below it *and* Home's "Continue this thread" resume hero. The active session's row in the history list is now the sole "back to the conversation" anchor, **strengthened** in `globals.css` (a walnut left spine via inset box-shadow + a stronger fill, so it clearly outweighs a hover — previously the active row and a hovered row were visually identical). Removed the sidebar's quick **Theme** cycle (Theme lives in Settings → Appearance only — same shared `useTheme` store, so nothing is lost). Gave **"New session"** a border so it reads as an *action*, not a peer destination alongside Home / Manual; its land-on-Home + reset behavior is unchanged. Retired the now-unused `onNavigateToSession` plumbing through `DesktopShell` → `MainApp` (MobileCrisis keeps its own back prop). Net **−2 sidebar rows**, clearer hierarchy (destinations / action / history / footer). Key audit finding: the same concept is correctly **kept** as the mobile "Talk" tab — mobile has no persistent session list, so the bottom nav is the only one-tap route back to the live thread; on desktop the always-visible list makes it redundant. Build clean, 1193 tests pass; fast-forward push. **Not browser-verified** (the desktop sidebar needs ≥1030px + login, and the dev server must run from the main repo root, not this worktree) — covered by the green build + full suite; visual confirm deferred to the main checkout.
- **Fix: Home doors now reflect live gate flips (drop cache-fragile server read)** (2026-06-24, commit `8b38e79`): Bug — after an admin flipped a conversation-mode gate, the Home entry doors kept showing the **stale** state: turning a mode back ON never re-enabled its door, and turning OFF only "worked" when the page happened to reload fresh. Root cause: `enabledModes` was read in the `/app` **server component** at render time and passed as a prop, and that value gets frozen by the browser / Router cache, so it never tracked later flips. (Confirmed by inspection: the server computed the correct values, but the client held a stale all-ON copy from cache.) Fix: read door availability via a **client fetch on every Home mount** instead — `/api/onboarding-status` (already called on mount; **no new endpoint, no extra request**) now also returns `enabledModes` (no-store, always fresh), `MainApp` owns it as state, and the `/app` server read is removed. This **supersedes the `/app` server read the situation-gate ship added** (`0fd18b7`), extending the client-fetch path to all three modes (situation / guided / upload). The admin toggle itself was never broken — it always persisted; only Home's reflection of it was stale. Verified live as devtest: flipping each mode off → its door shows "Coming soon"; flipping back on → door re-enables; both directions track. Rebased onto `origin/main` (the situation-gate ship landed mid-flight); the `/app` conflict resolved to the client-fetch version. tsc + build clean, 1193 tests pass.
- **Admin Users tab — sort by last active / sign-up date (filter chips removed)** (2026-06-24, commit `63b8b09`): Replaced the Users-tab filter chips (All / Active / Dormant / Guests — added earlier the same day in `d0acf44`) with a **sort dropdown**: Last active (default) and Sign-up date, both newest-first. The chips weren't earning their place — "Guests" (anonymous accounts) is always 0 in the closed beta, and "Active / Dormant" (has-conversations vs none) wasn't a useful cut. Net simpler (four chips + their count logic → one dropdown); the search box is retained and pagination resets on sort/search change. Verified live as devtest (both sort orders correct). tsc + 1193 tests + build clean.
- **Situation is now a per-mode gate — guided-/upload-solo configs** (2026-06-24, commit `0fd18b7`): Situation had been the always-on floor with no switch; added a `situation` feature gate so all three conversation modes are toggleable. Turn it off → the Home "Bring a situation" door renders "Coming soon" and new / fallback conversations resolve to the next enabled mode (so guided-on + situation/upload-off = a guided-solo experience). Reworked the single mode-resolution chokepoint into a pure, unit-tested `resolveConversationMode(rawMode, gates)`: the requested mode if its gate is on → else the first enabled mode in order situation → guided → upload → else **situation as the ultimate hard floor**, so a conversation is never left mode-less even with every gate off. `enabledModes.situation` is gate-backed in the `/app` server component; admin Tuning gains a "Situation mode" switch. **Migration `20260624000001`** seeds the gate ON (idempotent, run by hand per ADR-036) — though the toggle works without it too (flipping the switch upserts the row). +6 resolver tests (guided-solo, upload-solo, legacy "disabled optional mode → situation", all-off hard floor). Build clean, 1193 tests; rebased onto `origin/main` (a parallel structure-migration docs commit landed) and fast-forward-pushed.
- **Guided intake — unified, obviously-clickable SelectionTile (section + focus picks)** (2026-06-24, commit `30b11b1`): The section picker and the focus picks now render ONE shared `SelectionTile`, so the first- and second-order selection moments are the same control (they had been divergent — navy cards vs. serif-italic underlined lines). Design is the founder-approved "Option D, spec-matched": a navy-outlined cream tile, 10px radius via `var(--session-bubble-radius)` so it tracks the Jove bubble in both themes, a `--font-serif` title (so the dyslexic sans accommodation applies), a trailing chevron, and rest / hover / focus-visible / navy-press states. **Single action** — a tap selects AND advances, so there is no selected/checkmark state; `:active` is only the "go" feedback. The tile lives as a `.mw-seltile` class in `globals.css` (a deliberate departure from ADR-009's inline-styles default — the same exception the desktop `mw-dsk-*` controls take — because `:hover`/`:active`/`:focus-visible` can't be expressed inline). `SectionPicker` (title + tagline) and `QuickReplyChips` (title only) both render it; their public interfaces are unchanged, so `MobileSession` is untouched. Design explored via the frontend-design skill + 6 throwaway mockups (uncommitted, under `mockups/`). Build clean, 1187 tests pass; rebased onto `origin/main` (a parallel per-mode-gates ship landed mid-flight) and fast-forward-pushed.
- **Per-mode conversation gates + “Coming soon” entry doors** (2026-06-24, commit `cc6b55f`): Split the single `conversation_modes` feature gate into two independent per-mode switches — `guided_intake` and `upload` — and wired the Home entry doors to render **disabled with a “Coming soon” tag** when their mode is gated off. Situation is the always-on floor and has **no switch** (with both new gates OFF, every conversation runs Situation — the old single-gate behavior). **Reuse-first, no parallel machinery:** same `feature_gates` table, same admin toggle component (one row became two: “Guided intake mode” / “Upload mode”), same single server chokepoint in `persona-pipeline.ts` (now falls each disabled optional mode back to Situation — verified the only place mode is resolved, no decision logic duplicated). The client learns which doors are live via the **`/app` server component** reading the gates and passing an `enabledModes` prop down (MainApp → Home → `WaysToBegin`) — **no new endpoint, no new client fetch**, one cheap indexed read per render, fails open to all-ON. A disabled door is dimmed (0.55), non-clickable (native `disabled`), `aria-label="… — coming soon"`, with a “Coming soon” pill replacing the start cue. **Migration `20260624000000`** seeds both new gates ON and drops the orphaned `conversation_modes` row (append-only, idempotent, run by hand) — **already satisfied against the live DB** (rows present + ON, no `conversation_modes` row), so it's a no-op there and only matters for fresh environments. **Founder chose both gates default ON (status quo).** Build clean, 1186 tests pass, tsc clean. **Verified live as devtest:** admin Tuning shows the two switches (both ON, old label gone); a locally-forced off state (reverted edit, **no DB writes**) rendered Situation enabled with “START →” and Guided + Upload dimmed with “COMING SOON,” then reverted to all-on. Clean fast-forward push.
- **Admin Users tab — search + filter** (2026-06-24, commit `d0acf44`): The admin Users list (`UsersTab`) gained a **search box** (matches email *or* display name, case-insensitive) and four single-select **filter chips with live counts** — **All / Active / Dormant / Guests** (Active = ≥1 conversation, Dormant = zero conversations, Guests = anonymous accounts). Built to **reuse the established `WaitlistTab` pattern** rather than a parallel one: same chip/pill styling, same `paginate` helper, counts computed off the full set so they stay stable as the view narrows, and page resets to 0 on any filter/search change. The pre-existing last-active sort and pagination are unchanged; an empty result shows "No matches." No new shared styles and no sort dropdown (out of scope). `UsersTab` is already on the design-token allowlist (it carries the `rgba(255,255,255,0.6)` input/selection background, matching WaitlistTab). **Verified live as devtest** (search "jeff" → the 2 matching rows; Dormant chip → the 3 zero-conversation users; counts partition cleanly, 18 + 3 = 21). Build clean, 1186 tests pass; clean fast-forward push. **No new test** — these admin tabs have no component tests (WaitlistTab, which this mirrors, has none either); flagged to the founder as a follow-up if that convention should change.
- **Guided intake — section picker, marked-message selection, situation handoff wired** (2026-06-24, commit `0ea76c3`): The integration layer that makes the area-anchored flow run end-to-end, built to **reuse the chip pipeline** rather than a parallel one. (1) The tee-up emits a `---sections---` marker → the client renders a new **`SectionPicker`** (the five canonical Manual sections from `layers.ts`, name + tagline, navy accent cards); a tap routes through the existing `sendChipResponse`, so the section reaches the prompt next turn as `[selected from options] <name>` — no new selection pathway. (2) Focus picks reuse the existing `---chips---` mechanism. (3) The live-situation handoff is a **graduated escalation in the prompt** (reflect → redirect back to the area → if ignored/pushed, offer in words → only on an explicit yes emit `---start-situation---`) → the client renders a one-tap "Take this to its own conversation" button → `startConversation("situation")`. Plumbing: two boolean flags on the `message_complete` SSE event + `ChatMessage`, attached/cleared in `useChat`. Markers parse via a **tail-anchored `stripTrailingMarker`** helper (not a bare `indexOf`) so a token the model echoes in prose can't truncate a stored message — `ui-markers.test.ts` covers the regression. Desktop reuses `MobileSession`, so it renders there too. **Senior-engineer reviewed (SHIP WITH NOTES; both should-fixes — the indexOf hardening and a behavioral parse test — closed before ship).** Build clean, 1186 tests pass; clean fast-forward push. **Still pending (see Not Yet Functional):** live verification (guided can't run in dev — `conversation_modes` gate is OFF) and the model-behavioral parts (the escalation honoring the yes-gate; section-vs-focus disambiguation).
- **Guided intake rebuilt — area-anchored ("pick a section → drill to a focus → go deep")** (2026-06-23, commit `9da4563`): Replaced the person-anchored guided-intake mode (the "Pick someone of note in your life" opener + relationship fallback chain) with the area-anchored flow per the stated product intent: the user picks one **Manual section**, Jove **keeps them in it** and draws out the understanding it's about, and the **existing checkpoint ceremony** presents the save when ready (timing stays in the tracker/gate — this block does not re-decide it, so no duplicated proposal logic). The rewritten `guided-intake` Tier 3 block (`system-prompt.ts`) is generated, not pinned: **tee-up** (3 beats incl. the mandatory "one thing worth keeping" endpoint + the up-front "nothing saved unless you say so" deal) → **open the section** (orient + one short focus pick) → **drill to a focus** → **go deep** (episodic, behavior-before-feeling with an emotion-leading carve-out, skippable questions / "a blank is data") → **keep them in it** (no coverage-sweeping) → **invisible missing-piece steering** → **progress red-lines** (no "3 of 5"/percentages/streaks/"almost there"; progress named only backward) → **live-situation immutability** (reflect first, then offer a *separate* situation conversation; the mode never converts) → **posture yields to energy**. `POST-CONFIRM` gains a single-source guided branch (stop-only, area-framed off-ramp). The behavioral logic was reviewed by the applied-psychologist agent; its key finding — an always-eliciting mode must **defer to the existing `observation_miss_count` withdrawal-repair signal** rather than carry a second copy — was honored by *removal* (cut the over-assertions), not addition. **Teardown (net −344 lines):** deleted `guided-intake-copy.ts` (opener constant + fallback-variant detector), dropped the `guided_intake_opener_fired` analytics + its `useChat` wiring + the now-inert `guidedIntakeOpener` admin Voice-Studio override; fixed two admin "how it works" source strings. `intent.md` guided-intake line corrected from "more coverage than depth" to depth-on-one-section. Tests updated + tier3 snapshot regenerated (test count drops to 1172 as the dead opener-variant suite is removed). **Build clean, full suite green; rebased onto `origin/main` twice — parallel Home/desktop ships landed mid-flight — and fast-forward-pushed.** **Depends on (not built here — see Not Yet Functional):** the section-picker UI + its show-logic, the selection-arrives-as-a-message contract, and a "start a situation conversation" action; the block degrades gracefully without them (tee-up invites a typed area; Jove points rather than launches).
- **Manual as a Home tile + shared compact "ways to begin" doors** (2026-06-23, commit `95d283c`): Two Home refinements. **(1)** The 5-layer manual index (`LayerIndex`) is now wrapped in a white card matching Home's other tiles (cream-bright + hairline + `--session-card-shadow`); the trailing row divider is dropped so the card bottom reads clean; and **"Read your manual" hides until the manual has at least one entry** (`startedCount > 0`). **(2)** The three entry-point doors (Bring a situation / Guided / Upload) were pulled out of the per-surface markup into one shared **`WaysToBegin`** component (+ a shared `LineIcon` primitive), removing the prior desktop/mobile duplication where each Home hand-rolled its own doors. Desktop renders them as the 3-across vertical cards (visually unchanged); **mobile now renders the same doors as compact horizontal rows** (icon · title+desc · arrow) instead of the old one-primary-button-plus-two-secondary-links card, roughly halving the section's height. The door copy + launch wiring moved out of `MobileHome`/`DesktopHome`, so the onboarding + guided-intake source-string tests were repointed at `WaysToBegin` (the wiring moved, not deleted). Verified live on mobile + desktop as devtest (manual card renders; the doors launch all three modes; "Read your manual" shows with entries present). Build + 1191 tests + tsc green; rebased onto `origin/main` and fast-forward-pushed.
- **Orienting tile on Home for first-time users** (2026-06-18): a brand-new user (nothing to resume, no entries) now sees a one-time **WELCOME** tile in Home's top slot — *"Start a conversation below — what you confirm becomes your Manual, the five layers of how you operate. Nothing's saved unless you say so."* It orients them to the screen they just landed on (the conversation below + the 5-layer index they'll fill) and **restores the just-in-time "nothing's saved" control line** the 3-card removal dropped (the deferred item in the entry below). Renders only when `!heroConv && startedCount === 0`; returning users keep the resume tile; no button (it points to the ways-to-begin). Lives in the shared resume slot of both `MobileHome` + `DesktopHome` (`heroConv ? resume : startedCount===0 ? welcome : null`). tsc + full suite green.
- **Retire the in-session 3-card entry screen — Home is the single launchpad** (2026-06-18, ADR-049): the session view's "What's on your mind today?" empty-state (3 cards: Navigate a situation / Guided intake / Upload) duplicated Home's "ways to begin," and a new user landed on it after intake. Removed it: deleted the `entryCards` block + `chipsVisible` + welcome-greeting helpers from `MobileSession` and the orphaned `EntryCard.tsx`; dropped the now-unused `startConversation` / `firstName` / `confirmedEntries` props (MobileSession no longer starts conversations — it's only the active chat). Rerouted both entry points to Home: **new users land on Home after intake** (removed the `firstRun` exception in MainApp's landing effect — supersedes ADR-047's first-run-into-conversation) and **"+ New session" → Home**. All three modes stay reachable from Home on mobile + desktop. The two source-string tests pinning the modes to MobileSession were rewritten to assert against MobileHome/DesktopHome (the guard moved, not deleted). tsc + full suite + senior-reviewed. Deferred: the real first-run screen (Jove opener + chips, `first-run-plan.md`); moving the "nothing's saved" just-in-time line onto Home.
- **Desktop front-door — mid-width column + scoped header (Phases D–E)** (2026-06-18, ADR-048): **Phase D** — at mid-width (431–1029px) the signed-in app drops the phone-frame "vitrine" for a **centered phone-width column** (~430px on calm `--session-linen` margins, a soft `--session-card-shadow` lift, no frame/paratext). `DesktopVitrine` is retired from the authed path (`MobileLayout`) but **kept for `/login`**; the `DesktopShell` cutover stays at 1030px (the column owns 431–1029); the ≤430px phone view is geometrically unchanged. Founder chose the centered column over shrinking the desktop shell or keeping-and-fixing the frame. **Phase E** — the "Going deeper · {layer}" scoped context folds into `RoomHeader` on desktop; `MobileSession`'s in-body bar is gated on `showTopBar` so the context shows once (header on desktop, in-body on mobile), never doubled. Both phases tsc-clean, full-suite-gated, senior-reviewed (SHIP — the one noted item was this doc sync: the "~860px breakpoint" in the original ADR-048 forecast was dropped when the founder chose the centered column). Mobile + login untouched. Verification gap unchanged: no component tests; the ≤430px equivalence + mid-width look were reasoned from geometry and reviewed, live screenshots deferred to a founder pass.
- **Desktop front-door — Home + Manual (Phases A–C)** (2026-06-18, ADR-048; plan `docs/desktop-redesign-plan.md` + approved mockup `mockups/desktop-frontdoor-v1.html`): brought ADR-047's new concepts to desktop (≥1030px) natively, **sharing components, not forking** (founder mandate "no duplicated components, properly refactored"). **Phase A** (commit `3f7b48e`): extracted `useHomeModel` (greeting / date / resume-thread selection / layers / started count — one source of truth) + `LayerIndex` (the 5-layer go-deeper index; `variant: mobile|desktop` = density only) into `src/components/home/`; refactored `MobileHome` onto them, verified byte-identical (senior-reviewed); +`useHomeModel.test.ts` (10 tests). **Phase B** (commit `b31c78c`): new `DesktopHome` (greeting + slim resume ribbon + a 3-equal-card "ways to begin" triptych — Bring a situation / Guided / Upload, surfacing the existing `startConversation` modes — + `LayerIndex variant=desktop`); **no recents** (sidebar owns history); dropped the `home→session` coercion so returning desktop users **land on Home** (`LAND_ON_HOME`, same first-run/checkpoint/opener-streaming guards as mobile); the sidebar's big "Your Manual" card slimmed to a nav row with its count, joined by Home + Conversation rows + a Home rail button; orphaned `.mw-dsk-manual-card` CSS removed. **Phase C** (commit `7ca43a8`): the Manual "share your manual" half-sheet renders as a **centered modal** on desktop (gated by an `isDesktop` prop; the mobile bottom-sheet is unchanged). Each phase tsc-clean, full-suite-gated (54 test files via the pre-commit `next build` + vitest), senior-reviewed (three passes incl. a final integrated review). **Mobile path untouched.** **Verification gap, named:** these surfaces have no component tests and the authed app wasn't visually driven from the agent session (password-entry is barred by the agent safety rules) — live screenshots deferred to a founder-assisted pass. **Still in this initiative:** Phase D (lower the breakpoint to ~860px + retire the phone-frame vitrine for signed-in users — higher-risk, decoupled) and Phase E (fold the scoped "Going deeper · {layer}" bar into `RoomHeader`).
- **Guided intake + Upload reachable from Home (returning-user regression fix)** (2026-06-18, branch claude/admiring-cartwright-fd338c): Closes the regression the front-door redesign flagged below — with the drawer's entry-cards path gone, the Guided-intake and Upload conversation modes were only reachable by brand-new users. Added two quiet secondary links under "Bring a situation" on Home (`MobileHome.tsx`) — *"Let Jove lead with questions"* (guided-intake) and *"Bring something you've written"* (upload) — placed on the returning-user Home, deliberately decoupled from the new-user entry-cards screen so the deferred first-run redesign (focused input + seeded chips) won't re-orphan them. The handler in `MainApp.tsx` generalized from situation-only (`onBringSituation`) to `onStartConversation(mode)`. **Root-cause fix in `useChat.ts`:** `startConversation` early-returned on `if (messages.length > 0)`, so for any returning user (who always has an auto-resumed thread) *every* Home start — including the shipped "Bring a situation" button — silently no-op'd into the existing thread. It now resets-then-starts like `startExploration` (and completes the prior thread fire-and-forget); the in-flight `isLoading || isStreaming` guard still covers double-taps. The triplicated reset block (startNewSession / startExploration / startConversation) was extracted into one `resetConversationState(origin)` helper (no new duplication). Verified on mobile width as devtest: both links start fresh conversations; Upload renders its mode-gated opener verbatim (proves mode propagation end-to-end); guided-intake's prompt block injects (uncached prompt 2618→4368 tokens). Build + 1173 tests + eslint + tsc green; rebased onto `origin/main` (resolved the `MobileHome` import overlap with the Roman-numeral→`LayerIcon` emblem change) and fast-forward-pushed. (Note: the `conversation_modes` feature gate is OFF in dev, force-downgrading modes to "situation" server-side; toggled on only to verify, then restored to OFF.)
- **Front-door redesign — mobile-first (Phases 0–5)** (2026-06-17, branch claude/angry-zhukovsky-fff218): A six-phase, mobile-first redesign of the app's front door, merged as a unit (each phase tsc-clean, senior-reviewed, and build-verified by the pre-commit hook — `next build` + 1173 tests). **Phase 0 (engine):** a new `started_layer` `ExplorationContext` + opener branch in `system-prompt.ts` so "go deeper" on an already-populated Manual layer opens from the existing entries instead of telling the user the layer "is empty" (the old `empty_layer` bug fired on populated layers); the duplicated inline type in `api/chat/route.ts` updated too; analytics `EntryPoint` gains `"explore"` and `startExploration` reports it (was mis-reporting `"situation"`). **Phase 1 (foundation):** the LIGHT theme retuned to the prototype palette — warm-white ground, brown = the user, navy = Jove, Jove bubble top-rule removed (token → transparent) — and the type system swapped to **Fraunces** (display headings, opt-in via `--font-display`) + **Newsreader** (body serif, on `--font-serif` and the legacy `--font-spectral`) + **Plus Jakarta Sans** (UI) + **JetBrains Mono** (meta); dark theme untouched (the front door is light-first). Text/meta ink tiers kept at warm-graphite (≥6.5:1) rather than the lighter prototype values that failed AA. **Phase 2 (navigation):** the slide-out drawer is **RETIRED** (−572 lines: `SessionDrawer.tsx`, the edge-swipe gesture, the `onOpenDrawer` chain across 6 files, the TopBar menu glyph) and replaced by a persistent **bottom nav (Home · Manual · Talk · You)**; "You" is a relabel of Settings; **Crisis moved into "You"/Settings** (the real safety net is Jove's in-conversation protocol — the static link is the passive backstop); desktop keeps its sidebar (a `home→session` coercion keeps the widened `MobileView` enum compiling). **Phase 3 (Home + landing inversion):** a real **Home** screen (greeting, resume hero, "bring a situation", the 5-layer Manual index with a "N of 5 started" marker + per-layer "go deeper" wired to Phase 0, recent conversations with "Show all"); and the riskiest change — **returning users now LAND on Home** instead of auto-dropping into chat (first-run = `isNewUser`, pending-checkpoint, and opener-still-streaming users still go straight to the conversation), behind a one-line `LAND_ON_HOME` revert flag in `MainApp.tsx`. A `tagline` field added to the source-of-truth `LAYERS`. **Phase 4 (Manual read view):** layers **collapse (collapsed by default)** with an entry count + chevron; entries carry **provenance** ("Added from a conversation · {month}", `created_at` plumbed through `buildLayers`); the per-entry "Explore further" is removed (go-deeper lives on Home); Edit preserved; admin/readOnly renders everything open. **Phase 5 (Chat):** a "**GOING DEEPER · {layer}**" context bar on scoped chats. **Mobile-first** — view via device mode; desktop keeps its sidebar (the in-between "vitrine" width is awkward with the new bottom nav — a deferred desktop-pass item). **Deferred / tracked:** the first-run/activation redesign (seeded chips + focused input, per `first-run-plan.md`); **Guided intake + Upload reachability for returning users** (a regression introduced by the drawer retirement — task chip filed); the desktop pass; the first-person Manual masthead copy question; and the docs wrap (this entry + a pending `decisions.md` ADR superseding the dark-default + "every component works in both themes" decisions, and a `rules.md` Typography-table sync to the new fonts). **Merged via fast-forward push** after merging `origin/main` in cleanly (zero conflicts; main's 18 commits — incl. the onboarding collapse and checkpoint-tuning — are in).

- **Checkpoint: title-carries-the-discovery, body-depth-restore, + transition-leak bugfix** (2026-06-16, branch claude/loving-nightingale-1252a3): Three fixes from a live prod read of the prior change. **(1) Bug — transition line leaked into the card.** In split-delivery, Jove's turn is split into `leadIn` (reflection → its own bubble) and `remainder` (the "I want to put something in your Manual" transition onward); the remainder became the checkpoint message's text (`call-persona.ts`). The *failure* path cleared it; the *success* path didn't — so on every successful checkpoint the bare transition line rendered **inside the card, between title and body**. Fixed: the success path now clears it too (empty when the lead-in already shipped as its own row; `stripCheckpointFromText` when no split). Pre-existing — not caused by the prompt work. **(2) Body came out too thin** (~48 words) — removing the 80-word floor + "stop when delivered, don't pad" over-corrected into a flat two-sentence restatement. Rewrote the body instruction to **develop the ONE pattern to the depth of the "Deeper" exemplar** (follow its internal logic: what fires it, what it does, why they can't stop, what it costs) — depth-not-length, one pattern, explicitly "a flat two-sentence restatement is too thin." Dropped the {mechanism/protects/costs} facet-checklist framing (it produced either sprawl or thin). **(3) Title was the surface behavior, not the discovery.** "I perform interest to stay close to people I love" is something the user could have written walking in. The title now must clear THE BAR (the discovery, not the could-have-written-it behavior), with the worked contrast in-prompt (generic "perform interest to stay close" fails; discovery "I keep debating because going quiet feels like pulling away" lands). **Design boundary set:** the exact *scope/conditions* of a pattern (which relationships, which situations) is NOT a one-shot composer job — the composer scopes conservatively to the evidence and the **refine loop** (user pushback → re-compose) narrows it; that loop is working in prod. Zero new fields/tables/model-calls; the title fix extends an existing rule (THE BAR) to the title, the body fix is a rewrite, the bug fix mirrors the existing failure-path cleanup. Build clean, 770/770 persona tests pass. **Validation on prod.** **Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch.**

- **Checkpoint entry: title is the handle, body is focused depth** (2026-06-16, branch claude/loving-nightingale-1252a3): The composer kept producing entries that read as *summaries you can't hold*, even after the earlier rewrite. A fresh-session full-pipeline audit (define-the-artifact-first, not re-tune-the-prompt) found two structural causes in the composer system prompt, both fixed by **subtraction**: (1) the `content` block instructed a "Passage (80+ words): ground in the actual moment, the specific person, what it cost" — a length **floor** + a walk-the-scene **recap** instruction = the 190-word wall, by design; (2) the `content` block opened with a "Statement (first sentence)" that **duplicated** the `name` title (logic-duplication). Fix: **title promoted to lead the prompt as THE ARTIFACT** (the line shown in the Manual list; body collapses behind it, so the title carries holdability) — complete first-person sentence, what they DO, ~6–12 words, picturable/complete (north star: "I tend to stay in things I've outgrown until I'm forced to leave"); and the **body rewritten to focus-bounded depth, not length**: "pick the ONE or TWO of {mechanism, what it protects, what it costs} that carry the recognition — not all of them; go DOWN into the one pattern, never ACROSS the session; stop when the recognition is delivered, don't pad." Key design call (after a substance-vs-holdability debate across two sessions): **holdability lives in the TITLE, substance in the BODY; control body length by FOCUS, not a word-cap** — a hard short cap was rejected because it would resurrect the "too thin / unsatisfying" failure. `validateComposedEntry`: dropped the `<80` "too short" floor (false-warns on good lean entries); kept the `>150` ceiling as the sprawl/drift detector. Zero new prompt rules — net subtraction + a re-point + facet-focus replacing two instructions; removes the title/body duplication; no new model call. `deriveSummaryFromContent` (fallback uses content's first sentence) verified low-risk — composer always emits its own `summary`. Build clean, 770/770 persona tests pass. **Validation on prod.** **Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch.**

- **Checkpoint: behavior-not-feeling voice beat + title enforcement** (2026-06-16, branch claude/loving-nightingale-1252a3): A prod sim of the composer rewrite still produced a situational, non-"I", non-landing title ("The Decisions About Him Are Ones I Have to Make Without Him") and fired on the first emotional peak. Root cause: the composer only renders the VOICE's reflection — the voice handed it a *feeling/situation*, not a *behavior* — and the title rules don't bind in-prompt under load. Two-part fix. **(1) Voice (`REBUILT_MECHANICS`):** two revisions — *behavior-not-feeling* ("a feeling landing hard is the doorway, not the entry — keep going until you can name the behavior under it: what they do, across more than this one moment, not the feeling or the single situation that surfaced it"; and "the behavior itself — how they operate, not the feeling of the moment"), which fixes both "fired too quickly" and "not landing" at the root by changing what the composer receives; and *breadth* (for a how-they-operate pattern, "anywhere else" = a different person or part of life, not another scene with the same person; scope to one place if that's all there is). **(2) Composer title enforcement (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`):** `validateHeadline` now splits HARD failures (non-"I" subject, feeling-state subject, banned felt-state verb the user never said) from SOFT (word count, single-example hedge), and **dropped the old "trigger word required" check** (it mis-flagged good behavioral titles like "I reach for more depth than I get back"; word count loosened 4-10→4-12). On a HARD fail, a new `recomposeHeadline` fires ONE focused title-only model call to rewrite it behaviorally; it runs **only on the miss path**, so cost is bounded to the title-fail rate, and it reverses the prior single-call-no-rewrite policy deliberately (the policy assumed the prompt binds; the prod evidence says it doesn't). **Cost label:** one extra COMPOSITION_MODEL call per hard-failing title; deletion condition = titles stop hard-failing. Build clean, 770/770 persona tests pass (validateHeadline tests updated for the hard/soft split; trigger-word test replaced). **Validation on prod** (re-run a conversation; expect a behavioral "I …" title, fired after the behavior is named, not the first feeling). **Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch.**

- **Checkpoint composer rewrite — cut to taste + behavioral titles** (2026-06-16, branch claude/loving-nightingale-1252a3): The composer prompt (the Sonnet/Opus call that turns a confirmed reflection into the saved Manual entry — title + prose + layer + summary, `confirm-checkpoint.ts`) went from ~110 lines to ~38. Same treatment as the voice rebuild: the old prompt was ~half title aesthetics + repeated taste rules, and a real prod entry came out poetic ("I Feel Alone When I Reach and He Doesn't Reach Back"), recappy, and altitude-flipped **despite** the prompt already banning feeling-state titles, poetry, recaps, and over-generalizing — proof of **rule saturation**, not a coverage gap (applied-psychologist reviewed the old prompt vs the new draft and confirmed the lean version lands better by protecting the one attention-expensive move that produces recognition). What the rewrite KEEPS as red lines the model can't infer: the output/JSON contract, the clinical-term ban, fidelity-to-the-user's-charged-words (no vocabulary upgrades), scope-to-evidence, first-person, no time/session refs, the strength-care note (a strength is allowed to just be a strength), and ONE depth exemplar (recap→deeper). What it CUTS: the ~27-line headline rulebook with ~9 banned-title examples, two of three exemplars, and the 3–4× restated "not a recap / field note / no nominalizations / universal-tone" rules. **Two behavior changes inside the rewrite:** (1) **THE BAR raised** — the entry must make the user feel "seen and a little caught off guard" ("how did it see that"), a recognition that reorganizes, not a summary they'd nod at. (2) **Behavioral titles + openings** — the headline AND the statement (first sentence) must be the portable behavior ("I reach for more depth than I get back"), never scenario-specific (no "with him," no names) — the specific person/scene lives ONLY in the body. This moves the over-claim guard off the title onto the body's scope + the "can/sometimes" hedge + the voice's breadth-check (pending). Also added one **log-only** check to `validateHeadline` (prod-visible via the existing `console.warn`): flag feeling-state-subject titles ("I feel/am…") so we can measure the rate; it does not block. Build clean, 767/767 persona tests pass. **Validation is on prod** (re-run a conversation and read the entry against the north-star "I Reach for More Depth Than I Get Back"). **Known follow-ups (not in this ship):** the voice MECHANICS breadth-check (probe a different person/domain before a relational entry); a soft layer-preference so an entry started via a specific layer (exploration mode) prefers that layer; and `validateHeadline`'s trigger-word/word-count checks may now over-warn on good behavioral titles — align them once we see real new-prompt output. **Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch.**

- **Voice/checkpoint decoupling — Phase 1: extraction brief cut from the rebuilt voice** (2026-06-16, branch claude/loving-nightingale-1252a3): The rebuilt voice no longer receives the per-turn extraction **brief** (the `── BRIEF FOR YOUR NEXT RESPONSE ──` block from `formatExtractionForPersona` — the "stay in it / go for the mechanism / there's a real piece here / still thin on X" steering). `REBUILT_MECHANICS` already owns the "when to propose" decision; the brief duplicated it and turned each turn into a deliverable-countdown — the interrogation cadence a founder A/B ablation isolated (a transcript with extraction+checkpoint removed scored materially higher on being-seen/opening-up, ~3.8→4.3, and offered a higher-altitude entry, vs. the full-machinery arm). One-line change, scoped to the **rebuilt path only** (`system-prompt.ts` ~1047, removed the `if (extractionContext) rebuiltDynamic += extractionContext` append); the **legacy path still appends the brief** so the `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT="legacy"` rollback is byte-identical. Extraction still runs every turn and feeds the **SAVE-time composer** (`composeManualEntry` reads `language_bank` / `distinct_contexts` / `depth` / `sage_brief` / `current_thread` straight off the state object) + the **safety detectors** unchanged — only the per-turn *narration to Jove* is gone. **Safety verified before cutting** (applied-psychologist Mode-A + code trace): crisis 988 is covered by `detectCrisisInUserMessage`→`handleCrisisDetection` (regex on the raw user message, brief-independent) plus `REBUILT_LIMITS`; professional referral is covered by the always-on `clinical-and-tail` Tier-3 block (`shouldRender: () => true`) — so deleting the brief removes **no** safety behavior. `formatExtractionForPersona` left in place (legacy still uses it); it + the rebuilt-path comment are deleted in the Phase-3b legacy teardown. **Build clean, 767/767 persona tests pass.** Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch (shared main checkout holds other sessions' WIP). **Deferred — the gate half:** the planned removal of the count-based firing gates (2-scenes / depth / mechanism / pattern-engaged) was held because it collides with the just-shipped Checkpoint Tuning dials; that question is being tested first on prod via those dials (loosen min-scenes/depth/engagement-failsafe) before any code removal. See [[project_checkpoint_voice_decoupling]] memory + `docs/decisions.md` (pending ADR).

- **Checkpoint-firing dials in admin + new Tuning tab** (2026-06-16, branch claude/strange-feistel-e71e93): The four thresholds that decide WHEN a checkpoint fires were inline literals in `persona-pipeline.ts`. They're now admin-editable dials, live without a deploy: **min concrete scenes** (2, range 1–5), **cooldown turns since last** (5, 0–20), **engagement failsafe turn** (12, 3–40), and **depth floor** (mechanism; the 5-level dropdown). Architecture mirrors `persona_voice_overrides` exactly (reuse, not a parallel system): the literals are lifted into one `CHECKPOINT_TUNING_DEFAULTS` floor + a `CHECKPOINT_TUNING_FIELDS` single-source map (`checkpoint-tuning.ts`), backed by a new single-row **typed** `checkpoint_tuning` table (typed columns beat stringifying numbers into a key/value text store) + append-only `checkpoint_tuning_history`, RLS-on/no-policies (service-role only). `getCheckpointTuning(admin)` folds into the existing `loadConversationContext` `Promise.all` batch (no extra round-trip) and **fails open to the code defaults** on any missing row / null / out-of-range column, so an empty or unreachable table behaves exactly as today. The loaded values thread through `validateMaterialQuality` + `applyCheckpointGates` (both gate functions, both prod callers — web `call-persona` and SMS `persona-bridge`) via a trailing `tuning = CHECKPOINT_TUNING_DEFAULTS` param, so every other caller and the ~50 existing tests are unaffected. **Dials move eagerness only** — the quality gates (`pattern_engaged`, `has_mechanism`, behavior-driver link, charged-language, crisis) stay locked in code; shown read-only in the panel so the eagerness/quality boundary is legible. New **Tuning** admin tab (`/admin?section=tuning`) holds Feature gates + Voice editor + the new Checkpoint dials; **Health is now diagnostics-only** (the two tuners moved out). New `/api/admin/checkpoint-tuning` (GET fields+defaults+bounds, PATCH save-with-history / reset-to-null; requireAdmin + service-role + bounds-validated). New `checkpoint-tuning.test.ts` (14 — resolver fail-open/clamp contract + gating-honors-tuning proofs). **Build clean, 1174/1174 pass, design-tokens guard passes.** **MIGRATION PENDING MANUAL RUN** (`20260615000000_checkpoint_tuning.sql`) — additive, two new global-config tables, no user data; code fails open to the floor until it's run (panel renders defaults; Save 500s until the table exists). **Shipped via fast-forward push from the branch** — the shared main checkout held other sessions' uncommitted work, so an in-place merge there was unsafe; this never touched it. **Note for a later doc pass:** the admin "How Jove works" / prompt-architecture pages still describe these four thresholds as fixed numbers.
