- **Checkpoint title rule — kitchen-table test + intellectualizing-abstraction ban (tightened, not bolted on)** (2026-06-15, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98): Founder proposed a ~130-word title rule; ~85% already existed in the composer (the trigger format, observable-verb requirement, felt-state-verb ban, user's-own-words carve-out). Applied the revise-before-add discipline: kept the 3 genuinely-new ideas, dropped the 2 overfit traps, folded one redundant paragraph out. **Added** to `confirm-checkpoint.ts` title rules: (1) the **kitchen-table test** — "the title must sound like something the user would say out loud to a friend, never a document heading or a tagline; flattest plain verb; if it reads like a label it's wrong" (this is an organizing heuristic that *absorbed* the old SECONDARY VERB RULE paragraph, which was **cut** — net roughly flat); (2) **one plain clause** — if the title needs an "and" or a second comma clause it's carrying two patterns, split the entry before titling. **Enhanced** the "Nominalizations" bad-example into a stated ban on **intellectualizing abstractions** ("Sitting in Uncertainty," "The Not-Knowing") — name what they DO, not the state. **Deliberately rejected** from the founder's draft (overfit): the rigid "When [situation], I [behavior]" word order (the existing when/before/after format is more flexible and avoids every title sharing one rhythm — variance rule R-21), and the three-example ban list (collapsed to the tell + two examples so the model bans the register, not just those phrases). Build clean, 1160/1160 pass.

- **Cut #1: realign the checkpoint gate to ADR-043 — distinct_contexts is a signal, not a wall** (2026-06-15, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98): The cut-audit found (and verified against the decision log) that the checkpoint gate enforced a hard `distinct_contexts >= 2` block that **ADR-043 Decision 3 explicitly decided to DROP** ("a single vivid scene in the user's own charged language is saveable; requiring two contexts blocks exactly the single-powerful-moment case the recognition mechanism exists for"), and **ADR-045 recorded as "in force" (dropped)**. The code had drifted back to enforcing it, and soak iteration 12 made it stricter still (removed the first-checkpoint `=1` escape, setting `minContexts = 2` universally). Net effect: the product's best moment — one deep, vivid scene — was being silently blocked. **Fix:** removed the blocking `distinct_contexts` check from `validateMaterialQuality` (persona-pipeline.ts) and from the soft brief mirror in `formatExtractionForPersona` (extraction.ts gateReady + the "second situation" gap branch). distinct_contexts is untouched as data — it still feeds `validateHeadline`'s "can"/"sometimes" hedge, so a single-scene entry saves with a scoped title and the over-claim guard moves to the headline + the user's confirm, exactly as ADR-043 planned. `concrete_examples >= 2`, mechanism, charged-language, and depth gates are unchanged (cut #1 is only the distinct_contexts wall). New regression test pins that a single distinct context no longer blocks, so the wall can't silently return. Build clean, 753/753 persona tests pass. First of the removal-audit cuts; reversible in one commit, and `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT` still flips the whole voice back.

- **Landing copy — strengths-first Manual, trust cards, Lucas's edits** (2026-06-15, merge `625cfa1`, branch claude/festive-joliot-555715): Public marketing page (`src/app/page.tsx`) copy pass incorporating the founder's and Lucas's website edits. **The Manual list now leads with strengths** ("Where you're strong" → 01, previously last at 05), with friendlier first-person labels ("The patterns people misread in you," "How you process the world," "What helps you function at your best") — kept as **five distinct sections**; Lucas's suggestion to merge strengths into the friction line was rejected to preserve the equal-weight / strengths-first principle (see the manual-taxonomy review thread). The Manual blurb is now honest about authorship: "a living, shareable document… **from your own words**… nothing goes in without your say-so" (was "in your own voice" — Jove composes the entry prose, so "voice" overclaimed). **Two new "What makes it different" cards:** *Built with guardrails* (the safety-floor differentiator — care general AI chat tools don't build in; knows when to point to real help) and *Grounded, not guesswork* (a deliberately light credibility line — "draws on established approaches to self-understanding and research led by neurodivergent people" — true regardless of the still-open framework decision; the fuller "why you can trust this" page is deferred until that lands). Also: Jove is introduced on first mention as "Mywalnut's AI conversational partner"; the hero reads "a working manual **of how you operate**"; the gap section is tightened ("conversations," "late-diagnosed"); the footer self-understanding/crisis statement is cleaner with the crisis line on its own row ("call 988"). **Dropped** Lucas's "crack that nut open" tagline. **Copy/markup only** — no code, schema, API, or tests touched; the marketing labels intentionally differ from the in-app section names (`src/lib/manual/layers.ts`). **Tests/build:** the feature-branch pre-commit hook ran a clean `next build` + **1159/1159 pass**; landing render spot-checked (HTTP 200, strengths-first order, both new cards present, no error overlay). **Open (founder calls):** the "myWalnut" capitalization rebrand, and the framework-grounded trust page (pending the manual-taxonomy decision).

- **Walnut-brain favicon + app icon set replaces the M tile** (2026-06-14, merge `7c485d0`, branch claude/trusting-antonelli-26c66d): The browser favicon and all PWA/app icons are now Jeff's own walnut-brain logo (owned by mywalnut, Inc.) — a walnut shell with the kernel drawn as a brain in negative space. Vectorized 1:1 from the founder's raster (`mockups/walnut-icon/source-original.png`) with node-potrace; shell recolored to walnut `#AA7852`, kernel filled cream `#F4EDE0` so it stays solid on any background. Regenerated `src/app/favicon.ico` (16/32/48), `public/icons/icon-192|512.png` (dark graphite rounded tile), the maskable pair (full-bleed, mark inside the 80% safe zone), and `public/apple-touch-icon.png` (180, full-bleed). A higher-res source supplied mid-thread traces crisp enough that one master serves every size — the interim simplified-favicon variant was removed (no separate small-size artwork needed). Trace masters + a self-contained review sheet live in `mockups/walnut-icon/` (README documents the regeneration pipeline). **Assets-only** — no code, schema, API, or tests touched; `manifest.webmanifest` and `layout.tsx` icon metadata are unchanged (filenames identical). **Tests/build:** clean `next build`, 1139/1139 pass. **Founder note:** Chrome caches favicons aggressively — after deploy your own browser may need a hard refresh before the walnut shows; new visitors see it immediately.

- **Voice Studio — live-editable voice text in admin (v1: CHARACTER + openers + post-confirm line)** (2026-06-14, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98): The founder iterates on the rebuilt voice constantly, and every tweak was a code+deploy cycle through an agent. This adds an admin **Voice editor** panel (next to Feature gates) where four VOICE-text fields are editable live, no deploy: **CHARACTER** (REBUILT_CHARACTER), the **situation opener**, the **guided-intake opener**, and the **post-confirm first-entry line**. Architecture mirrors `feature_gates` exactly (reuse, not a parallel system): new `persona_voice_overrides` table (key pk, text_override, enabled, updated_at, updated_by) + append-only `persona_voice_override_history`, RLS-on/no-policies (service-role only). `getVoiceOverrides(admin)` is folded into the existing `loadConversationContext` `Promise.all` batch (no extra round-trip) and **fails open to `{}`** = all code defaults, so an empty/unreachable table behaves exactly as today. Resolution is `options.voiceOverrides?.x ?? CONSTANT` at each site: CHARACTER in the rebuilt `buildSystemPromptBlocks` tier1; the two openers + post-confirm threaded through `Tier3Flags` into their render blocks; the post-confirm's second consumer (`buildPostConfirmFallback` in call-persona, reached in a catch outside `ctx` scope) reads a hoisted `postConfirmLineOverride`. The **code constants stay the permanent floor**; "Reset to default" = `enabled:false` (non-destructive, code value resolves next turn). `buildSystemPromptBlocks` stays **synchronous** (the override is config, resolved upstream — the design's "make the builder async" seam was rejected: it ripples into the hot path + sim + ~150 sync tests). Admin route `/api/admin/persona-voice` (GET fields+defaults, PATCH save-with-history / reset), requireAdmin + service-role, never logs override text (key only). **Deliberately LOCKED (code-only, shown read-only in the panel):** REBUILT_LIMITS (crisis 988 protocol / no-clinical-names / no-prescribing), REBUILT_MECHANICS (carries the "in your Manual" contract), the crisis phrase list, the card-detector regex, OTP caps — "all of it visible, not all of it free-text" was the founder's accepted safety line. **Deliberately NOT in v1: the 4 checkpoint replies** — they're synthetic *user*-turn strings (the "I saved that" line is replayed as if the user said it) resolved in a pure function (`mapSystemMessages`) shared by chat+sim and wired to a suppression-detection set; lowest edit-value, highest coupling. Fast-follow if wanted; MECHANICS is the other fast-follow (behind a save-check that reuses detect-checkpoint's regex as the single source of truth so the phrase can't be dropped). Tests: new `voice-overrides.test.ts` (13 — resolver fail-open contract + actual prompt substitution). **Build clean, 1159/1159 pass, design-tokens guard passes** (panel is token-clean). **MIGRATION PENDING MANUAL RUN** (`20260614000000_persona_voice_overrides.sql`) — additive, safe; code fails open until it's run. Phases 2/3 (openers/post-confirm proved + MECHANICS-with-guard) and the numeric pacing knobs (turns-since-checkpoint via a `feature_gates.int_value` column) are deferred.

- **Soak iteration 12 — first-checkpoint lighter gate RETIRED + type-testing hint sharpened** (2026-06-12, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98): Founder's exchange 7 (bf/music) produced a first entry organized at the symptom level (sound) when the user's own material sat one level up ("couldn't control my environment" unprompted; lights confirmed ≠ sound; "i like music, but not all the time" — the never-probed discrimination in her opening line). The structural cause: the entry fired on the FIRST-CHECKPOINT fast path ("teaching moment... needs to experience the loop quickly"), which carried FOUR leniencies — extraction-prompt lighter gate (1 example / 1 context / mechanism-OR-link), the brief's gateReady mirror, and in the hard gate (`validateMaterialQuality`) depth-at-feeling (vs mechanism), 1 scene (vs 2), 1 context (vs 2), mechanism-OR-link (vs both). **All retired — one bar for every checkpoint.** The activation-era doctrine is superseded: the user's first entry was reliably their thinnest, and THE DEAL (iter 10) now teaches the loop up front; "fewer, deeper, later — from entry one." The `isFirstCheckpoint` params survive underscore-prefixed for signature stability across ~50 call sites (delete at the next signature change); the flag still drives POST_CONFIRM_FIRST_ENTRY_SCAFFOLD + legacy Tier 3 (untouched). **Also shipped:** the iteration-9 type-testing hint in the gateReady brief gains the bare-assent clause — "a yes to your list doesn't settle it; an exception they name or an instance they bring unasked does" (exchange 7: the prompted TV/fan/lights yes-list was read as settled span; the composer correctly refused the higher altitude because the span was never OWNED, so the conversation must collect ownership). docs/system.md first-checkpoint exception marked RETIRED. Tests updated to pin equalization (first checkpoint must fail/pass exactly like any other; 1140/1140 pass, clean build). **Watch:** first sessions now end with no entry more often — that's the design ("a finished conversation" with nothing to file is a valid ending); if betas churn before any first entry, revisit the bar's height, not the carve-out. Held: statements-vs-reads register (two strikes; third earns a revision of the existing offered-not-law line).

- **Soak iteration 11 — answerable questions + the dead-end seam (the lean cut of the robust-entry work)** (2026-06-12, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98): Founder note: "how long has this happened / when did it start / what does this cost you are hard to answer." Root cause was our own extraction brief commanding the failing forms. A 3-workflow pass (robust-entry anatomy → question-form mechanism → adversarial overbuild/overfit audit) found (a) the two founder-rated GOOD entries both contain a self-verdict reversal + a history line, all four THIN entries contain neither — but encoding that as composer rules was judged **partially overfit** (n=6, one subject, post-hoc; entry 6 itself contains a de-adopted self-label and is still thin) and the rule risked teaching Jove to FISH for self-judgments; (b) the answerable-forms swap rests on replicated mechanism (alexithymia ~50% of autistic adults, recognition-beats-recall, ADHD time-blindness), not on the n=6. **Shipped the lean cut only:** (E) `extraction.ts` brief lines swapped — "ask about the body, the cost, what fires it" → offer reads to confirm/correct (cost = name a likely consequence from the walked scene; body = what would someone watching have seen; history = a read to take or correct, never "ask how long"); same swap on the "Ask about the cost before proposing" readiness line. (C, trimmed) the JOVE BRIEF spec gains one bullet: record a self-judgment the user passed on themselves (their words) and whether it's still standing or overturned — so it survives the composer's 8-message window; observation only, no enforcement. (Seam) `voice-scaffold.ts` MECHANICS amends the 3-hour-old seam definition with the negative case: "A dead end is not a seam — 'I don't know' means change the angle or hand them the wheel, not reach for the proposal. Propose where something landed." (exchange 6 fired a checkpoint straight after two "i don't know"s). **Deliberately NOT shipped** (audit verdicts: defer/cut): composer source-gated reversal/history conditionals + bare-assent boundary (rule-pile signature: conditional enforcement on a prompt whose own REQUIRED rules are "regularly skipped" per its validators; revisit only if thin entries persist with >1 user in the dataset — first move then is a worked exemplar, never the conditional), and the voice never-said marker specialization (+14w over budget; "a reversal" already covers it; live verdict-hunting incentive). Watch list: thin entries post-E/C, hard-form questions persisting, proposed-reads harvesting bare assents (acquiescence), dead-end checkpoint fires. Build clean, 733/733 persona tests pass.

- **Drawer-switch checkpoint re-activation** (2026-06-12, merge `aed52c3`, branch claude/condescending-turing-03821f; closes the Known Issue logged by the split-delivery ship): `switchConversation` in `useChat.ts` cleared `activeCheckpoint` on switch but never re-derived it from the loaded messages, so picking a conversation from the drawer whose last message was a pending checkpoint rendered the proposal as an inert historical Plate — no "Tap to review," no confirm/reject until a full reload. Fix wires the drawer path to the same shared `pendingCheckpointFromMessages` helper the resume and reload paths already use, so all three conversation-load paths re-activate identically (3 lines + comments). +1 regression test pinning the drawer-row shape (extra `channel` column). **Tests/build:** 1145/1145, clean build. Commit `aed52c3`.

- **Checkpoint split delivery — the lead-in ships before the entry composes** (2026-06-12, merge `44bf2e6`, branch claude/festive-jackson-771a30; closes held item D from the 2026-06-03 checkpoint-quality batch): Off the founder's report of 1s-vs-15s response variance. Diagnosis: a checkpoint turn rendered NOTHING until the blocking Opus composition call finished — both visible artifacts (acknowledgment bubble + trigger card) are composition outputs, so the user stared at the typing indicator for reply generation + composition (~24s observed live; `composition_latency` read first as the held item required: 10,450ms on the verification run, confirming compose is the dominant wait). **Fix:** when the detector + gates pass, new `splitCheckpointLeadIn` (`call-persona.ts`; same `findCheckpointTransition` boundary as the detector and the suppression stripper — one transition contract, three consumers) carves the response at the transition line. The lead-in is saved as its own row and emitted immediately as a `message_complete` carrying a new `composing: true` flag; the remainder (transition line + entry prose) stays on the checkpoint row. The client (`useChat.ts` `composingCheckpoint` state → `MobileSession` typing-indicator gate) keeps the indicator pulsing under the lead-in bubble until the acknowledgment + card events land. Rows are backdated lead-in (−2s) → ack (−1s) → card, so a time-ordered reload reads exactly like the live stream and the card row stays last (pending re-activation on reload unaffected). Composition FAILURE after a shipped lead-in deletes the orphaned remainder row (the unbacked "I want to put something in your Manual" promise) and emits nothing more — the lead-in is exactly the text the strip path would have kept. No lead-in (model led with the transition) → single-row behavior unchanged; gate-fail strip is unchanged and runs before any emission (observed working live when an early proposal was suppressed at depth=behavior). Cost label: no new model calls, no new tables — one extra `messages` row + one UPDATE per checkpoint turn. **Browser-verified live** (devtest, fresh conversation driven to a real checkpoint): lead-in landed at +13s with the indicator still pulsing, ack + card +10.7s later; DB row order, reload rendering, and byte-identical non-checkpoint turns all confirmed. **Tests/build:** 1144/1144 (5 new `splitCheckpointLeadIn` tests incl. a shared-boundary contract test against `stripCheckpointFromText`); clean build. **Remaining latency lever (untouched):** Opus 4.7 on every conversational turn — the sharper-mirror WATCH decision is still open. Commit `28b9f7a`; merge `44bf2e6`.

- **Checkpoint trigger card promoted to the Plate** (2026-06-12, branch claude/interesting-grothendieck-9027d8, follow-on to the entry below): The Plate redesign below landed on the confirm *overlay*, but in the chat the checkpoint still appeared as a compact teaser (10px eyebrow, 17px Spectral-italic headline, "Tap to review"), so it didn't read as the Plate until tapped — the founder reported "I don't see the updated checkpoint" looking at the in-chat card. (Root cause of the not-seeing was twofold and worth recording: (1) the trigger ≠ the overlay — the Plate was one tap away; (2) the **PWA service worker** was serving the cached old app even though `6478dfc` had deployed to Production — reproduced live here: the throwaway preview page kept rendering the stale route until the SW was unregistered + caches cleared. Production fix for users: hard-refresh twice / quit-reopen the installed app.) Fix: the in-chat trigger card (`MobileSession` pending-checkpoint branch) now mirrors the overlay Plate — centered brass eyebrow, a **25px Instrument Serif display headline**, the brass rule, and a centered "Tap to review →", over the inset frame already on `.checkpoint-trigger-plate`. Tapping still opens the overlay for the full entry + Add/Edit/Discard. Net −13 lines (the teaser markup simplified). **Verified** by mounting the trigger markup in a throwaway page and screenshotting the Plate (then deleted). **Tests/build:** clean `next build`, 1139/1139 pass, tsc + eslint clean; clean fast-forward to origin/main. **Commit** `a155995`.

- **Checkpoint confirm overlay rebuilt as the letterpress Plate** (2026-06-12, branch claude/interesting-grothendieck-9027d8, follow-on to the checkpoint re-skin below): The previous pass only re-skinned the checkpoint (warm tab → brass eyebrow); the founder couldn't tell it had changed (and separately: "merged to main" ≠ deployed — the Vercel deploy *had* gone out, but the PWA service-worker cache + the checkpoint being a live-only moment hid it). So `CheckpointOverlay` is rebuilt into the demo's distinctive **Plate**: a letterpress **inset hairline frame** set inside the module edge, a **centered brass layer eyebrow**, a **big Instrument Serif display headline** (was 22px Spectral, now 30px `--font-serif`), and a brass rule under the headline. Confirming now plays a **"press"** — `handleConfirm` strikes the rule solid and dips the plate (`scale(0.985)`, the entrance `cpModuleIn` lost its `forwards` so the inline transform can take over) for 280ms before handing off to the existing composing → confirmed phases; **reduced motion commits immediately**. The in-chat **trigger card** (`.checkpoint-trigger-plate`) gets the matching inset frame via a `::before`. **All overlay logic preserved** — the 6 source-contract tests still pin the `onCloseRef` auto-close, the Escape-in-every-phase handler, the `[confirmStatus, open]` / `[open]` effect deps, and the body-scroll-lock. **Verified in-browser** via a throwaway harness page (mounted the overlay with a mock checkpoint, screenshotted the resting Plate + the full press→confirmed flow, then deleted the page) — the live in-chat trigger frame wasn't screenshotted (needs a real pending checkpoint; it's a one-line CSS add). **Tests/build:** clean `next build`, **1139/1139 pass** (incl. `CheckpointOverlay.test.ts`), tsc + eslint clean; clean fast-forward to origin/main. **Commit** `6478dfc`.

- **Manual rebuilt to the editorial layout + checkpoint re-skinned to Slate & Brass** (2026-06-11, branch claude/interesting-grothendieck-9027d8, follow-on to the Slate & Brass ship below): The Manual page is rebuilt from the per-layer plate + warm walnut chapter-tab ("TabPip") to the demo's editorial layout — each layer is a **brass Roman-numeral + caps name + hairline rule** header, and each entry is **its own card** (accordion), instead of rows inside one plate. `LayerHeader` (numeral/name/rule + info popover), `PopulatedLayer` (header + card stack, no plate), `EntryItem` (each a card; display-serif title; dotted sibling-divider removed), and `EmptyLayer` (header + light doorway card) were rebuilt; all behaviors preserved (expand/collapse, contentEditable edit + save, explore-further, the layer info popover, empty-layer tap-to-explore, and **read-only** — `AdminManualView` renders the same components for the admin "view a user's Manual" pane). Content/wording untouched. The rebuild is **structural so it applies in both themes** — dark renders the new layout with its dark colors (verified clean), which is wider than "don't touch dark" but unavoidable without forking the components. **Checkpoint:** the in-chat trigger card drops the warm TabPip for a **brass layer eyebrow** (`formatLayerEyebrow`, matching the Manual headers + the confirm overlay), and `CheckpointOverlay` (already fully token-driven) had its one hardcoded warm literal (`rgba(170,120,82,0.30)`) swapped to `--session-walnut-meta`. The trigger→modal framework is kept (the demo's inline-confirm was a static-mock limitation; the modal carries guest→account conversion + the edit/refine flow). **Cleanup:** removing the tab from both surfaces left `TabPip.tsx` fully unused → **deleted** (with its import + a redundant local removed from `MobileSession`); an unused `React` import in `PopulatedLayer` was also removed. Net −162 lines. **Tests/build:** clean `next build`, **1139/1139 pass** (incl. `CheckpointOverlay.test.ts`), tsc + eslint clean; clean fast-forward to origin/main (no parallel divergence this time). **Browser-verified** (devtest, light + dark): the Manual layout on desktop + mobile, expand/edit/popover, dark-mode parity, no console errors. **Not visually captured:** a live pending-checkpoint trigger (a dev-simulate run didn't surface one quickly; the change is a low-risk token re-skin) and the admin read-only Manual pane (build/test-verified only). **Known remaining warm spot:** the desktop vitrine marketing frame. **Commit** `32c1de3`.

- **Slate & Brass theme — the rebrand's first surface (chat + sidebar); light is now the default** (2026-06-11, branch claude/interesting-grothendieck-9027d8): The light theme is re-palettized to **Slate & Brass** — cool greige stone ground (`#E8E6DF`), graphite ink (`#1C1B18`), aged-brass accent (the `--session-walnut*` token family carries brass values in light; the names stay theme-agnostic, dark still binds them to walnut), with seamless off-screen-anchored surface gradients (centered pools anchored above/below the frame so no elliptical boundary ever shows). **Light is now the default** — the FOUC script, SSR `data-theme`, and `useTheme` all flip their fallback to light; `setTheme` now persists "system" explicitly so follow-OS stays distinguishable from the no-choice default (theme-color updated to `#E8E6DF`). Because the app is token-driven, the whole app re-skins from the one token block; **chat + sidebar are the hand-polished surfaces this pass** — Jove bubbles run squarer (10px) with a brass top-rule and the brass JOVE label, the user bubble is a defined greige card (`#ECE9E0→#E4E0D5`, graphite hairline), the composer is stone glass, the desktop sidebar + mobile drawer inherit brass automatically (no component rewrite — drawer widened to 88% / 420px cap), and the chat-scroll bottom feather widened 4px→28px so messages dissolve above the composer instead of hitting a hard edge. **Dark mode is untouched** — verified byte-identical (slate Jove, sage persona, 16px radius); two new tokens (`--session-jove-rule`, `--session-bubble-radius`) default to dark-preserving values (transparent rule, 16px). The in-chat checkpoint's hardcoded warm-amber halo was recolored to brass so it's on-brand; its full structural redesign (the demo's letterpress Plate + press-confirm) rides with the **Manual + checkpoint pass (next)** since the checkpoint shares components with the Manual. Design exploration committed under `design-demos/` (five initial directions → Kernel refinements → five chat/color palettes; Slate & Brass chosen). **Tests/build:** 1139/1139 pass, clean `next build`, both re-run on the integrated state after a clean **rebase onto origin/main** (11 parallel-session commits — soak 6/7/8, hover-scrollbars, session titling — zero conflicts) and a **fast-forward push** that never touched the parallel session's dirty local checkout. **Browser-verified** (devtest, light theme, real dev server): chat bubbles, sidebar, the wider drawer, dark-mode parity, and no console errors. **Commits** `ae0edc6` (theme), `ecbc750` (demos).

- **Hover-reveal scrollbars on desktop** (2026-06-10): scrollbar thumbs are transparent until the pointer is over the scroll area (`.mw-scroll` in globals.css, applied to conversation/manual/settings/crisis/login/sidebar scrollers) — the claude.ai pattern; wheel scrolling implies hover so the bar shows exactly while scrolling, pure CSS, fine-pointer devices only, mobile native bars untouched.

- **Session titles follow the user, not Jove's opener** (2026-06-10, one-line prompt fix in `generate-summary.ts`): with a long returning-user opener and short replies, Haiku titled sessions after Jove's framing (e.g. "Testing norm-matching pattern…" when the user wanted to talk about dogs). The title instruction now anchors on the user's side of the transcript and follows the user when they steer away from the opener. Existing titles regenerate on each session-summary pass. Follow-up (commit `90cc01b`): the title now also generates EARLY — useChat fires the same summary endpoint once after the user's 3rd message (per-conversation ref guard + existing-title check), so the always-visible desktop sidebar names the session mid-conversation instead of after the 30-min stale pass; verified live ("Phone anxiety blocking dental care" appeared in-session). Same commit fixes a desktop-shell bug: the sidebar refresh effect depended on unmemoized `refreshConversations` and looped /api/conversations on every render — deps now `[isDesktop]` only (comment in MainApp marks it load-bearing).

- **Desktop login shell + dark/light contrast pass** (2026-06-10, follow-up to the desktop shell, commit `39c8d45`): `/login` at >=1030px now renders `AuthShell` — the desktop room treatment without the sidebar (same 56px header spec with the wordmark, full-bleed welcome gradient, centered form via `LoginScreen showTopBar={false}`, colophon footer carrying the statement + Privacy/Terms/version) — so the login -> app threshold keeps the chrome still and just adds the sidebar; below 1030px the phone-frame vitrine is unchanged. Plus a WCAG contrast pass on the tokens (computed on composited colors, not eyeballed): dark `walnut-meta` moved to the brightened walnut family (2.8 -> 5.5 — the darker hue maxes at 3.4 on the dark ground at any alpha), dark `ink-faded` .45 -> .55 (3.7 -> 5.0; sidebar dates, header meta, rail icons — the founder's "squinting" report), dark `ink-ghost` .38 -> .50, light `ink-faded` `#7A5E40` -> `#6B4E32` (3.8 -> 4.9), light `ink-ghost` .42 -> .55, light `persona` `#5E7A4F` -> `#4E6346` (JOVE label 3.6 -> 5.0). Light `ink-mid` already passed (4.5) and was left alone. Body text was AAA (10.7-16.7) in both themes before and after — the pass raised the whisper tier's floor without flattening the hierarchy. Sidebar "A private manual" eyebrow removed (founder call).

- **Desktop shell — a true desktop layout for the authenticated app at >=1030px** (2026-06-10, merge `5fc94ca`, branch claude/vigorous-brown-8e497d; ADR-046): The app stops rendering as a 430px phone frame on wide screens. New chrome only — `DesktopShell` (sidebar + room header) consumes the SAME four `activeView` panels MobileLayout renders, so there is no new state model and no new data path; the Manual replaces the conversation as a full center view, exactly like the existing mobile view-swap. `DesktopSidebar` is fed the same props as SessionDrawer (which was already fully prop-driven) and hosts the theme toggle, legal links, and the admin DevToolsPanel (its window-event bridge is location-independent, no rewiring). The four mobile views gained a `showTopBar` prop (default true — mobile is byte-identical); BetaFeedbackButton gained an `inline` variant for the header. Breakpoint is 1030px via a SSR-safe `useIsDesktop` matchMedia hook folded into the existing pre-paint gate (no hydration flash), aligned to the vitrine's existing wide-desktop boundary. Dead code removed with it: the vitrine's now-unreachable DevToolsPanel slot + `.mw-dev-tools` CSS. `docs/rules.md` Dead Features amended with founder sign-off ("Desktop layout" reintroduced deliberately; stale "Theme toggle" entry removed). Design exploration lives in `mockups/desktop-*.html` (five rounds; option 5 "the Rail," Claude-style, was chosen) — and `mockups/` is now excluded from the app typecheck (`tsconfig.json`), which also unblocks builds in checkouts carrying untracked side projects there. Founder-approved scope cuts: no "new since last view" badge (no data source — entry count only), checkpoint overlay keeps dimming the full window, 431-1029px keeps the phone vitrine. Verified live: view swap, rail collapse (persisted via `mw_sidebar_collapsed`), theme cycle, both themes, 1029/1030 boundary, build + full suite. One watch item: under viewport EMULATION, growing the window across 1030px needed a reload to switch shells (shrinking switched live; fresh loads correct on both sides) — sanity-check a real window drag.

- **Login flow streamlined — splash step deleted, `/login` opens on the form** (2026-06-10, claude/stupefied-thompson-4f4551 branch): "Sign in" on the marketing landing went landing → EntryScreen (a second branded splash whose only action was another "Sign in" button, with a 400ms cross-fade) → LoginScreen. **EntryScreen + OnboardingFlow deleted** (−357 net lines; OnboardingFlow existed only to fade between those two views) — `/login/page.tsx` now renders `LoginScreen` directly inside DesktopVitrine, back button → `/`. Landing "Sign in" anchor swapped for a `next/link` Link so `/login` prefetches while the visitor reads the page (form renders instantly on click). Also removed: dead `.mw-mobile-only` CSS (only consumer was EntryScreen's legal footer), the EntryScreen copy tests, two stale comments (DesktopVitrine, PostLoginOnboarding). Middleware paths unchanged: logged-in users still skip `/` + `/login` → `/app`; logged-out users bounced from protected routes now land on the form, not the splash. **Considered and rejected** (complexity gate): a login modal on the landing page (a permanent second auth surface + auth bundle shipped to every marketing visitor, while `/login` must exist anyway as the middleware bounce target / reset-password return) and `?next=` deep-link preservation after the login bounce (no user destination other than `/app` exists today — revisit when shared Manual pages ship). Tests 1139/1139, clean build. Not browser-verified in-session (worktree-anchored preview); the change is route rewiring fully exercised by the build. Commit `a0fc3ba`.

- **Voice rebuild Phases 0–3a — the REBUILT voice is LIVE** (2026-06-10, merge `9af5641`, branch claude/cranky-neumann-2d0f98; full plan `docs/voice-rebuild-proposal.md`): The three-tier ~130-rule voice is replaced on both live paths (app `call-persona` + SMS `persona-bridge`, via `buildPromptOptionsFromContext`) by **CHARACTER + LIMITS + MECHANICS** — Jeff's redlined character text, five hard limits, and the entry-mechanics contract — at the top of `voice-scaffold.ts`, plus a **trimmed Tier 3** (checkpoints / first-checkpoint / adapting-short-answers / readiness-gate blocks excluded — MECHANICS replaces them; openers, intake/upload modes, post-confirm templates, clinical/referral/fabrication guards kept). Voice footprint ~2.2k tokens vs ~12.7k legacy (−82%). **Rollback is one word:** `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT = "legacy"` in `config.ts` — the legacy arrays stay in the tree until the Phase-3b cleanup. **Validated by a 10-conversation lab A/B** (`scripts/voice-ab.ts`, transcripts in `scripts/transcripts/voice-ab/`): rebuilt beat legacy on every engaged scenario; the recoded `pattern_engaged` gate verified (a sharpening correction now counts as engagement; the polite-complier persona got 1 earned checkpoint vs legacy's 2). **Two red-line leaks found and fixed before ship:** (1) crisis — the A/B exposed that `CRISIS_PHRASES` failed the doctrine's own canonical phrase ("I don't see the point anymore" — a live production bug in BOTH voices); detector phrase added + LIMIT 2 gained non-negotiable lexical triggers; re-run verified 988+741741 land on the signal turn. (2) prescription — the minimal prompt broke under three "just tell me what to do" asks (verbatim scripts, "Before. Not during."); LIMIT 2 gained the no-scripts / no-scheduling hard stop; re-run verified the spine holds while naming the question-under-the-question. Also shipped: confirm `naturalReply` no longer fabricates "That resonates" (now neutral "I saved that to my Manual." — it fires on EVERY confirm/reload, not legacy rows; comment corrected); sim-user correction shapes parse as refine; admin prompt-architecture viewer renders the live rebuilt prompt (`SECTION_DEFS` "Live Voice ·" entries + `LIVE_VOICE_VARIANT` pass-through); AT-3a supersede banners stamped on CLAUDE.md Prompt Structure + rules.md Voice Principles + `/evaluate` quality framework (now graded against CHARACTER+LIMITS for new transcripts) per the guidance-regression sweep. **Tests:** builder default stays legacy so the 1144 pre-existing tests pass unchanged; 1152/1152 on merged main. **Now in Phase-3a soak:** read real transcripts (watch withdrawal, checkpoint quality under minimal instructions, ND fit); Phase 3b (delete legacy arrays, migrate taste-pin tests, collapse doc restatements) waits for the soak ± the retention-holdout decision. Persona deltas are inert on the rebuilt path (composeTier2 not called) — ND handling returns as a subordinate layer in Phase 5.

- Fourth feature gate — `extraction_brief` (voice-only mode) (2026-06-04, claude/great-elion-fe339a branch): Follow-on to the three-gate ship below, added on founder request after walking through what still steers Jove with the first three off. `extraction_brief` OFF puts Jove in **voice-only mode**: the background Sonnet extraction call is skipped (web `call-persona.ts` + SMS `persona-bridge.ts`) AND — the load-bearing part — the stored `extraction_state` is **nulled at the source in `loadConversationContext`**, so no brief renders and the whole pipeline sees no analysis. **Senior-engineer review caught a real defect in the first cut and it was fixed before ship:** the first version only cleared the *rendered brief* and skipped the *new* call, leaving the previously-stored `extraction_state` intact — so a returning user (or anyone past turn 1) kept a frozen non-null state that the checkpoint material-quality gate could still pass, firing an entry composed from *stale* analysis while the operator believed they were in voice-only mode. Nulling the state at the source makes the documented "checkpoints fail closed with extraction off" actually true, stops a stale admin `extraction_snapshot` for free, and makes the gate structurally symmetric with the `checkpoints` gate (both zero everything downstream). DB row untouched → flipping back ON restores real state on the next turn (one-turn catch-up, same as the normal extraction lag). Defaults ON, fails open to ON, production unchanged until flipped. Seed row in **append-only migration `20260604000003`** (run after `0002`); the admin PATCH also upserts the row, so the switch works even before the migration. **Tests/build.** 1152/1152 pass (gate tests extended to the 4th key); clean `next build` (pre-commit hook); typecheck clean. **Browser-verified** (devtest, `/admin?section=health`): the 4th switch "Extraction analysis" renders and reads ON via the per-key fail-open default (its row didn't exist yet), no console errors. **Commit** `233ef29`.
- Runtime feature gates to isolate the core voice+extraction loop (2026-06-04, claude/great-elion-fe339a branch): Off a founder request to see what's actually built into Jove and switch features off to test the core loop in isolation. A two-workflow audit (8-agent feature inventory → 8-agent Tier-3 gate analysis with adversarial verification) inventoried 33 deduped features and found the minimal gate set. **Built: three global runtime switches** — `persona_deltas`, `conversation_modes`, `checkpoints` — each sitting at a chokepoint where one boolean collapses a whole subsystem, so 3 flags cover 5 subsystems (chosen over the founder's original "boolean per feature," per removal-first). Backed by a new `feature_gates` table (one row per gate; RLS enabled, no policies → service-role-only, same posture as `messaging_events`/ADR-037). `getFeatureGates` (`src/lib/persona/feature-gates.ts`) is read **once per turn inside `loadConversationContext`, folded into its existing `Promise.all`** so there's no added round-trip, and **fails open to all-ON** on any error/missing table. When ON, every clamp is a no-op (production byte-identical). When OFF: `persona_deltas`→`personaModes` clamped to `['general']` (base voice only); `conversation_modes`→`mode` clamped to `'situation'` (no guided-intake/upload branching; the stored `conversations.mode` is never written, so flipping back ON fully restores); `checkpoints`→`checkpointApproaching`+`priorCheckpointSuppressed` zeroed and `checkpointsEnabled=false` skips detection on **both** the web path (`call-persona.ts`) and SMS path (`persona-bridge.ts`). **Tier 3 needs no gating** (verified, 0/5 adversarial refutes): the conversation_modes + checkpoints gates already neutralize every mode/checkpoint Tier-3 block; the rest is always-on safety/competence (clinical-and-tail, adapting-short-answers — must stay) or state-derived-inert (returning-user / post-action / readiness-gate can't fire in the stripped config). **Admin:** writable switches in the Health section (`FeatureGatesPanel`), `GET`/`PATCH /api/admin/feature-gates` (`requireAdmin`, key-validated). **Senior-engineer review: SHIP** — hot path can't break (every failure caught; can't reject the batch), fail-open airtight, clamps only degrade (no crash/corruption), no duplication (genuinely the first global runtime-config table). One known behavior (intentional, left as-is): the confirm route doesn't read the gate, so a checkpoint card already on a user's screen can still be confirmed after `checkpoints` is flipped off — honest behavior, idempotent write, blast radius is one already-offered entry during a debug window. Documented deletion condition (migration header + module doc + [[project_feature_gates]] memory): drop the table + read sites once the core loop is validated. **Tests/build.** 1152/1152 pass (+8 gate tests); clean `next build`; typecheck clean; design-tokens admin check green. **Browser-verified** (logged in as devtest, `/admin?section=health`): panel renders all three switches ON, `GET` returns the all-ON fail-open default (table not yet created), auth guard 403s a non-admin caller. **Migration `20260604000002_feature_gates.sql` is NOT yet applied — run by hand; until then the switches show all-ON and can't be flipped (see Not Yet Functional).** **Commit** `9cb5148` (fast-forward merge to main).
- Pattern-altitude headlines + post-rejection theme-not-title fix + model reconcile + gate dedup (2026-06-04, claude/laughing-napier-3a6cd2 branch): Off two live transcripts the founder pasted into `/evaluate`. **(1) Checkpoint altitude** (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`): a checkpoint narrowed a cross-context life-pattern down to its triggering scene — headline "I Can Mistake My Son's Quiet for Wiring" when the user had described reading his OWN quiet that way as a kid and still not talking to people. The composer's trigger-format rule (`:185`, "I [verb] when [trigger]") was outranking ALTITUDE FIRST (`:183`). Sharpened the altitude clause (name the pattern when it reaches past the one scene — the user's past, other people, themselves) and added a worked failing example using that transcript (right altitude: "I Can Read Quiet as Wiring and Stay on My Side of the Door"). Investigated the "Can" hedge — **NOT a bug**: `distinct_contexts` counts narrated scenes (`extraction.ts:204`); one scene narrated = hedge correct, so the fix is purely altitude (hedge stays, altitude rises). No extraction change. Live-behavior change — judge via `/evaluate` on a fresh checkpoint. **(2) Post-rejection wrong-title bug** (`system-prompt.ts` POST-REJECTION block): after a user discarded a card titled "I Dig In Before They Finish Saying It," Jove's next line referenced "The Defense Fires Before the Charge" — a title-cased phrase the user never saw, confabulated from the conversation theme. Senior-engineer trace: the displayed headline lives only in `messages.checkpoint_meta` (never in any `content` field the next turn loads), so the block's "reference the entry" instruction made the model invent one. Per founder, referencing the *theme* is fine; framing it as a *named card* is the bug — so it's a prompt-only fix (no title injection / no context-pipeline change): the block now tells Jove to name the substance in plain conversational language and forbids title-casing / "the [Name] one didn't land" framing, with the exact failure as a counter-example. Added a regression test (the prior tests only checked the block rendered, never that it forbids title-framing). **(3) Model reconcile** (`config.ts`): `COMPOSITION_MODEL` `claude-opus-4-6` → `claude-opus-4-7` to match `PERSONA_MODEL` — unintentional version drift; entries are the core artifact and now run the same Opus tier as the reply (fires only on checkpoint turns, small cost delta). **(4) Gate dedup** (`call-persona.ts`): the two post-stream `applyCheckpointGates` calls (admin-overlay snapshot + fire/strip decision) used identical inputs — collapsed to one hoisted pure call reused by both, removing the drift trap that caused the 2026-06-03 suppression incident. **Tests/build.** 1143/1143 pass; clean `next build`; typecheck clean (src). Shipped via direct fast-forward to `origin/main` without touching a parallel session's in-progress worktree (no file overlap). **Commits** `7148a8e`, `cc4a053`.
- `/insights` added to admin Agents & Skills; CLAUDE.md Migrations rule (2026-06-04, claude/distracted-shannon-66b982 branch): Two small changes. **(1)** Added an `Insights` entry to `REFERENCE_CAPABILITIES` in `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts` so the built-in Claude Code `/insights` command (usage-report generator — what's working, friction patterns, suggestions) shows on the admin Agents & Skills page (`/admin/skills`). Same pattern as Dynamic Workflows / frontend-design: the filesystem scan can't find built-in commands, so they're listed as curated founder-facing reminders. Renders under User skill · Installed · `/insights`. No new categories, no API/page change. **(2)** Added a `## Migrations` section to `CLAUDE.md`: for Supabase migrations, check for timestamp collisions with parallel branches and verify prod constraints before shipping; there's no automated migration pipeline, so flag migration steps for the user to run by hand. **Tests/build.** 1143/1143 pass; clean `next build`; typecheck clean (two pre-existing `docs-mtime.json` errors in the worktree, unrelated). **Browser-verified on this branch** (`/admin/skills`, logged in as devtest): Insights card renders in the User skill group with the `/insights` chip. **Commit** `0b7a995`; merge `5e23cf0`.
- New `/doc-audit` skill — multi-agent documentation freshness audit (2026-06-04, claude/happy-swartz-e35c3f branch): Added `.claude/skills/doc-audit/SKILL.md`, a read-only audit skill in the `/overbuild-check` + `/evaluate` family. On `/doc-audit` it fans out one verification agent per documentation surface — (1) the foundational docs (`intent`/`system`/`rules`/`decisions`/`state`/`CLAUDE.md`), (2) the skills/agents/commands under `.claude/`, and (3) the admin "how it works" pages (`how-it-works`/`prompt-architecture`/`extraction-map`/`schema-map`/`skills`/`docs` `page.tsx`, flagged as the highest-drift surface since they hardcode token counts, model IDs, the prompt tier structure, and the schema). Each agent reads its doc **and the actual code**, then buckets every concrete claim as ✅ Current / ⚠️ Stale / 🔴 Broken with `file:line` evidence on both sides, ranks ⚠️/🔴 by blast radius, and recommends a single best fix per finding. Discipline carried over from overbuild-check: verify the contradiction in code before calling a claim stale; never assert from memory. Guardrails baked in — read-only until Jeff approves; if a doc encodes a decision (ADR/Tier-1 rule) and the *code* violates it, the finding is flagged as a possible **code bug, not a doc to "fix"**; surfaces are discovered by globbing at run time (no hardcoded list); the reference/audits archives are out of scope; adds nothing to the product (on-demand audit, zero running cost). Scopeable: `/doc-audit admin` or `/doc-audit docs/system.md`. **Surfaces automatically** on the admin Agents & Skills page (`/admin/skills`) via the existing filesystem scan in `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts` — verified the route's parse picks it up as project-skill · Built · `/doc-audit` with no page or API change. Internal dev tooling, not a user-facing feature. **Tests/build.** 1143/1143 pass; clean `next build` (markdown-only repo change — no source code touched). Branch rebased onto main's `9de33ff` (the overbuild-check rewrite landed mid-session from a parallel session; different files, clean rebase). **Commit** `f190f72`.
- Overbuild review cleanup + Removal-first prevention system (2026-06-04, claude/laughing-napier-3a6cd2 branch): Off a cold no-sunk-cost panel review (18-agent workflow) of the persona architecture the founder feared he'd overbuilt. Verdict: the moat (persistent/shareable Manual + safety guardrails) is lean and was never the thing that got loosened; the bloat is conversation-quality scaffolding. This ship is the prevention system + the verified-dead cuts; the bigger voice-trim is GATED on an `/evaluate` read of the now-Opus-every-turn build and not done here. **Prevention system.** New CLAUDE.md hard rule **"Removal-first / Complexity Gate"** (before adding any model call/table/flag/prompt rule: try removal first; declare what consumes the output, its running cost, what it overlaps, and its delete condition; for prompt content, only encode what a frontier model gets *wrong*). New **`/overbuild-check` skill** (`.claude/skills/overbuild-check/`) — a periodic deletion-only counter-ratchet with "verify before removing" and "schema drops are the one irreversible move" as hard rules. **Cuts.** (1) **Phase-0 shadow monitor removed in full** — `monitor.ts` + test, `scripts/replay-monitor.*`, the `/replay-monitor` skill, `fireBackgroundMonitor`, `MONITOR_MODEL`/`MONITOR_ENABLED`, all call sites, and the `monitor_reads` table (drop migration `20260604000000`). It was gated off in every env and read by nothing ("consumed by nothing" per its own admin copy), with a cost landmine (wired to Opus while labeled Haiku). Per-web-turn footprint: 3 potential model calls → **2 guaranteed**. (2) **Dead `NEXT PROMPT` block** removed from `EXTRACTION_SYSTEM` (generated a field gone from the type/schema/parser); extraction sections renumbered 5–12 → 5–11. (3) **Empty voice override slots** (`DEEPENING_ADDITIONS`/`WEAK_STRONG_EXAMPLES`, empty in all four personas) removed from the `VoiceModule` type, `composeTier2`, and the four `voice-*.ts` files — output-neutral (all 397 system-prompt/tier3 snapshot tests unchanged). (4) **`manual_changelog` table dropped** (zero app writers; drop migration `20260604000001` recreates `cleanup_stale_anonymous_users()` without the dead DELETE, then drops the table; removed the dev-reset DELETE + schema-map entry + prose). (5) **Single-call headlines** — removed `composeHeadline()`, the conditional second Opus call (max_tokens 64) that rewrote a title when `validateHeadline` failed; folded its sharp banned-subjects guard into the single composer so the title is right in one call; `validateHeadline` stays as a deterministic log-only smoke detector. **Deferred / not cut (the gate said so):** `Tier3Flags.checkpointApproaching` (panel called it a "dead field" — it's a redundant output copy of a load-bearing input of the same name; ~50 test refs, strict type contract — **skipped, not worth the churn**); the remaining log-only validators (`validateComposedEntry`/`validateResponseStructure`/`findUniversalToneViolations` + now `validateHeadline`), the 3× gate-eval dedup, and the model-version sprawl (reply opus-4-7 vs composition opus-4-6) — all open. **Roadmap.** ADR-045 records the monitor removal and **pauses the two-layer-engine roadmap** (parent ADR-044 draft + ADR-043 Decision 1 selector) as a candidate overbuild, decision deferred per founder ("I don't know — this is a risk of overbuilding"); ADR-043 Decisions 2 & 3 (live gate logic) untouched. `master.md` carries a dated SUPERSEDED banner so no agent treats the removed monitor/selector as current. **Tests/build.** 1143/1143 pass; clean `next build`; typecheck clean. Lesson logged: of the panel's flagged items the genuinely-dead ones cut clean, but ~half the "simplify" items needed the gate applied at execution time (2 of 4 "verified-dead" had entanglements; the headline-retry was reframed, not cut as proposed). **Commits** `0def548`, `4c3bca2`, `05cef7e`, `fa3d659`, `e7eb549`; merge `d5688bb`.
- Sharper-mirror NOW bucket — Opus conversational turn, 4x tokens, synthesis room, reassurance-fishing (2026-06-04, claude/jove-sharper-mirror branch): Off a delta analysis the founder requested — he pasted a claude.ai health/anxiety conversation he loved and asked why Jove doesn't feel like it. Multi-agent verdict: that conversation is Claude as a **directive analytical coach** (agenda, variable-isolation, pushback, tiered ACTION PLAN, pharmacology facts) on an Opus-class model with his whole health project in memory. Half is a posture Jove forbids on purpose (prescribe / clinical facts / draft artifacts / single-session closure — kept forbidden; "feels like therapy" is the kill signal). Half is closeable: model tier, token cap, no cross-session memory in the live turn, and a few un-fired craft moves. Core finding: **the non-directive thesis is sound; the current voice is too timid an expression of it** (commit-a-read / variable-isolation / pushback exist in the prompt but are outranked by "compress, one or two beats" + "restraint is a move," so Jove reads as withholding, and early turns feel like an interview because the commit-read rule won't fire until turn 3). Fix = sharper mirror, not a coach. **This ship is the NOW bucket.** (1) **`PERSONA_MODEL` `claude-sonnet-4-6` → `claude-opus-4-7`** (`config.ts`) — the conversational turn is the one the user feels and was the only major surface still on Sonnet (composition was already Opus 4.6, monitor Opus 4.7). **Opus on EVERY turn is a real cost/latency increase** (the turn fires on every message); reversible to Sonnet, can be gated to complex/synthesis turns later if cost bites. This is also the A/B for "how much of the gap was model vs craft." (2) **`PERSONA_MAX_TOKENS` 2048 → 8192** (4x, founder's call) so a synthesis turn (evidence → read → test) completes without truncating the evidence trail; dial down later. (3) **Voice Rule 4** ("compress, one or two beats") relaxed to **yield on synthesis turns** — Jove may take the room to lay out the full shape when pulling threads together / isolating what's tangled / landing a pattern; still one handoff, compression stays the default for ordinary turns. (4) **Reassurance-fishing detection** (founder explicitly asked; was absent): a "Naming reassurance-seeking" register example + a weak→strong pair + a `BANNED_PATTERNS` entry — when the user stacks successive detail fishing for a comforting read, Jove names the move and hands back the real read instead of supplying comfort (thesis-aligned: positions on truth, not on what to do). **Deferred (NEXT):** variable-isolation + numbered-decomposition as first-class voice moves; lower the commit-a-read threshold + mid-conversation thread-naming (kills the interview feel); a scannable CAPS/numbered register for users who signal a format preference. **LATER:** richer in-conversation Manual use + a latched within-session working hypothesis; then full cross-session memory + a between-session synthesis pass (the bulk of the "it knows me" feeling; roadmap). **Tests/build.** 1177/1177; clean `next build`; one system-prompt assertion updated for the reworded Rule 4. **Not browser-verified in-session** (conversational behavior; preview worktree-anchored) — judge via `/evaluate` on a NON-medical tangled discovery conversation: Jove commits a provisional read by turn 2 (not 4), variable-isolation fires, it catches reassurance-fishing at least once, synthesis lands in one turn. Negative controls: zero prescriptions, zero clinical content, one handoff per turn, and a test reader must NOT say "feels like therapy." **Commit** `01cbccb`; merge `8c07c8d`.
- New `applied-psychologist` review agent (2026-06-03, claude/hopeful-poitras-cd0818 branch): Added `.claude/agents/applied-psychologist.md`, a mechanism-level review agent for Jove's conversation logic, modeled on `senior-engineer`. Two modes — **logic review** (default; reads prompts/engine/monitor/persona deltas and prescribes changes to produce a target effect like opening up or feeling seen) and **transcript review** (reads a session, cites turn numbers and the mechanism behind each felt-seen / withdrawal / rupture / transformational moment, and cross-checks against the live `monitor_reads` rows). Grounded in published research (Rogers/Kohut/Stern/Gendlin/Fosha, Safran-Muran rupture taxonomy, Bordin, Ryan & Deci, MI, Gollwitzer); anchored on `src/lib/persona/monitor.ts` as the executable alliance model. Read-only (Read/Grep/Glob/Bash/WebSearch/WebFetch); founder-ratified inline field notes, no autonomous memory; recommends persuasion techniques where they fit and flags ethical concerns alongside rather than refusing. **Surfaces automatically** on the admin Agents & Skills page (`/admin/skills`) via the existing filesystem scan in `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts` (scans `.claude/agents/*.md` at request time) — no page or API change needed; it is a committed project agent, so it does **not** go in `REFERENCE_CAPABILITIES`. Internal dev tooling, not a user-facing feature. **Tests/build.** 1168/1168 pass; clean `next build` (markdown-only repo change — no source code touched). **Commit** `5006bab`; merge `d20ed8e`.

- Checkpoint-quality batch — headline altitude, save-phrase, body question, cross-session (2026-06-03, claude/jove-frame-batch branch): Second pass off the conversation audit (now 5 transcripts), addressing the recurring "the checkpoint draws a specific conclusion, not the broad pattern" complaint, after a senior-engineer review reshaped the queue (several proposed composer prohibitions became examples/code instead). **A — headline names the user's pattern, not a narrowed proxy.** The headline validator (`validateHeadline`, `confirm-checkpoint.ts`) banned felt-state verbs ("lose myself," "disappear") outright, so the user's own truest phrase got rejected and the retry forced a narrow observable proxy ("I Scan Before Speaking" instead of "I Lose Myself…"). Added a mirror-exact-language carve-out: a banned verb is allowed when it appears verbatim in the user's own messages (call site now passes the user's text), still banned otherwise (vague/poetic titles stay out). Plus an ALTITUDE-FIRST composer rule (name the pattern the user described, in their words; altitude beats trigger-specificity when they conflict). `validateHeadline` was unexported with zero coverage (it gates every title) — now exported with 9 tests (baseline rules + carve-out + the user-said-it regression guard). **E — save-phrase salience.** Jove kept writing "I want to put that down" instead of the contract "I want to put something in your Manual," which silently fails to render the card; the phrase was already required + the slip already listed, so this just leads the checkpoint-delivery rules with the exact words and calls out the slip by name (the `detect-checkpoint` regex was deliberately NOT widened, per the review — it would false-fire on ordinary prose). **F — gentler body question** (founder framing): the autistic body-substitute rule drops the clipped "where in your body?" for "take a moment. How does that show up for you, physically or mentally? What's it like?" — physical OR mental, body is one anchor not a reflex. **B — cross-session connection without takeover** (`confirm-checkpoint.ts` composer): draw the connection to a prior entry in the user's voice when it genuinely connects, but the spine of THIS entry stays this conversation's pattern (founder wants cross-session references KEPT; what's curbed is the over-import that made a prior "scan engine" the backbone of a new entry). **Founder decisions:** C (file the entry by the user's lead material) CUT — "the starting point may not be the pattern; pull the best content." Two-question-marks enforcement also CUT (founder: more questions get the user to type more; the only real issue is the frame-smuggling either/or, already covered). **Held:** D (composing-state loading bubble) — the chat uses batch rendering and a checkpoint is one message (reflection + card), so the loading-bubble split is a streaming-protocol restructure in `useChat.ts`/`MobileSession.tsx` that needs a working preview to build safely; G (Moment field) — to discuss. **Tests/build.** 1177/1177; clean `next build`; system-prompt + confirm-checkpoint tests + tier-3 snapshot updated. **Not browser-verified in-session** (conversational behavior; preview worktree-anchored) — judge via `/evaluate` (does the title name the broad pattern in the user's words; does the body question feel gentler; does the save-card reliably render). **Commits** `f091172` (A), `6990a70` (E+F), `6426a84` (B); merge `70d7215`.
- Frame-fidelity batch — voice + composer + opener (2026-06-03, claude/jove-frame-batch branch): Follow-on to the authenticity audit, off four real transcripts where Jove substituted its own frame for the user's, mis-pitched entry altitude, or over-pushed body questions — pressure-tested by a senior-engineer pre-implementation review (which reshaped several items from new composer prohibitions into examples/code, to avoid re-stuffing the composer prompt the NOW block just trimmed). **Changes.** (a) **Voice-layer frame fidelity** (`voice-scaffold.ts`): Rule 15 gains a spin-vs-driver clause (seeing past the framing is not swapping the subject — the user's named driver stays theirs; a different driver must be built from their words and checked); Rule 5 gains a terse-user clause (fewer words = less evidence, don't supply the read); DEEPENING_OUTRO's either/or ban now names the worst case (the binary that smuggles Jove's own frame — "was it the ask, or you?"); a new WEAK_STRONG pair. (b) **New `SITUATION_OPENER`** (`situation-copy.ts`): discovery-first, situation-first, strengths-included — pulls a specific situation telling about something the user wants to understand about themselves, "what works as much as what gets in the way," and drops the therapy-register "explore the parts that aren't obvious / find the words." (c) **Composer frame exemplar** (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`): a worked substituted-frame-vs-user's-frame pair (the music transcript) in the examples-first block — teaches frame + layer + altitude fidelity by example rather than new prohibitions. (d) **Sensory-load somatic tokens** (`persona-pipeline.ts` `SOMATIC_WORD_PATTERNS`): drain / exhaust / nois* so the log-validator stops false-flagging entries whose anchor is the environment (the sound), not a body location. (e) **Composition latency measurement** (`call-persona.ts` + `log.ts`): new `composition_latency` log event (`duration_ms` + `manual_entry_count`) around the blocking Opus `composeManualEntry` call — the founder-reported "feels like a bug" delay after the acknowledgment bubble; measure before optimizing. (f) doc fix: stale "Sonnet" comments → Opus (`COMPOSITION_MODEL = claude-opus-4-6`). **Held (founder decisions / follow-ups) — see In-Flight:** the headline verb carve-out (blocked on `validateHeadline` having zero tests), the two-question-marks + transition-contract prompt reinforcements, the layer-fidelity hard rule (lead-vs-deeper is a product call), the composing-state UI (measure first), and the `voice-autistic.ts` body-default calibration. **Tests/build.** 1168/1168 pass; clean `next build`; opener test substring + tier-3 snapshot updated. **Not browser-verified in-session** (conversational behavior over live Anthropic calls; preview worktree-anchored) — judge via `/evaluate` on a real conversation (frame fidelity, the new opener, whether the entry stays in the user's frame/layer). **Commits** `1462f91`, `e37e14f`; merge `5d80d86`.
- Schema-map page — full reframe around "follow one user's words" (2026-06-03, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Ground-up rebuild of `/admin/schema-map`, the same diligence applied to extraction-map. Three evaluation agents (current-page audit, ground-truth reconstructed from all 30 migrations, sibling clarity patterns). **The organizing idea:** the database is one short spine the user's words travel — `profiles → conversations → messages → manual_entries` — and everything else watches or guards that path. **Changes:** (1) Tables reclassified by **family** + a **spine / satellite / deprecated** tag + an **access** tag (user-readable / backend-only / signup); dropped the "Layer 0–4" stage names (they collided with the Manual's five Layers — the third page to carry that bug). (2) New **front door** ("Follow one user's words," open by default): the spine, the working memory, and everything-else, each with a "show it below ↓" jump. (3) Walkthrough **re-sequenced as the lifecycle**: the spine → the working memory → the watchers → **who can see what** (a new RLS/security lens) → **what survives a delete** (the cascade contract) → by the numbers. Security and the delete-contract — the schema's genuinely unique content, with no analog on the sibling pages — get their own steps instead of being buried in per-table notes. (4) **Cards/detail** lead with plain meaning; columns + foreign keys hide behind a "show the columns" toggle; the glossary is demoted to a collapsed box. (5) Header retitled **"Where a user's data lives"** + cross-link to the extraction map (and a deep-dive link on the working-memory step). **Drift fixed** (the hand-curated page had gone stale vs. the migrations): added the missing **`beta_allowlist`** table (dead leftover, kept until prod login is verified); `phone_numbers` OTP columns renamed `verification_code`/`code_expires_at` → `otp_code`/`otp_expires_at` + noted `UNIQUE(user_id)`; surfaced `conversations.calibration_ratings` as known-dead + added `processing_sms`; added `messages.processing_text`/`sender_phone` and `waitlist.invited_at`; confirmed `monitor_reads` is Opus (`MONITOR_MODEL = claude-opus-4-7`) and dropped a stale line-number citation. Now **17 tables** (8 user-readable / 7 backend-only / 2 signup; 2 deprecated). Token-only styling (hex guard passes); counts derive from the data. **Tests/build.** Typecheck + design-tokens guard + clean `next build` (page 17.6 kB); full suite green via pre-commit hook. **NOT browser-verified in-session** (preview anchored to the main repo root, not this worktree) — substantial visual rebuild; pull main and eyeball `/admin/schema-map`. **Commit** `f225042`.
- Cross-link prompt-architecture ↔ extraction-map + tsc hygiene fix (2026-06-03, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): The extraction-map page is the deep dive behind one specific block on the prompt-architecture page — the "Extraction Brief" live-context block (the `sage_brief` Jove reads). Wired the two together contextually (both already sit in the admin nav rail; this is a topical deep-dive link, not generic nav): on `/admin/prompt-architecture`, the Extraction Brief block's detail panel gains a "Deep dive — every note this call writes →" link to `/admin/extraction-map`; on `/admin/extraction-map`, the header gains a backlink — "This is the deep dive behind the 'Extraction Brief' block in Jove's prompt — see it in context →". Plain `<a>` (neither page used next/link), token-only styling. **Also** fixed a tsc-only error the parallel checkpoint-loop fix (`5bc54f4`) left on main: it added `priorCheckpointSuppressed` as a required `ConversationContext` field but didn't update the `makeCtx` mock in `persona-pipeline.test.ts` — vitest (esbuild, no typecheck) and `next build` (skips test files) both stayed green, so only `tsc --noEmit` caught it; added the neutral `false` default. **Tests/build.** Typecheck now clean, design-tokens guard + clean `next build`, full suite green via pre-commit hook. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview worktree-anchored) — eyeball the deep-dive link on `/admin/prompt-architecture` (Extraction Brief block) and the backlink on `/admin/extraction-map`. **Commits** `b0d916a` (tsc fix), `db3827b` (cross-link).
- De-formulaic Jove conversation — NOW block of the authenticity audit (2026-06-03, claude/great-grothendieck-9884b9 branch): A multi-agent audit diagnosed why Jove's output felt formulaic / performative / shallow rather than producing recognition, then shipped the first-priority ("NOW") batch of fixes. Root causes addressed: (1) the per-turn extraction brief recomputed checkpoint-readiness every turn and handed Jove a "what's still missing" checklist, turning the whole conversation into a countdown to a deliverable; (2) the entry composer saw only the last 8 messages, so entries read as recap; (3) the emotional peaks were hard-coded strings; (4) heavy negative constraints flattened the voice. **Changes.** (a) **Depth-gated readiness brief** (`extraction.ts` `formatExtractionForPersona`): the "there's a real piece here" hint is withheld until conversation depth reaches the mechanism (why a pattern fires), first checkpoint included (founder decision: fewer, deeper, later); when not ready it points Jove DOWN to the live edge ("stay in it, you haven't reached why it fires") instead of a missing-items checklist; one soft gap line when deep-but-thin; silent during crisis. The hard checkpoint gate (`applyCheckpointGates`) is deliberately unchanged — this governs the soft per-turn hint only. The CHECKPOINTS Tier-3 block wording was aligned to the new brief. (b) **Session depth wired into the composer** (`confirm-checkpoint.ts` + `call-persona.ts`): `composeManualEntry` now receives `depth` / `sage_brief` / `current_thread`, so the entry is written from what the whole session built, not just the recent window — the mechanical fix for recap-shaped entries. (c) **Generated post-rejection line** (`system-prompt.ts`): dropped the verbatim "That entry didn't land. Was it off, or just not ready?" fixed string for a varied, entry-specific one-liner. (d) **Softened dash rule** (`voice-scaffold.ts`): from an absolute em-dash ban to default-periods with a single dash allowed in body prose for rhythm; the ban was flattening the voice into a monotone staccato. (e) **Examples-first composer prompt** (`confirm-checkpoint.ts`): exemplars moved ahead of the ~28 prohibitions, plus a strength exemplar (so strengths stop being bent into friction) and a worked recap→deeper pair for the load-bearing "go one level deeper" move. (f) **Phase 0 shadow monitor gated off** (`config.ts` + `persona-pipeline.ts`): new `MONITOR_ENABLED` env flag (default false); `fireBackgroundMonitor` early-returns when off — it ran Opus 4.7 on every web turn and nothing read the result back. Code path intact for offline `/replay-monitor`. **Deferred (NOT in this ship):** the dead `so_what` column drop (separate destructive migration) and the audit's NEXT/LATER backlog — both in In-Flight. **Tests/build.** 1168/1168 pass; clean `next build`; extraction + system-prompt tests rewritten for the depth-gated behavior; tier-3 snapshot refreshed. **Not browser-verified in-session** (the change is conversational behavior over live Anthropic calls, not a renderable UI surface; preview is also worktree-anchored) — judge it via `/evaluate` before/after on a real conversation (recognition test, voice variety across consecutive turns, turns-before-first-checkpoint should rise). **Commits** `3bbc862`, `6aef01c`, `dbacb01`; merge `a30f5ec`.
- Fix the checkpoint suppression doom-loop + ghost checkpoint (2026-06-03, claude/upbeat-diffie-d8e0b3 branch): A live conversation looped for six turns on a context-blind question the user couldn't parse ("What was happening right before that landed?"), then emitted entry prose with no card. Root cause, confirmed from the founder's own extraction dump: the extraction model emitted `checkpoint_gate.strongest_layer` as the **string** `"1"` while `language_bank[].layers` were numeric `[1]`. The Lock-1 charged-phrase-on-layer gate runs `layers.includes(strongest_layer)`, and `[1].includes("1") === false` — so the gate silently failed on ready material and **suppressed every checkpoint from turn 4 on**. Each suppression ran `stripCheckpointFromText`, which stapled a fixed canned question onto the saved turn; the model saw its own mouth saying nonsense next turn, tried to repair *and* re-proposed (the brief was ordering it to), got re-stapled — doom loop. Turn 14 escaped only because the model phrased the transition as "Here's what I want to put in your Manual" (no object word), which the detector regex doesn't match → no card, raw prose leaked. **Five fixes.** (1) **Root cause** — `mergeExtractionState` (`extraction.ts`) now coerces `strongest_layer` + every `language_bank[].layers` element to numbers at the parse boundary (`toLayerNumber`/`coerceLayerList`), with defense-in-depth `Number()` coercion at the two consumer sites (`validateMaterialQuality`, `deriveCheckpointApproaching`) so legacy DB rows can't re-trigger it. (2) **One transition contract** — deleted the separate, narrower `SUPPRESSION_PATTERN`; the stripper now slices at the same match the detector used via new `findCheckpointTransition` (`detect-checkpoint.ts`), closing a latent second ghost path (a transition the detector caught but the old suppressor couldn't strip, e.g. "write this up for your Manual"). (3) **Clean strip** — deleted the context-blind `SUPPRESSION_CONTINUATION`/`SUPPRESSION_FALLBACK_FULL` staples; suppression now keeps the model's genuine lead-in and only falls back to one neutral grounding directive when nothing usable precedes the line. (4) **Loop circuit-breaker** — a gate-suppressed turn tags `messages.metadata.checkpoint_suppressed`; next turn `loadConversationContext` surfaces `priorCheckpointSuppressed`, which holds the checkpoint-proposal instructions for one turn and renders a short POST-SUPPRESSION Tier-3 block ("don't re-propose, get concrete ground first"). (5) **Honest overlay** — the admin ExtractionPanel "Gate met" was a looser inline formula that read "yes" while the engine suppressed; it now shows the real `applyCheckpointGates` verdict + held-reason, frozen server-side onto the snapshot (`gate_eval`) where `isFirstCheckpoint`/`turnCount` exist. **Tests/build.** +new guards: string-layer merge coercion (4), the incident reproduction at the gate (`validateMaterialQuality` passes with string `"1"`), unified-contract strip incl. the "write up for" variant, `findCheckpointTransition` detector-agreement, and the tier3 domain extended for the new flag; affected persona suites 580/580; `npm run build` + design-tokens clean. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview is worktree-anchored; the `checkpoint_suppressed` metadata + overlay land at runtime) — confirm a ready conversation now fires the card, and the overlay shows held-reasons, in prod. Senior-engineer reviewed the diagnosis + design. **Commit** `5bc54f4`.
- Extraction-map page — full reframe around "one call, two jobs" (2026-06-03, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Ground-up rebuild of `/admin/extraction-map` after founder feedback that it was still unclear what the page is, what each section does, and how to walk it. Driven by three parallel evaluation agents (current-page audit, ground-truth mental model verified against the real code, sibling prompt-architecture clarity patterns). **The organizing idea:** a background "extraction" call keeps a case file that does exactly two jobs — it **briefs Jove** (soft prose woven into the next prompt; shapes how Jove sounds; advisory) and it **gates checkpoints** (hard deterministic checks that decide whether Jove may propose a Manual entry; can silently veto Jove). A few raw notes — above all the language bank — feed both. **Changes:** (1) Fields reclassified by **job** (`gate` / `brief` / `material` / `signal`) instead of by JSON category; each carries a soft/hard tone driving color + tags. (2) New **front door** ("One call, two jobs," open by default): the two jobs in plain English + a language-bank callout, each with a "show it below ↓" button that drives the stepper. (3) Walkthrough **re-sequenced as the lifecycle** — the call (one-beat-behind) → the case file → Job 1 briefs Jove → Job 2 gates checkpoints → the raw material → by the numbers; a new `LifecycleStrip` visualizes the parallel, one-turn-lag flow with the court-reporter framing. (4) **Field cards** lead with the plain-language meaning, code path demoted to small print; `FieldDetail` leads with meaning + a job / load-bearing tag and hides the storage path + `file:line` readers behind a "show the code" toggle. (5) Header retitled **"What Jove tracks between turns"** with a plain subtitle + "use this page to" purpose line; dropped the standalone reading guide (terms now explained inline); by-the-numbers reworked to "notes by job." Token-only styling (hex guard passes); all counts derive from `FIELDS` so they can't drift — 20 notes / 13 load-bearing / 7 auxiliary. **Tests/build.** Typecheck + design-tokens guard + clean `next build` (page 12.4 kB); full suite green via pre-commit hook. **NOT browser-verified in-session** (preview is anchored to the main repo root, not this worktree) — this is a substantial visual rebuild (new front door, lifecycle strip, job-grouped diagram, reworked cards); pull main and eyeball `/admin/extraction-map` before relying on the layout. **Commit** `3465a4d`.
- Extraction-map page — remove dead `next_prompt` field + correct field truth table + naming rebuild (2026-06-03, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Three changes to the extraction system and its admin map (`/admin/extraction-map`), output of a teaching + audit walkthrough. **(1) Removed the dead `next_prompt` field.** It was write-only: the extraction call produced a 3–6 word placeholder hint, threaded it through the SSE `message_complete` payload (and the upload-opener event) to the client, but the ChatInput placeholder was hardcoded empty — nothing ever rendered it. Distinct from `sage_brief` (the live "BRIEF FOR YOUR NEXT RESPONSE" Jove actually reads), which is preserved. Removed from the `ExtractionState` interface + default, `EXTRACTION_SYSTEM` prompt (JSON example + the 3–6-word rule), the parse + fallback paths, both `call-persona` SSE payloads, the `sse-parser` type, and the extraction-map field card. Test mocks updated. **(2) Corrected the field truth table.** `depth` was tagged auxiliary/prunable but is a **hard checkpoint gate** (`persona-pipeline.ts:567` blocks the checkpoint until depth ≥ `feeling` first / `mechanism` subsequent) — moved into the gate group, marked load-bearing, added the missing pipeline reader. `has_charged_language` was tagged load-bearing but Lock 1 (ADR-043) replaced the boolean gate with a deterministic language-bank read — demoted to auxiliary (its own readers note already said "no longer a gate"). Header counts corrected to **20 fields / 13 load-bearing / 7 auxiliary** (was the self-contradictory "21 / 14 / 7"); dropped the false "charged-language anchor" gate claim and the now-removed "UI placeholder hint" from the card captions. **(3) Naming rebuild.** Dropped the "Layer 0–5" card-title prefixes — they collided with the Manual's five Layers (a reader couldn't tell "Layer 2" the page section from Layer 2 the Manual layer); titles are now plain ("The extraction call", "The brief", "The checkpoint gate", …). Renamed Stage → Step throughout (`Step` type, `STEPS`, `stepIndex`, `StepCaption`, the visible chip, aria-label) to match the prompt-architecture page, which already uses "Step"/"Stepper". Tightened the front-door subtitle into two sentences (what the call writes + the one-turn lag + how to read the cards), removing the same Layer collision; field count stays auto (`FIELDS.length`). **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 pass; typecheck + design-tokens guard + clean `next build` green. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview worktree-anchored) — eyeball `/admin/extraction-map`. **Commits** `5d8330d` (next_prompt removal), `f1519b0` (truth table), `bd49264` (naming).
