- Elevate Strengths to Layer 1 as "My Strengths" (2026-06-03, worktree-misc-bugs-6.3.26 branch): Reordered the five Manual layers so the strengths layer leads as **Layer 1**, retitled **"My Strengths"** with a value-forward description ("Understand the traits that make you exceptional and the environments that bring them out."). The other four shift down one, relative order preserved: Some of My Patterns (2), How I Process Things (3), What Helps (4), How I Show Up with People (5). Implementation renumbers the canonical layer ids in `src/lib/manual/layers.ts` (the single source of truth); every downstream surface — extraction catalog, manual-context compression, UI tiles/eyebrows/Roman numerals/accent colors — re-derives automatically. The strengths layer keeps its stable `where-strong` slug. Two hardcoded references corrected: `extraction.ts`'s per-layer "mechanism" note now resolves What Helps + Strengths **by slug** (reorder-proof), and a `system-prompt.ts` inline example bumped Layer 1→2. **DB migration** (`20260603000000_reorder_strengths_layer1.sql`) atomically remaps the `layer` int on both `manual_entries` and `manual_changelog` (5→1, 1→2, 2→3, 3→4, 4→5) with an inline rollback block — **runs against live entries on deploy**; no-op on a fresh DB. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 pass; 4 tier-3 prompt snapshots regenerated (diff was only the Layer 1→2 example); clean `next build`; typecheck clean. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview is worktree-anchored; Manual UI is data-driven from the canonical file and covered by unit tests) — eyeball the Manual tab after deploy to confirm My Strengths renders first. **Commits** `fd6146a`, merge `d22c510`.
- Prompt-architecture page — 3-part "The prompt" overview front door (2026-06-02, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Added a collapsible overview at the top of the diagram column on `/admin/prompt-architecture` — the plain-language front door the detailed diagram lacked. "The prompt — one call, every turn" with three parts (Instructions / About the user / The conversation), each expanding to its section headers + a one-line description (Tier 1/2/3; the four live-context blocks; the message parts). A "Show it in the diagram below" button per part jumps the stepper to that region (Instructions → tiers, About the user → live context, The conversation → alongside). Token-only styling (passes the design-tokens hex guard). Output of a teaching session that landed the mental model — one prompt, three parts — with the existing tier/cache diagram kept as the reference below. A standalone explainer **Remotion video** (~71s, `mockups/prompt-video/`) and several HTML design mocks (`mockups/prompt-architecture-*.html`) were produced alongside but are untracked, not part of the app. **Tests/build.** design-tokens + typecheck clean; clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview worktree-anchored) — eyeball the overview on `/admin/prompt-architecture`. **Commit** `b539fbc`.
- Admin skills page lists Remotion / Frontend Design / Dynamic Workflows as reference capabilities (2026-06-02, claude/awesome-pare-a7c6de branch): The admin Agents & Skills page (`/admin/skills`) auto-discovers skills by scanning `.claude/` and `~/.claude/` folders at request time. Three capabilities never showed reliably: Remotion (`remotion-best-practices`) and Frontend Design (`frontend-design`) live as symlinks into `~/.agents/skills` (not committed, so absent on the deployed page), and Dynamic Workflows is a built-in Claude Code orchestration tool with no file at all. Added a committed `REFERENCE_CAPABILITIES` list in `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts`. These are the **curated founder-facing view and take precedence**: when one matches a scanned skill by id, the reference entry replaces it (so the clean label/description wins over the skill's technical name) — Remotion renders as **"Remotion · Create videos in React"**, not the raw `remotion-best-practices` skill name, and the slash chip is dropped. This also covers the deployed page (and Dynamic Workflows everywhere), where the symlinks/built-in tool aren't present. Purely a founder reference so these tools aren't forgotten; no new categories — they slot into the existing Project-skill / User-skill grouping. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 + clean `next build`; typecheck clean. **Browser-verified on main** (`/admin/skills`, logged in as devtest): all three render, deduped, in the right groups. **Commits** `ec373a3`, `f5df556` (merges `97d8c3d`, `f13dbfa`).
- Calibrate Jove's body-question pressure — body is one valid anchor, not required (2026-06-02, fix/extraction-body-calibration branch): Founder-reported: Jove asks about the body too often ("what did your body do," "where do you feel that"). The scan found the source is the extraction layer (`extraction.ts`), which pushed somatic framing uniformly and persona-blind, overriding the voice layer — which is already well-calibrated (general = "no somatic-first defaults"; ADHD = "register, not the somatic register"; autistic = body available but "most turns don't need this"). Two changes. (1) **Body is one valid checkpoint anchor, not required** — the `extraction.ts` Jove-brief line and the `confirm-checkpoint.ts` composer rule now accept a body sensation OR a concrete behavioral/system response as the anchor; Jove no longer keeps steering toward the body when the user isn't going there; body is still preferred when the user gives it (keeps the autistic-interoception benefit). (2) **The next-prompt input hint follows the user's register** instead of "prefer somatic openings over emotional ones." Prompt-text only; no schema or logic change. **Tests/build.** Full suite + `next build` green via pre-commit hook (persona suite 719/719; no test pinned the changed strings). **Commit** `8561d79`.
- Correct checkpoint-pipeline model attributions + sync post-confirm block doc (2026-06-02, inspect/page-current branch): Doc-accuracy fixes surfaced while prepping the prompt constructor for hands-on learning. **`system.md`:** the checkpoint detector was documented as a "Haiku classifier" (a model call) — it is actually a deterministic regex (`detect-checkpoint.ts → detectCheckpointInResponse`), no model; and the composer was documented as Sonnet — it is Opus (`COMPOSITION_MODEL = "claude-opus-4-6"` in `config.ts`). Fixed all three references (pipeline diagram, multi-channel flow line, checkpoint-lifecycle paragraph). **`prompt-constructor-delta.md`:** collapsed the two post-confirm rows (`post-confirm-first-message-2` / `post-confirm-subsequent-single`) into the single `post-confirm` block from merge `76136bc`, in both the block-condition and parser-miss tables. Docs-only; no code change. **Commit** `d1a37dd`.
- Prompt-architecture page — clearer live-context labels + how-it-operates detail (2026-06-02, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Further refinement of the live-context (dynamic) blocks from the teaching walkthrough. Dropped the redundant "Live ·" prefix from the band labels (the `LIVE CONTEXT (PARALLEL)` section header + per-block descriptions carry it). **Renamed for clarity:** Confirmed Manual → "Recent confirmed Manual entries"; Earlier Entries (compressed) → "Earlier confirmed Manual entries (compressed)"; Session Context → "Last session recap". Each dynamic block's expandable detail now leads with a "HOW IT OPERATES" explanation of the mechanism, then labels the illustrative mock text under "── EXAMPLE (illustrative — not a real user) ──", so the per-user sample can't be mistaken for shared prompt content. **Extraction Brief** gained an explicit in-scope / out-of-scope: it reads and summarizes the conversation for Jove (scene, body words, the bind, layer signal, checkpoint readiness); it does not decide the reply, never writes to the Manual, isn't shown to the user, and lags one turn. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 (UI/copy only); typecheck + clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview worktree-anchored) — labels + detail copy to eyeball on `/admin/prompt-architecture`. **Commit** `beef054`.
- Prompt-architecture page — collapse Tier 1/2 spine bands + describe live-context blocks (2026-06-02, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Follow-on cleanup to the same page, surfaced in the teaching walkthrough. **Reduced visual load:** the always-on "foundation" spine collapsed from 7 individual bands to **2** — `Tier 1 · Identity + Constitutional Rules` and `Tier 2 · Base Voice` — each keeping its sub-section headers visible *inside* the band (Introduction · Constitutional Rules; Voice · Banned Phrases · Pacing · Repair Protocol · Advisory) with combined token counts, and a new `GroupDetail` panel that breaks the sections out header-by-header on click. Mirrors the real cache structure (`buildSystemPromptBlocks` bundles intro + Tier 1 into one block). New `group` Selection kind + `GroupBand`/`GroupDetail`; the now-unused `SectionBand` was removed. **Self-explaining live context:** each dynamic block's detail panel (Extraction Brief, Confirmed Manual, Earlier Entries, Session Context) now carries a plain-language "what this is" description + an explicit "the rendered text is a placeholder example, generated per user, never shared" marker — closing the gap where the mock extraction brief read like shared prompt content. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 (no test delta — UI only); typecheck + clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified in-session** (preview is worktree-anchored) — the collapsed bands + new detail panels are a layout/copy change to eyeball on `/admin/prompt-architecture`. **Commit** `7dd1159`.
- Prompt-architecture page accuracy + post-confirm block merge + agent no-flattery rule (2026-06-02, claude/stupefied-haibt-fa8b75 branch): Output of a teaching walkthrough of the prompt constructor that surfaced real fixes. **Admin `/admin/prompt-architecture`:** dropped the "Layer 0–5" step labels (Layer is the reserved noun for the five Manual sections, and the numbers fought "Tier") for plain names + an orientation line that the views group by change-frequency; renamed the tour's "Stage N" → "Step N" (visible + internal `STEPS`/`stepIndex`/`StepCaption`) to de-conflict from "Phase"; corrected the mode-opener pill from "1 of 3" to "entry phase only" (mode openers are turn-gated and usually zero mid-conversation); flagged the page's conversation state as illustrative fixture data, not a real user; moved the live-context panel from a floating left sidecar to the bottom of the prompt stack so it reads as the prompt's dynamic tail; added parser entries + a sidecar footnote for the two conditional live-context blocks (Transcript detected, Exploration focus) the examples never trigger; expanded `MOCK_ENTRIES` 3→6 so the compressed "EARLIER ENTRIES" block actually renders (the recent-full floor is 4, so 3 entries never demonstrated compression). The sibling-calls strip heading ("the other three" over four cards) was also corrected to "what else runs this turn". **Persona (`system-prompt.ts`):** a senior-engineer audit of all Tier-3 blocks (no dead blocks; upload/guided-intake/post-confirm all reachable — founder confirmed upload + guided are intended live) found one real merge — `post-confirm-first-message-2` and `post-confirm-subsequent-single` collapsed into one `post-confirm` block (byte-identical output per mode, so the snapshots are unchanged), and the shared "A Manual takes time to build…" paragraph extracted into `POST_CONFIRM_FIRST_ENTRY_SCAFFOLD`, used by both the block and `call-persona`'s `buildPostConfirmFallback` so the two copies can't drift; the G4 co-render guard id-list and the `call-persona` source assertion were updated to match. **Process:** added a "Talk straight, no flattery" hard rule to CLAUDE.md (agent→founder tone; distinct from Jove's product-facing "never patronize" voice rule) plus a matching memory note. **Tests/build.** 1154/1154 (the post-confirm merge removed 3 per-block iterated tests); typecheck + clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified in-session** — the live-context attach-as-tail is a layout change and the preview is worktree-anchored; everything else is copy/fixture/logic covered by build+tests. Pull main and eyeball the live-context tail on `/admin/prompt-architecture`. **Commits** `2b0c1c4`, `655b80e`, `76136bc`.
- Invited rows show login activity + true invite date (2026-06-02, claude/invited-login-dates branch): Follow-up to the beta-access unification. Invited rows in the admin Beta tab now render `Invited <date> · First sign-in <date> · Last login <date or Never>` — the who-hasn't-shown-up signal, inline where you review signups (previously only in the Health tab's active-users panel). **Migration** `20260602200000_waitlist_invited_at.sql` adds `waitlist.invited_at timestamptz` (nullable, NOT backfilled) — stamped whenever a row's status flips to `invited` (PATCH invite + POST manual-invite, insert and promote paths). Rows invited before the column existed (incl. the unify backfill) have no `invited_at` and the UI falls back to `created_at` so they're never blank. **Login data:** `GET /api/admin/waitlist` enriches invited rows with `last_sign_in_at` + `first_sign_in_at` by joining `auth.users` (reuses `listAllAuthUsers`; skipped entirely when there are no invited rows; fail-soft — an auth-list error just omits the login fields rather than failing the tab). **Removed** the auto "Invited via admin on <date>" note — every invited row was invited by an admin, so it was noise; the Invited date carries the timing (the `notes` field still shows genuine admin notes). Waiting/declined rows unchanged. **Tests/build.** 1157/1157 (+2: GET auth-enrichment path, `invited_at` stamping); typecheck + build clean. **Not browser-verified in-session** (`invited_at` lands when CI applies the migration; preview is worktree-anchored) — confirm the invited-row display in prod after deploy. **Commit** `b377126`.
- Unify waitlist + beta_allowlist into one status-driven list (2026-06-02, claude/unify-beta-access branch): Beta access used two tables — `waitlist` (funnel record, with a cosmetic `status` that gated nothing) and `beta_allowlist` (the real auth gate). "Invited" on the waitlist did nothing; access was granted only by a non-atomic copy-into-allowlist + delete-from-waitlist that could drift. **Now one source of truth: a `waitlist` row with `status='invited'` IS beta access.** `isEmailAllowlisted` (signup + OAuth gates) reads `waitlist WHERE email=? AND status='invited'`, still fails CLOSED; inviting is a single atomic status flip (no copy/delete). **Migration** `20260602100000_unify_beta_access.sql` adds `waitlist.notes` and backfills every allowlisted email as `invited` (idempotent, merges by `UNIQUE(email)`); the old `beta_allowlist` table is left in place and **dropped in a follow-up migration after prod login is verified on the new gate** (see In-Flight). **Admin:** the separate Allowlist tab + `/api/admin/beta-allowlist` route are gone; one filterable/sortable list (status filter chips with counts + email search; default sort waiting → invited → declined, newest within each), per-row Invite / Decline(revoke) + Mark seen, and an "Invite an email directly" box (`POST /api/admin/waitlist`, insert-or-promote, `seen=true`). `active-users` panel reads invited rows; the new-signups badge derives from the loaded list and ticks down live. **Tests/build.** 1155/1155 (+11; incl. a direct `isEmailAllowlisted` gate test); typecheck + build clean; senior-engineer review verdict: ship. **Not browser-verified in-session** (the `notes` column lands when CI applies the migration on this push; preview was also worktree-anchored) — verify the invite/decline/login flow in prod once the migration lands. **Commit** `4844375`.
- Persona-delta selector mirrors runtime stacking (2026-06-02, claude/persona-delta-stacking branch): The admin prompt-architecture page (`/admin/prompt-architecture`) let you select only ONE persona delta — the fan was single-select ("1 of 4 · click to switch"), so the documented autistic + adhd stack was impossible to view, even though `composeTier2` and the API route already accept and stack a `PersonaMode[]`. **Fix: mirror runtime by reusing runtime logic, not copying it.** Selection now runs through `togglePersonaMode` (the exact function the app uses — neurotypes stack, `general` is mutually exclusive), so the admin selector can't drift from production composition. Page state `personaMode: PersonaMode` → `personaModes: PersonaMode[]`; the page sends the CSV the route already splits and feeds to `composeTier2` (via `buildAllPhases`), so the rendered Tier-2 is the true stacked prompt for any combination. Pills are multi-select (`active = personaModes.includes(p)`); copy changed to "neurotypes stack · general is exclusive". Single-persona labels (Header "Active:", PersonaDetail token line, ExampleAssemblyFooter token budget) now render a combined label via a new `personaLabel()` helper that mirrors `composeTier2`'s effective-modes rule (empty → General; any neurotype drops General). API route empty-default `autistic` → `general`, matching `composeTier2`. **Tests/build.** 1144/1144; typecheck clean; clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified in-session** — the preview tool was anchored to a different worktree/branch; correctness rests on reusing `togglePersonaMode` + `composeTier2` verbatim (no duplicated stacking logic to drift). **Commit** `9fd35e7` — fast-forwarded into main.
- Fix false count in the admin prompt-architecture "Sibling calls" heading (2026-06-02, fix/sibling-calls-heading branch): The strip renders four sibling-call cards (Extraction, Shadow monitor, Checkpoint detector, Manual entry composer) but the heading read "the other three this turn" — stale from before the fourth card landed. The four aren't even the same kind of call (the detector is a regex, the composer fires only on checkpoint turns), so the count was dropped rather than bumped to "four": the heading now reads "what else runs this turn." Surfaced during a teaching walkthrough of the page. Text-only JSX change; verified no test depends on the string. **Commit** `493bbae` — fast-forwarded into main.
- Waitlist new-signup notifications become per-row dismissible (2026-06-02, nervous-hertz-f03192 branch): The admin "new signups" badge (in-app pill `AdminSignupsBadge` + admin-nav "Beta" count) was a live count of all `status='waiting'` waitlist rows — so the only way to clear it was to *act* on each signup (invite/decline/add-to-beta), and the waitlist tab gave no way to tell which rows were new. Added a `seen` flag mirroring the existing `beta_feedback.is_read` pattern. **Schema.** Migration `20260602000000_waitlist_seen.sql` — `waitlist.seen boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`; existing rows default unseen so the badge count doesn't jump on deploy. **Count.** `GET /api/admin/waitlist/count` now counts `status='waiting' AND seen=false` (decouples *new/undismissed* from *needs-action* — dismissing no longer forces invite/decline). **Routes.** `GET /api/admin/waitlist` selects `seen`; `PATCH` gained a `seen:true` branch alongside the status update (`seen` takes precedence when both present). **UI.** `WaitlistTab` labels unseen rows with a NEW pill and gives each a "Mark seen" button; `useAdminData.markWaitlistSeen` PATCHes + updates local state; the admin-nav badge ticks down live on dismiss (the in-app pill is eventual-consistency — refreshes on next mount). All new styles use `--session-*` tokens (design-token test green). **Tests/build.** Suite 1144/1144 (+2 for the `seen` PATCH branch); clean `next build`. **Not browser-verified** — the `seen` column isn't in the dev DB until the migration applies via CI `supabase db push` on this push; verify the badge/label/dismiss flow in prod once the migration lands. **Note:** authored against the main checkout's working tree (worktree-session cwd drift), committed directly on `main`. **Commit** `3d04440`.
- Remove inert `PERSONA_VERSION` + correct the Versioning doc (2026-06-02, chore/remove-inert-persona-version branch): The 2026-05-29 dead-code audit flagged `PERSONA_VERSION` in `src/lib/version.ts` as a contradiction — the file comment and `docs/system.md` claimed it drove Anthropic prompt-cache invalidation, but it was imported nowhere and drove nothing. Confirmed the truth: Anthropic caching is content-addressed (`cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }`), so any edit to the system-prompt text invalidates the cache on the next call automatically — no version string is involved. Deleted the constant; kept `APP_VERSION` (live — desktop footer badge in `DesktopVitrine.tsx`). Also fixed the stale `version.ts` comment (it claimed a pre-commit hook enforces version bumps; the hook only runs test + build) and rewrote the `system.md` Versioning section to describe only `APP_VERSION`, with a note on why there's no persona-prompt version (so it isn't re-added). Pure dead-code removal + doc correction; no runtime change. **Tests/build.** Green via pre-commit hook (full suite + `next build`). **Commit** `fd1ab7a` — fast-forwarded into main.
- Hide sticky mobile CTA when the contact form is in view (2026-06-01, mobile-cta-hide branch): UX follow-up to the mobile landing pass. The "Request beta access" pill scrolled users to the form but then floated redundantly on top of it. A second `IntersectionObserver` in `RevealInit` toggles `.is-hidden` on the pill when `#contact` intersects (threshold 0.18, both directions — re-shows on scroll up); CSS fades/translates it out. Chose this over a modal/bottom-sheet: a form inside an overlay is exactly where the mobile keyboard misbehaves in Instagram/LinkedIn in-app browsers (the fragility this whole pass set out to fix), and the in-page form avoids it by scrolling naturally. Desktop unaffected (pill is `display:none`). **Tests/build.** 1129/1129; clean `next build`. **Commit** `49f053b` — fast-forwarded into main. (Resolves the deferred CTA-visibility note on the entry below.)

- Mobile landing pass — optimize for social arrivals (2026-06-01, mobile-landing-pass branch): Mobile-only polish on the public landing (`src/app/landing.css` + `src/app/page.tsx`); **desktop layout untouched** (verified: `.mobile-cta` is `display:none` and `.hero-grid` keeps its two columns at desktop width). Motivation: the beta funnel is Instagram/LinkedIn, which is ~95% mobile arriving in in-app browsers, and the page was built desktop-first so mobile was a reflow. Decided against a separate mobile layout/codebase (drift + double-maintenance for one page where the message doesn't diverge) in favor of treating the `≤560px` breakpoint as a first-class designed view. **Fixes.** (a) **Rotating-word clipping** — the lockup rendered at a single huge size (54px floor on mobile), so long words from the rotator (`frustrating`, `thoughtful`, `perceptive`) overflowed the narrow column and clipped; the lockup is now `clamp(38px, 12vw, 50px)` on mobile, sized so the longest word fits. (b) **`vh` hero height** — `min-height: 84vh/78vh` is unreliable in in-app browsers (toolbars resize `vh`), which pushed/cut content; mobile hero is now natural height (`min-height:auto`, `align-items:flex-start`) so the value prop + CTA sit above the fold. (c) **Rotator descenders** — given `min-height:1.2em` + `overflow:visible` so italic descenders never clip or crowd "walnut". (d) **Sticky CTA** — a new `.mobile-cta` ("Request beta access") fixed to the bottom, thumb-reachable and persistent (the single biggest conversion lever for social traffic); rendered always, shown only at `≤560px`. (e) Tightened section rhythm for mobile scannability. All changes are additive inside the existing `≤560px` media block. **Tests/build.** 1129/1129; clean `next build`. **Commit** `666e253` — rebased onto main and pushed (`2530865`; origin had concurrent prompt-injector work that integrated cleanly). **Deferred:** could fade the sticky CTA in only after the hero scrolls past (currently always visible) — left simple pending real conversion data.

- Dead-code removal + prompt-injector single-source refactor + silent-bug fixes (2026-06-01, dead-code-audit branch): Two workstreams off two multi-agent audits. **(1) Dead code (`3339e14`).** Removed symbols/files with zero references after adversarial verification: `formatAdminDateShort`, `LAYER_COUNT` (+ its decisions.md mention), `getVendorsByStatus`/`getVendorById`, the `trackEntryEdited`/`trackFirstCheckpointCompleted` analytics emitters (+ tests), and `scripts/test-phase{1..5}-gate.sh` (stale harnesses asserting on long-deleted files). Report: `docs/audits/dead-code-2026-05-29.md`. **(2) Prompt-injector overhaul (`2e179bc`).** Audit `docs/audits/prompt-injector-2026-06-01.md` traced the Tier-3 injector for the silent "wired-but-never-fires" bug class (35-agent workflow; 4 confirmed bugs, 5 intent calls). **Refactor — one source of truth.** The two 1:1 builders (`buildSystemPromptBlocks` web + legacy `buildSystemPrompt` SMS/admin) each hand-rolled the identical 10-field `Tier3Flags` literal; collapsed into one `deriveTier3Flags(input): Tier3Flags` both call, with an explicit no-spread typed return so a forgotten flag is a compile error, not a silently missing block. Output byte-identical (the existing `bothForms` equivalence + staticContext/dynamic byte-stability suites pass unedited — the cache split is provably untouched). **Bug fixes.** (a) **post-rejection timing** — the pinned "That entry didn't land…" line gated on `checkpointApproaching` (a verbatim alias of `showCheckpointInstructions`), so it could fail to fire right after a rejection when extraction no longer reported approaching; now gates on a real `postRejection` signal wired through `call-persona.ts` (new option) + the confirm route (`action === "rejected"`). (b) **post-action co-render** — the checkpoint-proposal blocks (CHECKPOINTS, first-checkpoint) loaded on post-confirm/post-rejection turns alongside the pinned response, handing the model contradictory turn instructions; `showCheckpointInstructions` now requires `postConfirmMode === null && !postRejection`. (c) **SMS transcript gap** — the text bridge never computed `transcriptContext`, so a pasted thread over SMS missed the TRANSCRIPT DETECTED guardrails (incl. the do-not-profile-other-people safety instruction) the in-app prompt carries; `persona-bridge.ts` now mirrors the web detection. (d) **copy** — readiness-gate claimed "all 5 layers… Five layers, each with a core picture" when it fires at ≥3 entries on any distribution (now honest about a working first version with layers still to fill); first-checkpoint cited the deleted "PROGRESS SIGNALS" section (removed). **Intent calls (owner-decided).** `pattern_engaged` now honors its documented reset (true→false on explicit user reversal) instead of latching true forever; the "Previous session:" label is now the honest "Earlier in this conversation:"; the inert `guidedPostureSoftened` dead flag (produced by nothing) was removed. **Guard tests (new).** `src/lib/persona/tier3-blocks.test.ts` — guards over the *real producer-reachable* flag domain (not hand-built flags, which is what hid `guidedPostureSoftened`): every block reachable, always-on allowlist, post-action↔proposal mutual exclusion, boolean-flag reachability ledger, and copy contracts (no overclaiming, no deleted-section references). **Tests/build.** Full suite 1129/1129 on merged main (branch was 1098; +31 from main's flow-review work integrated cleanly); type-clean (no new errors); build clean. **Commits** `3339e14` (dead code), `2e179bc` (injector). Fast-forwarded into main.

- Landing visual elevation + final tagline (2026-05-29, landing-design-elevation branch): Design polish on the public landing (`src/app/page.tsx` + `src/app/landing.css`) — no routing, data, or behavior change. **Depth.** The flat Bloom ground became a layered six-radial mesh (warm light pooling top + corners, espresso pooling bottom) plus a soft full-page vignette, grain bumped 0.025 → 0.04, so the page reads as a lit surface rather than a single fill. **Header.** The floating wordmark is now a proper sticky translucent bar (`backdrop-filter: blur(16px) saturate(125%)`, hairline bottom border) carrying a nav: `Sign in` link + a `Request beta access` outline pill (fills walnut on hover; pill hidden < 560px). **Accents.** Section dividers (`.rule`) went from a flat hairline to a center-weighted `linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, hair, transparent)` fade; each `.eyebrow` label gains a short walnut tick (`::before`); cards gained a top-edge light sheen (`::before` highlight) + 6px radius so the tiles catch the page light. **Tagline finalized** across hero thesis, page `<title>` / OG / Twitter metadata, and the OG share card: "A different path to understanding yourself, and being understood. / For neurodivergent adults." **OG card** also de-flattened — warm diagonal `linear-gradient` wash (Satori-safe; radial is unreliable) + walnut tick before the eyebrow. All colors via `--session-*` tokens (background-mesh + header-glass rgba literals are intentional, scoped to `.mw-landing`, outside the admin design-token test scope). **Tests/build.** 1058/1058; clean `next build`. **Commit** `af3d27b` — fast-forwarded into main (`39de506 → af3d27b`).

- Flow-review Tier-2 remainder F/G/H/I (2026-06-01, flow-review-tier2-remainder branch): The four correctness findings from `docs/audits/flow-review-2026-05-29.md` held while the prompt-injector workstream was in flight — picked up once it merged, each re-verified against current main first. **F — mid-stream Anthropic stall (High).** `anthropicStream` cleared its connect timeout the moment headers arrived, leaving the downstream body read unbounded — a 200-then-stall (truncated SSE that never sends `message_stop`, or a network partition) hung the function until Vercel's wall-clock kill with a frozen UI. New tested `withIdleTimeout` wrapper (`src/lib/anthropic.ts`) re-arms a per-chunk idle timer (30s default), aborts the upstream fetch on a stall, and errors the stream as an `AbortError` so call-persona's existing timeout path surfaces "took too long, try again." **G — confirm-vs-refine race.** The reject/refine/defer UPDATE in `checkpoint/confirm/route.ts` gated only on the stale `msg` fetched earlier; a concurrent confirm (FOR UPDATE) landing between read and write would be clobbered, orphaning the just-written `manual_entries` row. Added a DB-level `checkpoint_meta->>status = 'pending'` precondition + `.select()` — the write no-ops (returns `alreadyHandled`) when a concurrent action already moved it out of pending. **H — optimistic-edit 404.** The optimistic confirmed entry in `useChat.ts` carries the checkpoint message id, not the real `manual_entries.id`; editing it during the multi-second follow-up stream PATCHed a non-existent row (404). `loadManual()` (server rows, real ids) now runs BEFORE the follow-up stream instead of after. **I — empty-summary overwrite.** `generateSessionSummary` could blank a good stored summary with `''` on a 200-but-empty Anthropic completion; it now requires a non-empty, TITLE-bearing summary before persisting. **Tests/build.** Merged suite 1142/1142 (+8 across anthropic/confirm/generate-summary); typecheck fully green (the 9 stale fixtures were cleared upstream in `aafa607`); build clean. **Closes the actionable flow-review backlog** — Tier-1 (7) + Tier-2 conflict-free (5) + F/G/H/I (4). **Commit** `f879b9f`. **Merge SHA** `d70026e`.

- User-flow review audit + Tier-1 security & Tier-2 correctness fixes (2026-06-01, cleanup branch): A multi-agent review of 11 core user flows (169 verified findings; report at `docs/audits/flow-review-2026-05-29.md`), then the conflict-free fixes off the back of it. **Tier 1 (security + data).** (1) **IDOR on `/api/chat`** — the route used the client-supplied `conversationId` with no ownership check (the admin client bypasses RLS, so the route was the only boundary); now 404s on a foreign/missing id before `callPersona` runs. (2) **Anthropic rate limits** — `/api/conversations/complete` had none (now shares `sessionSummaryHour` with the sibling summary route); the SMS path gained the durable daily cap (fail-open). (3) **Waitlist anon INSERT** locked down via migration (see Known Issues). (4) **OAuth beta gate** re-checks the allowlist on every callback (not just a 60s window) and only deletes freshly-created rows. (5) **`GET /api/manual`** no longer returns 200-with-empty on a DB read error (which the client stored as a wiped Manual). (6) **`/api/account/delete`** is now an ordered, fail-closed cascade (manual_entries before messages per the RESTRICT FK; never deletes the auth user if a data delete failed). (7) **PII in logs** — stopped logging the raw phone (PostgREST error `details`) in the OTP route and the raw email in admin beta-allowlist. **Tier 2 (correctness / dead-ends).** Resume now re-activates a pending checkpoint (was inert) and skips the synthetic `text-channel` pseudo-conversation (shared `pendingCheckpointFromMessages` helper, dedups `loadConversation`); `sendMessage` isolates the `messaging_events` audit write so a bookkeeping failure can't report a delivered OTP as FAILED; the OTP send route sends before persisting (a failed send can't wipe a verified link) with an insert→update 23505 fallback + a `UNIQUE(user_id)` migration; the onboarding modal ladder advances in one session. **Held for the prompt-injector workstream (file overlap):** Tier-2 F/G/H/I — see In-Flight. **Tests/build.** 1089/1089 (+31 across chat/manual/callback/account-delete/complete/send/user-phone/useChat); type-clean; build clean. **Two migrations pending application** — see Known Issues. **Commits** `bd32e4c` (report), `83cd9fe` (Tier 1), `a0abfa8` (Tier 2). **Merge SHA** `29d0e7a`.

- Admin prompt-architecture: foundation frame + tier/live header prefixes (2026-05-29, admin-foundation-frame branch): Admin-UI polish on `/admin/prompt-architecture` — no constructor or runtime change. The always-present cards (intro, Tier 1, Tier 2 voice, banned phrases, pacing, repair, advisory — the `SpineBands` cluster) are now wrapped in a bordered "THE FOUNDATION · IN EVERY PACKET" frame, so the always-on group reads as one unit, distinct from the persona/mode switchers and the conditional Tier 3 ladder. Every card header also gains a consistent origin prefix via a new `displayLabel()` helper in `page.tsx`: `Tier 1 ·` / `Tier 2 ·` / `Tier 3 ·` for the three prompt tiers (derived from each section's `tier` field) and `Live ·` for the runtime-appended dynamic blocks (confirmed-manual, earlier-entries, session-context, extraction-brief). The helper skips labels already carrying a `Tier …`/`Live …` prefix, so the three baked-in labels (Constitutional Rules, Voice, Conversation Mechanics) don't double up. Frame border + heading use CSS-var tokens only — design-token test green. **Tests/build.** 1058/1058; build clean (admin pages are typechecked by the build, no dedicated unit tests). **Commit** `eca4210` — fast-forwarded into main (`7a0f1f8 → eca4210`).

- Public marketing landing at `/`, app moved to `/app`, dormant middleware activated (2026-05-29, marketing-landing-page branch): The first public face of the product. **Landing.** New editorial light ("Bloom") page at `/` (`src/app/page.tsx` + `src/app/landing.css` scoped under `.mw-landing`), ported from `mockups/landing.html`: hero with a shuffled blur-crossfade word rotator (32 self-descriptors, half affirming strengths / half owned quirks, `prefers-reduced-motion` aware), then the gap / how-it-works (3 steps) / the Manual (5 layers) / differentiators (5 tiles incl. strengths + "meets you where it's easiest") / why-walnut / contact. Reuses the app's `--session-*` tokens and `next/font` faces (no CDN, no duplicated tokens); light is forced via a scoped `data-theme="light" .scrollable-page` wrapper so the dark-default FOUC script never fights it. Client components: `src/components/landing/{HeroRotator,RevealInit,ContactForm}.tsx`. **App moved `/` → `/app`** (`src/app/app/page.tsx`, `force-dynamic`). **Middleware relocated root → `src/middleware.ts` — and was previously DORMANT.** A `src/`-directory project requires `src/middleware.ts`; the root `middleware.ts` was never compiled (empty `middleware-manifest`, no edge gating — every protected route returned 200 unauth). Relocating activated it: `/` public (landing), authed `/` and `/login` → `/app`, unauth `/app` & `/admin` → `/login`, `/waitlist` added to public routes (fixes a latent bug where the `not_allowlisted` OAuth bounce redirected to `/login`). Matcher widened to exclude `_next`, `opengraph-image`, `api(?!/admin)`, and any path with a file extension (robots.txt, sitemap.xml, manifest.webmanifest, sw.js, icons, *.png, …) so public assets + metadata stay reachable logged-out. **Implication: edge auth-gating is now enforced where it previously was not** — the app's real gating had been client-side + per-route `requireAdmin`; verify prod auth/session behavior after deploy. Hardcoded `/` redirects retargeted to `/app` (`LoginScreen`, `SeedScreen`, `reset-password`, `AdminNavRail` EXIT ADMIN), `auth/callback` default redirect `origin` → `/app`, PWA `manifest.webmanifest` `start_url` → `/app`. **Contact capture.** The landing form writes to the existing `/api/waitlist` (waitlist table, IP rate-limited, deduped); name is folded into the `source` field — no schema change, deliberately avoiding a migration/deploy-ordering hazard (a dedicated `name` column is deferred). **Admin signup notifications (no webhook).** New `GET /api/admin/waitlist/count` (`requireAdmin`, `status='waiting'` count); an admin-only in-app pill (`AdminSignupsBadge`, fixed top-center on `/app`, links to `/admin?section=beta`) + a count badge on the admin nav "Beta" tab. **SEO (indexable).** Per-route metadata + `metadataBase` (`https://mywalnut.app`), generated OG image via `opengraph-image.tsx` (edge `ImageResponse`, brand palette), `robots.ts` + `sitemap.ts` (allow `/`, `/privacy`, `/terms`; disallow app/admin/api/auth). **Verified in dev:** clean `next build`; waitlist + admin-waitlist + design-token tests pass; unauth routing (`/` 200, `/app`+`/admin` → `/login`, `/waitlist` 200), authed flow (`devtest@test.com` login → `/app`, `/` → `/app`), and robots/sitemap/OG/manifest all reachable logged-out. **Deferred:** dedicated waitlist `name` column; OG brand-serif font (currently Satori default). **Stale doc found:** `system.md`'s onboarding section describes an anonymous-guest/seed flow that `OnboardingFlow` no longer implements (entry/login only) — flagged, not edited.

- Admin reference pages reconciled with the pipeline (2026-05-29, admin-reference-reconcile branch): Docs-style correction (content/data only, no behavior change) — the four `/admin` reference pages (how-it-works, prompt-architecture, extraction-map, schema-map) had drifted from the code and are now current against the pipeline reference doc (`docs/architecture/jove-pipeline.md`). **Spine: the shadow Monitor was invisible across three of the four pages.** It now appears on all three that cover the per-turn call structure — how-it-works (Stage 2), prompt-architecture (`SIBLING_CALLS` + the per-turn recount), and schema-map (the `monitor_reads` table) — with consistent framing: runs every web turn, writes `monitor_reads`, **consumed by nothing** (a validated sensor wired to no actuator, awaiting the unbuilt deterministic selector). extraction-map correctly omits it (the Monitor writes `monitor_reads`, not `extraction_state`). **Also corrected:** the checkpoint classifier was mislabeled a Haiku model on how-it-works (it's a deterministic regex); the Composer was mislabeled Sonnet and shown running on-confirm (it's Opus, composing at proposal time — the on-confirm step is a non-model DB write); the per-turn call count was wrong (three model calls every turn — Jove, Extraction, Monitor — with the Composer a conditional fourth on checkpoint turns); seven `validateMaterialQuality` citations on extraction-map were re-pointed to their post-Lock-1 lines (the function moved from the ~424–464 range to 508+); and the `has_charged_language` reader was corrected from "always required" to informational (Lock 1 replaced that boolean gate with a deterministic `language_bank` read). **Persona-mode label:** the `adhd` mode, mislabeled "AuDHD" in two places on prompt-architecture, was corrected to ADHD — there is no `audhd` system mode (AuDHD describes a person who stacks autistic + adhd). **Tests/build.** Suite 1058/1058; build clean (admin pages are typechecked by the build — no dedicated unit tests). **Deferred:** three stale "Haiku" code comments (`monitor.ts:4`, `persona-pipeline.ts:366`, the `monitor_reads` migration header) → a separate code-comment sweep. **Commit** `ced5f83`. **Merge SHA** `a85f2e3`.

- ADR-043 upstream merged-gate — `deriveCheckpointApproaching` charged + crisis gate (2026-05-28, jove-checkpoint-gate-upstream branch): The second implementation half of ADR-043's merged-gate principle; **Lock 1 (2026-05-27) closed the firing gate, this closes the instruction-loading gate.** **The bug.** `deriveCheckpointApproaching` — the upstream decision that loads the CHECKPOINTS instructions into Jove's prompt — had a signal-ready short-circuit (`if (signalReady) return true`) that returned *before* `validateMaterialQuality` ran, so every gate in that function (charged-material, crisis, depth, mechanism) was bypassed from the signal path. Because extraction bootstraps any layer with a confirmed entry to "explored" minimum (`extraction.ts:353`) and `extraction_state` is conversation-scoped, a returning user starts every new session with an empty `language_bank` but their confirmed-entry layers already at "explored" — so **every returning user's session opener hit the bypass**, priming Jove toward a checkpoint with no charged material (and, during a crisis, conflicting with the crisis protocol). The save was still blocked downstream by the firing gate (Lock 1); what was wrong was the prompt being primed. **The fix.** The signal-ready short-circuit now returns true only when a high/medium charged phrase in the `language_bank` is tagged to a signal-ready layer AND no crisis is active; otherwise it falls through to `validateMaterialQuality`, which applies all gates uniformly. **Signal-ready is a candidate, not a verdict — at both thresholds now.** `pattern_engaged` is deliberately NOT required at the instruction-loading threshold (instructions must load before Jove can name a pattern for the user to engage; the firing gate still enforces it). **Crisis guard is net-new coverage.** The same bypass meant signal-ready-during-crisis could load checkpoint-seeking instructions; the `!crisisActive` condition closes it, and a new test exercises that path for the first time (the prior crisis test passed only because its default layers were non-signal-ready). **Tests.** 1058/1058 (was 1055; +3 net). Two signal-only tests renamed to match the charged contract (+fixtures) so the suite no longer asserts the removed "signal alone fires" behavior; three new regression guards (signal-ready + uncharged → false; charge on a different layer → false; signal-ready + charged + crisis → false), each paired with a one-variable contrast that flips the result back to true. Build clean. **Does NOT change.** The firing gate (Lock 1) is untouched; output-fidelity (Lock 2, the verbatim-in-saved-entry backstop) remains deferred. **Docs.** ADR-043 second implementation note; `master.md` edit-history; the admin `extraction-map` page citation for this reader refreshed (line + behavior). The `jove-pipeline.md` reference doc is a separate in-flight branch (`jove-pipeline-doc`), not part of this ship. **Commit** `ef6df2b`. **Merge SHA** `3287f70`.

- Lock 1 charged-material gate + architecture docs + admin page fixes (2026-05-27, jove-prompt-architecture-3.1 branch): One merge carrying three separated commits. **(1) feat(persona) — Lock 1, the charged-material gate.** A checkpoint can no longer fire unless the user's `language_bank` actually holds a high- or medium-charge phrase tied to the candidate pattern. The check folds into the existing ripeness logic in `validateMaterialQuality` (`src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts`): the old `gate.has_charged_language` boolean (model-reported, could read true on an empty bank) is replaced by a deterministic read of the real `language_bank` — at least one entry with `charge` in {high, medium}, linked to the candidate `strongest_layer` when one has resolved, with an unlinked fallback (any high/medium phrase) when `strongest_layer` is null. The `has_charged_language` field is kept (still read by `formatExtractionForPersona` and the Stage A narrative) but no longer gates. **Fail-closed.** The function's null/empty-state early return flipped from `{ ok: true }` to `{ ok: false }`: a missing extraction state, an empty bank, or a low-only bank now reads as not-ripe rather than ripe — closing a fail-open hole the old two-gate design had backstopped with a post-composition check that this one-gate design removes. **Input gate, not output check.** Lock 1 guarantees charged material EXISTS in the bank before a checkpoint fires; it does NOT verify the composed entry uses the phrase verbatim — output-fidelity is trusted to the composer prompt for now. Lock 2 (the verbatim-in-saved-entry backstop) is deferred; trigger to build it: the composer observed paraphrasing charged language away in beta. Decision context: ADR-043 + its 2026-05-27 implementation note. **(2) fix(admin) — prompt-architecture page corrections.** The `/admin/prompt-architecture` page had stale sibling-call metadata and a section parser that silently dropped several emitted blocks: the "classifier" relabeled from a Haiku call to the deterministic regex it actually is (`detectCheckpointInResponse`); composer model corrected Sonnet → Opus (`claude-opus-4-6`); the `first-message` regex fixed (it never matched in situation mode, silently hiding the block); SECTION_DEFS gained entries for `returning-user-first-turn-situation`, the two post-confirm blocks, and the compressed EARLIER ENTRIES block; and the extraction-brief header regex corrected to the real runtime header (`── BRIEF FOR YOUR NEXT RESPONSE ──`). Read-only admin tool; no user-facing change. **(3) docs(architecture) — new reference doc set.** `docs/architecture/master.md` (the engine described around its six actual components — monitor, extraction, selector, generator, composer, Manual — plus the `master.html` visual), `phase-0-closeout.md` (the Phase 0 monitor verdict: promote to Phase 1, see In-Flight below), `prompt-constructor-delta.md` (what the admin page shows vs what the constructor actually does), and ADR-043 (the three Lock 1 blocker decisions: pre-prompt selector, Surface ripeness threshold, drop the cross-context gate). **Tests.** Full suite 1055/1055 passing on merged main (branch alone was 1052; +3 from the infra-guard work already on main). Net +7 in `persona-pipeline.test.ts`: 7 new charged-material-gate cases plus a flipped null-state fail-closed proof, after repairing 7 fixtures that had leaned on the old boolean and hardening 3 muddied tests to isolate their intended variable (one of which — "first-checkpoint gate fails when neither mechanism nor link" — had silently never tested its named behavior). Build clean. **Commits.** `c8da4ec` docs(architecture), `2706670` fix(admin), `9b25205` feat(persona) Lock 1. Merge SHA `d8acaf2`.

- Infrastructure-question guardrail in Jove prompt (2026-05-25, claude/infra-question-guard branch): Follow-up #2 to the retry-storm incident — closes the prompt-side issue identified during that debug session. **Motivation.** When credits ran out and the user asked Jove "what happened to our chat, the history is gone," Jove first responded reasonably ("Still here. The history is intact on my end..."). But when the user pressed ("i don't see anything before..."), Jove confabulated a plausible-sounding technical reason: "I can't push the history back to your screen. That's a platform limitation." The history wasn't actually lost — Jove just didn't have visibility into the app's database, message history loading, or session routing, and instead of saying so, invented an answer. **Fix.** New `APP AND PLATFORM QUESTIONS` section added to Tier 3's always-rendering `clinical-and-tail` block in `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts:716-717`, placed right after `FABRICATED CONTENT` because the posture is identical: "you can't see X — say so." The block tells Jove: (a) what it doesn't have access to (database, message history, session routing, conversation lists, anything platform-level), (b) the forbidden move (don't speculate, don't fabricate platform limitations, don't invent technical reasons to fill the gap), (c) the right move (point them at the team and stay with the work), and (d) the exact worked example from the incident — both the line Jove DID say that triggered this fix and the corrected version it should have said. **Why the worked example.** The pin in the test asserts the literal "I can't push the history back to your screen, that's a platform limitation" string appears in the prompt as a do-NOT example. A future edit that softens this rule has to confront the exact confabulated line that motivated it — making the regression cost visible at edit time, not at user-impact time. **Tests.** Three new tests in the existing `fabricated content guard` describe-adjacent block in `system-prompt.test.ts:998-1029`: section header present, "Do not fabricate platform limitations" instruction pinned, both worked examples (the do-NOT and the correct-version) pinned. Tier 3 snapshot tests in `system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap` regenerated for the 16 block-firing snapshots that include the always-rendering tail — legitimate edge case of adding new prompt text. Full suite 1048/1048 passing (was 1045 — net +3 from the new tests). Build clean. **What this does NOT cover.** Jove's behavior when the user reports the SAME class of issue (lost history, broken UI, can't see X) on a third or fourth turn. The guardrail is a "don't confabulate" rule, not a redirect-to-support flow. If users start reporting that Jove repeatedly responds with "I can't see into the app" turn after turn, we'd want to add an escalation path that points at a feedback/support surface. Deferred until we see real cases. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.ts` (+3: new section in the clinical-and-tail block, right after FABRICATED CONTENT), `src/lib/persona/system-prompt.test.ts` (+31: three pinning tests with the worked-example assertion), `src/lib/persona/__snapshots__/system-prompt.tier3.test.ts.snap` (+48: 16 snapshots regenerated). Merge SHA `51b4e78`.

- Chat retry-storm fix — X-Conversation-Id header + user message dedup (2026-05-25, claude/fix-retry-storm branch): Two bugs on the chat send path closed in one ship, both root-caused to a real production incident the same morning when Anthropic credits ran out and a single user typing during the failure window produced 8 ghost conversation rows + 8 duplicate user message rows. **Diagnosis came from live data.** Five user-facing screenshots plus SQL inspection of the actual `conversations` and `messages` tables identified the bugs precisely: 6 dup `conversations` rows with 0 messages each created between 09:23 and 09:40 (Fix A's smoking gun), and 8 identical "i don't know..." user rows in conversation `c9972767` inserted between 09:22:27 and 09:22:58 with no assistant message between them (Fix B's smoking gun). The incident is documented in this commit's message and worth reading for future debugging. **Fix A — conversation continuity across retries.** Client previously only learned its `conversationId` from the `message_complete` SSE event. When the Anthropic call failed before that event fired (credit error, network drop, abort), the client retried with `conversationId: null` and the server created a brand-new conversation row. Fix: server now sets `X-Conversation-Id` on the SSE Response headers in `src/app/api/chat/route.ts:178-191` right after the conversation id is known and BEFORE `callPersona` is invoked. The client reads it off `res.headers` as soon as `fetch()` resolves in `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts:603-614`, before any failure branching, and captures it into `conversationId` state via `setConversationId`. Retries now carry the real id forward via the request body and the server reuses the existing conversation instead of creating a ghost. **Fix B — user message dedup on retry.** `retryLastMessage()` in `useChat.ts:706-718` only pops the user message from client state; it never deletes the prior user row from the DB. Each manual retry called `sendMessage` which inserted a fresh user message row, accumulating duplicates in the same conversation. Fix: new exported `findRetryStormDuplicate()` helper in `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts:241-294` looks back 30 seconds for an identical user message in the same conversation. If one exists AND no assistant message followed it, treat the new attempt as a retry — reuse the existing row instead of inserting a duplicate. The "no subsequent assistant" check is the discrimination test: a user who genuinely sends the same message twice after Jove answered gets both rows kept; a retry storm collapses into one. callPersona's step 1 in `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts:368-401` now delegates to the helper. **Why a server-side dedup rather than client-side coalescing.** Server-side is the boundary that protects the DB regardless of which client path triggered the duplicate — manual retry, double-click on Send, two browser tabs racing, future SMS retry, an offline-queue replay. Client-side coalescing only protects the path that ships with it. **Why 30 seconds.** Long enough to catch a frustrated user mashing Retry across several network round trips; short enough that legitimate "send the same thing again" intent (typically minutes apart, with a Jove response between) is preserved. The window is exposed as `RETRY_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS` constant + `windowMs` parameter on the helper for future tuning. **Why no migration.** Both fixes are pure code — no schema change. The dedup is a runtime query against the existing `messages` table. **Tests.** Two new tests in `route.test.ts` (header present on new-conversation creation and on existing-conversation reuse) and four new tests in `call-persona.test.ts` covering `findRetryStormDuplicate` (no dup → null; dup, no assistant → reuse id; dup, assistant followed → null; custom windowMs parameter accepted). Full suite 1045/1045 passing (was 1039 — net +6). Build clean. **What this does NOT fix.** The 8 ghost conversations + 8 duplicate user rows already in your DB from this morning's incident remain. They're harmless (the ghosts are 0-message rows; the dups are unreachable from the UI's standpoint as long as the active conversation is the right one) but a cleanup SQL would be cheap and worth doing in a follow-up. Also out of scope: the Jove-confabulating-infrastructure-answers issue (Jove told the user "I can't push the history back to your screen. That's a platform limitation" when the history wasn't actually lost — a prompt-side change, separate fix). **Files.** `src/app/api/chat/route.ts` (+8: `X-Conversation-Id` header with explanatory comment pointing at the incident), `src/lib/hooks/useChat.ts` (+13: header capture in the fetch resolution path with explanatory comment), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+89/-15: exported `findRetryStormDuplicate` helper + step 1 refactored to use it), `src/app/api/chat/route.test.ts` (+29: two header tests), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.test.ts` (+95: `MockAdmin` test helper + four dedup tests). Merge SHA `1318eb9`.

- Design-system cleanup + Agents & Skills admin page + senior-engineer subagent (2026-05-25, direct-to-main): Three coordinated changes shipped together as the ratified output of a senior-engineer subagent audit + verification cycle. **(1) New `senior-engineer` subagent.** Project-scoped subagent at `.claude/agents/senior-engineer.md` for change review (default mode) and codebase audit (on request). Reads `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/rules.md`, `docs/system.md`, `docs/decisions.md`, `docs/intent.md`, plus its own Field notes section as the disciplines it measures against. Tools: Read / Grep / Glob / Bash. Model: opus. The Field notes section accumulates confirmed facts about this codebase across reviews — each new entry is proposed at the end of a review under "Proposed field note" and only lands after founder ratification. Initial ratified entries cover the design-tokens hex-regex gap, state.md token-name drift, and dark-mode-redesign orphan files. **(2) Design-system cleanup.** Senior-engineer Mode B codebase audit surfaced 6 should-fix and 2 consider-level findings; this ship lands all of them except SF2 (admin panel consolidation, deferred). **SF1** — defined `--session-card-shadow` token; light gets the spec value from `docs/implementation/light-mode.md:102` (`0 5px 14px rgba(31,20,10,0.08), 0 1px 3px rgba(31,20,10,0.06)`), dark stays `none` to preserve documented border-only treatment. Three consumers (AppearanceToggle, SessionDrawer, MobileCrisis) were reading `var(--session-card-shadow, none)` against a token that never landed. **SF3** — deleted 4 dead files (`NavIcons.tsx`, `ExploreWithPersonaButton.tsx`, `FeedbackPanel.tsx`, `useKeyboardOpen.ts`), removed now-empty `src/components/icons/` directory, dropped `FeedbackPanel` from the design-tokens ALLOWLIST. **SF4** — deleted 7 dead tokens (`--session-bubble-user`, `--session-bubble-user-border`, `--session-button-inset-strong`, `--session-glow-cp`, `--session-hair-faint`, `--session-ink-user`, `--session-slate-soft`) plus stale comment fragments. `--session-vignette` HELD pending human visual call on `DesktopVitrine.tsx:101` (which has a hardcoded literal that drifted from the token). **SF5** — replaced hex literal `#EFEADF` in `UserProfilePane.tsx:321` with a new `--session-cream-bright` token (light: `#EFEADF`, dark: `#1A1C24`) so the checkpoint card gradient now theme-flips correctly. Previously rendered nearly-black → white in dark mode (broken). **SF6** — dropped stale ALLOWLIST entry for `prompt-architecture/page.tsx` (file has zero literals today, exemption was dead). **C1** — fixed token-name drift in this `state.md`'s 2026-05-12 light-mode entry: `--session-bubble-jove` → `--session-jove-bg`, `--session-plate` → `--session-walnut-surface-soft` (neither shipped under the original names; both alpha values match the corrected tokens exactly). Other historical references to tokens deleted this session left intact — they were accurate at the time of the original ship log; rewriting them would erode the log's purpose as a record. **C2** — removed duplicate effects from `ChatWindowModal` and `PatternFormingModal`. Both modals had been reimplementing body-scroll lock, focus save/restore, and Escape handling on top of `<Modal>` (which already provides them). Removed the duplicates. Kept button-focus-on-open (intentionally overrides Modal's default plate-focus, since these modals have a single primary action button) and the Tab trap (Modal doesn't provide). Source-string tests updated to assert delegation to `Modal.tsx` for the moved behaviors. **(3) Admin `/skills` page extended and renamed.** API route at `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts` extended to scan `.claude/agents/*.md` and `~/.claude/agents/*.md` in addition to commands and skills — auto-discovery, no manual list to maintain. Page heading and nav rail label updated from "Skills" to "Agents & Skills"; URL kept at `/admin/skills` so existing links don't break. Two new scope categories surface in the UI: Project agent (with senior-engineer as the first entry) and User agent (currently empty). Pre-existing `id`-derivation bug fixed in the API while extending it — old logic returned the parent-directory name `"commands"` as the id for every slash command (invisible today because the UI keys by `${scope}-${id}` and only two commands exist, but would have caused expand-toggle collisions at any scale). Agent invocation field is `null` (subagents invoked via natural language or `/agents` menu, not slash). The `.claude/skills/replay-monitor/SKILL.md` gained YAML frontmatter (`name` + `description`) so the page shows useful info instead of the body-heading-derived "Replay Monitor". **Verification.** Senior-engineer verification pass (a second invocation of the agent against the first pass's findings) confirmed 7 of 8 findings with one scope correction on SF2 (ActiveUsersPanel uses different opacities than the other three cloned panels — different visual primitive, same hardcoded sage RGB family). Build clean. 1039/1039 tests pass. **Risks deferred for human verification.** SF2 (admin health-panel consolidation + sage-token drift in "Clean" banners — 4 cloned panels + 1 missed `SchemaHealthTab` + 1 stats-grid variant) deferred to a focused follow-up PR; needs browser walkthrough in both themes. C2 modal refactor needs keyboard-only navigation check in both themes (focus restoration is subtle). SF1 light-mode card lift on Settings appearance toggle, drawer active row, and Crisis card hasn't been visually verified. SF4 `--session-vignette` decision (adopt token at `DesktopVitrine.tsx:101` or delete the token — `state.md` log says it was adopted but live code disagrees) needs human visual call. **Branch shape.** Worktree state at session start showed a feature branch `claude/phase-0-shadow-monitor` whose work had already been merged to main earlier in the day; effective ship is direct-to-main on top of `4551057`. Three atomic commits: `7f3e39c` `chore(agents): add senior-engineer subagent`, `2902562` `refactor(design-system): clean dead code, dead tokens, modal duplication`, `6cad334` `feat(admin/skills): show subagents alongside skills, rename to Agents & Skills`. **Files.** `.claude/agents/senior-engineer.md` (new, 123 lines), `.claude/skills/replay-monitor/SKILL.md` (+5: frontmatter), `src/app/globals.css` (+11/-40: dead-token removal + new `--session-card-shadow` + new `--session-cream-bright`), `src/components/admin/UserProfilePane.tsx` (+1/-1: hex → token), `src/components/modals/ChatWindowModal.tsx` / `PatternFormingModal.tsx` (-66 net: dup-effect removal), `src/components/modals/ChatWindowModal.test.ts` / `PatternFormingModal.test.ts` (+30/-12: assertions repointed at `Modal.tsx`), `src/lib/design-tokens.test.ts` (-4: ALLOWLIST cleanup), `src/components/admin/FeedbackPanel.tsx` / `src/components/icons/NavIcons.tsx` / `src/components/shared/ExploreWithPersonaButton.tsx` / `src/lib/hooks/useKeyboardOpen.ts` (all deleted, -363 lines), `src/app/admin/skills/page.tsx` (+22/-4: agent scopes + heading rename), `src/app/api/admin/skills/route.ts` (+33/-7: agent scanners + id fix), `src/components/admin/AdminNavRail.tsx` (+1/-1: label rename), `docs/state.md` (+1/-1: C1 token-name fix). Net `+235 / -483` across 17 files.

- Phase 0 follow-ups — Opus flip, replay harness, /replay-monitor skill (2026-05-21, claude/phase-0-shadow-monitor branch): Two follow-ups on the Phase 0 shadow monitor that just shipped. **(1) MONITOR_MODEL flipped from `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` to `claude-opus-4-7`.** Phase 0 is a ceiling test: we want to know whether rupture / withdrawal / sinking detection is POSSIBLE at the best model we can throw at the problem. If Opus catches the signal, we know how far Haiku has to climb when we cost-optimize later. If Opus misses, Haiku won't catch it either. Live production turns now hit Opus on the monitor pre-call; the rest of the pipeline (extraction Sonnet, Jove Sonnet, classifier Haiku, composer Opus) is unchanged. **(2) Replay harness `scripts/replay-monitor.ts`.** A CLI that runs the real `runMonitor()` over a stored transcript, accumulating turn by turn — for a transcript with N user turns, runs the monitor N times with progressively larger history slices and prints the read at each step. Plain-text transcript format (USER:/JOVE: prefixes, multi-line allowed, `#` comments stripped) or JSON. Output is per-turn blocks + summary table to stdout; **never writes to `public.monitor_reads`** so handcrafted replays don't contaminate the live shadow dataset that drives the Phase 0 verdict. Imports `runMonitor` directly — same prompt, same model, same parser as production. The one engine touch is a backwards-compatible opt-in `fullHistory?: boolean` flag on `runMonitor`'s options that bypasses the 8-message window (defaults false; live wiring never sets it). Flags: `--full-history`, `--compare` (run both passes + divergence table), `--json`, `--out=<path>`. **(3) `/replay-monitor` skill at `.claude/skills/replay-monitor/SKILL.md`.** Non-technical wrapper so Jeff doesn't need to manage files or terminal commands. He types `/replay-monitor` and pastes a transcript inline; the skill saves it to `scripts/transcripts/replay-<timestamp>.txt`, runs the harness via Bash, renders the output in chat, and adds a one-or-two sentence read of what the monitor caught. Skill enforces "no live data," "use the real runMonitor," and rejects malformed transcripts before spending Opus tokens. **(4) `vitest.config.ts` include glob.** Extended from `src/**/*.test.ts` to also include `scripts/**/*.test.ts` so the harness parser tests (`scripts/replay-monitor.test.ts`, 15 cases covering both transcript formats + the cumulative-slice builder) run as part of `npm test`. **What this does NOT change.** Live conversation behavior is unchanged on the new code path — `fireBackgroundMonitor` still calls `runMonitor` exactly as before, just with Opus instead of Haiku as the model. No extraction changes, no checkpoint pipeline changes, no voice prompt changes. ADRs 001 / 002 / 017 / 020 all preserved. **Tests** 1039 passing (was 1021 — +18 net: 3 monitor tests for the fullHistory flag + 15 harness parser tests). **Build** clean. **Cost shift to watch.** Opus is ~5× Haiku per token. The monitor's input is small (≤8 messages) and output is capped at 256 tokens, so per-call cost stays in the cents range. If the Phase 0 verdict comes back "promote," we flip back to Haiku for cost — or to Sonnet if Opus's signal can't be matched by Haiku and we want to pay the gap. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/config.ts` (+8/-4: MONITOR_MODEL flip + new ceiling-test comment), `src/lib/persona/monitor.ts` (+10/-3: opt-in fullHistory flag + window-skip branch), `src/lib/persona/monitor.test.ts` (+38/-10: 2 new flag tests + 1 model-pin relaxation), `scripts/replay-monitor.ts` (new, 568 lines), `scripts/replay-monitor.test.ts` (new, 156 lines), `scripts/transcripts/example.txt` (new, 39 lines — "sinking user" example), `.claude/skills/replay-monitor/SKILL.md` (new, 39 lines), `vitest.config.ts` (+1/-1: `scripts/**/*.test.ts` added to include glob). Merge SHA `db9cf35`.

- Phase 0 shadow monitor — per-turn alliance read, log-only (2026-05-21, claude/phase-0-shadow-monitor branch): First slice of the two-layer engine plan (`docs/reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md` + `docs/reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md`, proposed ADR-044). Adds a Haiku pre-call that runs on every web turn alongside extraction and writes a structured alliance read — bond holding, task agreed, scope (in_scope / drifting / out_of_scope), rupture (none / withdrawal / confrontation), direction (steadying / drifting / sinking) — to a new admin-read-only table `public.monitor_reads`. **Log-only. Does not gate any behavior in this phase.** The goal is to collect a week of shadow data, eyeball 20-30 transcripts against the structured outputs, and decide whether the classifier reliably catches withdrawal/sinking before promoting it onto the critical path. **What runs.** `runMonitor()` in `src/lib/persona/monitor.ts` calls `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` with a focused system prompt (reads the alliance, not the topic), 8-message window, max 256 output tokens, JSON-only output with an adversarial parser that returns null on any shape error. `fireBackgroundMonitor()` in `persona-pipeline.ts` mirrors `fireBackgroundExtraction`: `waitUntil` from `@vercel/functions` keeps the function alive until the call settles, structured `[persona-pipeline] monitor_attempt` / `monitor_failed` log lines bucket failures by `error_class` (abort / parse / response_shape / http / other), DB write only on success — failures preserve the in-flight turn intact. Wired from `call-persona.ts` step 3 next to extraction; gated on `hasUserContent` exactly like extraction so opener turns and synthetic `[Session started]` placeholders don't fire. **Web only.** SMS persona-bridge intentionally does NOT call the monitor (Q-5 in the evaluation report — keep latency surface area small while validating). **Persistence.** New migration `20260521120000_add_monitor_reads.sql` creates a dedicated table keyed by conversation + user with the five enum/boolean read columns (CHECK-enforced to mirror the TS union types — drift here surfaces as INSERT failure), telemetry (model, input_tokens, output_tokens, latency_ms, turn_index), reason text, and indexes for the two queries we'll actually run (per-conversation timeline + global direction filter). Admin-read RLS policy via `public.is_admin()`; service-role bypass for writes; no end-user surface. **Why a new table vs extending `extraction_state` JSONB.** Shadow data needs to be cleanly droppable when the experiment concludes; mixing it into production extraction state would have made cleanup ambiguous and admin queries awkward. Table is structured-rowed for SQL ergonomics. **Why background vs blocking-with-cap.** Phase 0 wants maximum data volume with zero TTFT impact. The blocking-with-1500ms-cap pattern (Q-6 in the evaluation) is what Phase 2 needs when monitor output starts shaping the generator's posture; it's deliberately not in Phase 0. **Latency posture.** Zero impact on the user-visible turn. The monitor's roundtrip happens inside `waitUntil`, which Vercel keeps alive past the parent response close — same plumbing extraction uses since the 2026-04-21 hardening pass. **What this does NOT change.** Extraction unchanged. Checkpoint pipeline unchanged. Voice prompt unchanged (still 21 base rules with the three timer-based forced moves intact — Phase 3 of the plan addresses those). `validateResponseStructure` unchanged. ADRs 001 (parallel extraction), 002 (one-turn lag), and 017 (fire-and-forget) all preserved — the monitor is a new third background call, not a reordering of the existing two. **Tests.** 16 new tests in `monitor.test.ts`: happy path, every malformed-JSON parse branch, every enum boundary cross-product, markdown-fence strip, 8-message window trim, model identity + cache-control assertions. Full suite 1021/1021 passing (was 1005 — +16 net). Build clean. **What to do with the data after deploy.** After a week of traffic, query patterns: `select created_at, conversation_id, direction, rupture, reason from monitor_reads where direction = 'sinking' and created_at > now() - interval '7 days' order by created_at desc` to surface candidate withdrawal moments; per-conversation slope via `select turn_index, direction, rupture, scope, reason from monitor_reads where conversation_id = X order by turn_index`. Cross-reference against `messages` for the original transcripts; eyeball 20-30 and write up the verdict (Phase 0 exit criteria in `two-layer-engine-evaluation.md` § 3). **Cost note.** One extra Haiku call per web turn; output capped at 256 tokens. Order of pennies per session — fine for a week of shadow collection. If the Phase 0 verdict is "promote," cost is the floor we already accepted. If "rework," roll back is `git revert` + drop the table; data is opt-in admin-only so no user-facing reversal needed. **Files.** `src/lib/persona/monitor.ts` (new, 215 lines), `src/lib/persona/monitor.test.ts` (new, 179 lines), `src/lib/persona/persona-pipeline.ts` (+88: fireBackgroundMonitor helper + classifyMonitorError), `src/lib/persona/call-persona.ts` (+19/-3: capture user-message id from step 1, fire monitor in step 3 next to extraction), `src/lib/persona/config.ts` (+4: MONITOR_MODEL constant), `supabase/migrations/20260521120000_add_monitor_reads.sql` (new, 62 lines), `docs/reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md` (new, 478 lines — the scoping/collision report), `docs/reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md` (new, 72 lines — proposed ADR-044 awaiting decision). Merge SHA `3b77673`.

- Upload mode — pre-beta audit: locked opener, recoverable errors, hardened detection (2026-05-21, upload-mode-prebeta-fixes branch): Pre-beta audit of Upload mode surfaced three blockers and four should-fixes. All seven ship in this change. **B1 — returning-user opener was a model-driven "locked" invitation that wasn't actually locked.** UPLOAD_OPENER ("Paste something here. A text thread, an email chain, a journal entry, notes you wrote to yourself…") was supposed to render verbatim per ADR-042 §3, but live verification showed Jove substituting a generic returning-user opener ("Whatever's been sitting with you since last time. Or something new.") that dropped the format inventory entirely. Fix: server-emit UPLOAD_OPENER directly from `call-persona.ts` step 2a — no Anthropic call, no model variance. Extracted `shouldEmitUploadOpener` predicate for unit coverage. The OPENER section of the upload Tier 3 block is removed and the `UPLOAD_OPENER` import dropped from `system-prompt.ts` (block now exists solely to frame the paste turn). **Catch from live verification, real save.** Initial predicate gated on `turnCount === 0`. Live test showed it never fired. Trace: `loadConversationContext` injects a synthetic `[Session started]` placeholder user message when the DB has zero rows, bumping `turnCount` from 0 to 1 (see `persona-pipeline.ts:150-152`). Predicate corrected to `turnCount <= 1` with comments + regression test pinning both 0 and 1 cases. Without the live integration test, this would have shipped silently broken. **B2 — 16k oversize paste was unrecoverable.** Pasting >MAX_UPLOAD_LENGTH (16,000 chars) returned a 400 with the actual error string `"Upload is too long. Please keep uploads under 16,000 characters."` but `useChat.ts` swallowed the body and showed "Something went wrong. Try again." The textarea cleared on send, the optimistic user bubble was dropped, and the user had no way to recover their paste. Fix: parse 400 JSON body, surface the server's `error` field as the on-screen message, expose `draftToRestore` + `clearDraftToRestore` from `useChat`, plumbed through `MainApp → MobileSession → ChatInput`. `ChatInput` consumes the signal via a `useEffect` that rehydrates the textarea, focuses it, and places the cursor at the end. **B3 — `wrapPastedContent` injection defense had a gap on Upload pastes.** The wrap fired only when `detectTranscript` classified the message as a transcript. A short journal entry (<100 chars), a paste without a date header, or any content the regex missed reached the model unwrapped — even though the user had explicitly clicked Upload, which is a stronger signal than regex inference. Fix: in `call-persona.ts`, force-wrap when `mode === "upload" && turnCount === 2` (the paste turn — bootstrap is short-circuited at turnCount<=1, and conversational replies at turnCount>=4 stay unwrapped to avoid wrapping non-paste content). **S1 — dead "low confidence" hedge block removed.** `system-prompt.ts`'s `renderTranscriptContextBlock` had a branch that fired when `transcriptContext.confidence === "low"`. The detector returns `confidence: "low"` only when `isTranscript: false` (every message under 100 chars and every long-but-no-signals message). The hedge text ("The user's message is unusually long or structured. It may be pasted content…") was wrong for short messages and noise for long ones. Deleted with a comment explaining why. **S2 — `detectTranscript` false-positive tightened.** The "≥10 lines, avg <80 chars" fallback signal previously counted as a standalone signal sufficient to mark `isTranscript: true`. A heavily line-broken emotional monologue ("I keep replaying it.\nLike, every single beat.\nWhat I said.\nWhat he said back.\n…") tripped it. Refactor: split signals into `strongSignals` (format-specific detectors — email / speaker-alternating / timestamped / journal) and `corroboratingShortLines` (the fallback). Strong signal alone → isTranscript with `medium` confidence. Strong signal + corroborating fallback → `high`. Fallback alone → NO_TRANSCRIPT. Added adversarial long-narrative test case + iMessage-with-short-lines positive case. **S3+S4 — test coverage on length cap + upload suppression.** S3: added 6 cases to `route.test.ts` — 16k exact / 16k+1 reject / 8k accepted in upload / 4k+1 still rejected in situation / follow-up reads mode from DB with upload conv accepting 8k / follow-up rejects 4k+1 when DB mode is situation. Extended the admin-client mock with `mockConvModeFromDb` so the `from("conversations").select("mode").eq("id", X).single()` path returns a mode lookup distinct from the insert-and-select-id path. S4: extracted `selectTranscriptContextForPrompt` as a pure helper from `call-persona.ts` step 7b, with 5 unit cases pinning that upload mode zeros transcriptContext even when `isTranscript: true`. **Tests** 1005 passing (was 994 before this change — net +11 across the new predicate, suppression-helper, length-cap, and transcript false-positive tests). **Build** clean. **Live verification** in dev (`devtest@test.com`): fresh upload bootstrap returns UPLOAD_OPENER verbatim as a single `message_complete` event with zero `text_delta` events (confirming the LLM is not called); 16,500-char paste surfaces the specific "Upload is too long…" error and rehydrates the 16,500 chars into the textarea so the user can edit down. **Audit findings deferred to follow-up (nice-to-have, not shipped here).** N1 (16k cap applies to all turns of upload conversations, not just the paste) — would need turn-aware capping; N2 (the "16,000" string in the error message is hardcoded separate from `MAX_UPLOAD_LENGTH = 16000`); N3 (two independent DB lookups for `conversations.mode` — one for the length-cap check in `route.ts`, one in `loadConversationContext`); N4 (`UPLOAD_OPENER` doesn't say "text only" — ADR-042 §9 defers file/image/PDF support, so the copy could pre-empt the obvious "can I attach an image?" question). **ADR-042 implementation notes.** §3 ("opens with a locked invitation") is now actually locked — server-emitted, model variance eliminated. §5–§6 (shared paste template + injection wrap) unchanged in shape;
