- **Thin-entry investigation — `language_bank` bleed fix shipped; timing fix parked** (2026-06-29, merge `d8305ea`). Diagnostic thread (3 dogfood runs against frozen build `3eb223d`, all snapshotted) on why guided-intake entries read thin. **Core finding (confirmed 3/3, NOT yet fixed):** the checkpoint fires on *pattern-nameability* (`has_mechanism` + `pattern_engaged` + `depth: mechanism`), and in every run that moment landed on the FINAL turn, coinciding with the live edge opening (the user's ambivalence / "what do I actually want"). The save captures the stable diagnosis and drops the open edge, then offers to stop — the thin entry is a SYMPTOM; the disease is the conversation ending right as it gets useful. Ruled out along the way: a composer/function-development fix (function develops fine — run 2's payoff was in the title) and hard-gating cost (rejected as fabrication-inducing for an alexithymia/masking population, per applied-psych eval — `user_named_cost` stays a soft hint). **The timing fix is unscoped and PARKED** until the measurement path is de-contaminated. **SHIPPED this merge — the bleed fix (prompt-only, `extraction.ts`):** the extractor sees the full Manual by design (for threading), but "be aggressive about capturing language" had it copying prior entries' charged phrases into the new conversation's `language_bank`, so the charged-material gate counted accumulated Manual charge as live ripeness (proven in run 3 — 11 of 15 bank phrases were foreign to the gym conversation). Currently firewalled by the gate's layer-scoping (`builtOnCharged` only counts phrases on the candidate layer) and capped by the live-mechanism requirement — so a passenger, not the driver, in these runs — but it COMPOUNDS on same-section returns and corrupts measurement, which is why it goes first (hygiene, not severity). Fix makes `language_bank` live-transcript-only; the Manual stays as threading context, not a bank candidate (source-keyed, so a phrase the user re-says live is still captured). No schema/gate/merge change; threading preserved (Manual still injected → brief + composer). **Verification pending (needs prod):** next dogfood run in a section that already has entries → the gating snapshot's `language_bank` should carry live phrases only. **Parked/open:** (a) the timing fix itself — scope only after the bleed fix is verified clean; (b) **Hole #1** — what Jove does if pushed PAST the checkpoint into an open edge, never tested (you stopped at the save all 3 runs); needed to scope the post-checkpoint half of the timing fix; (c) `user_named_cost`/`user_named_stance` boolean bleed — non-gating hints, NOT addressed by this fix (watch on the verification run); (d) tracker temperature is unset (~1.0 default) — pin extraction to 0 deferred to the eventual timing-fix config lock; (e) origin policy (a "received-not-pursued" brake, applied-psych-recommended, needs a clinician sanity-check) undecided; (f) `manual_entries.source_message_id`→snapshot join is off-by-one — the gating snapshot lives on the assistant message *before* the `is_checkpoint` message, so a naive join returns NULL (fix eventually).
- **Reflection meter (user-pulled checkpoints) — built behind a flag, OFF** (2026-06-18, merge `a3bb7af`; spec `docs/depth-meter-spec.md`, mockup `docs/mockups/depth-meter-mockup.html`). Replaces the Jove-pushed checkpoint trigger with a user-pulled "Reflection": a brass depth meter in the chat fills from the existing extraction `depth` signal; at full the USER pulls the entry (bloom → persistent top strip after "Not yet" → straight to the existing review overlay), and Jove stops auto-proposing. Gated by the new `reflection_meter` feature flag (**defaults OFF, fails closed** — the current Jove-pushed flow is byte-for-byte unchanged until an admin flips it: Admin → Feature gates → "Reflection meter"; no migration needed, the gate row upserts on first toggle). Reuse-first: `checkpointsEnabled` collapses under the gate to suppress proposals via the existing tested "checkpoints OFF" path; composes on demand via new `POST /api/checkpoint/compose` reusing `composeManualEntry` + the existing confirm route + overlay (net AI calls DOWN — composes only on real pulls). Composer `checkpointText` made optional with a user-pull framing; transcript window widened 8→50 (compose latency is output-bound, so the window is not a timeout factor — the earlier load-test worry is resolved). Senior-eng change-review: no blockers; added compose double-tap idempotency + consolidated the anon conversion gate into a shared `anon-checkpoint-gate` helper. Naming: **"Reflection"** (pending a `rules.md` amendment — "moment" / "reflection card" are currently banned synonyms). **Pre-enable gate before flipping ON:** a crisis defer→crisis integration test + a human safety sign-off on the one-turn extraction lag (the meter/strip hide on `reflectionMeter: null`, read from `previousExtraction`) — task chip open. Renders on **both mobile and desktop** (the desktop shell reuses `MobileSession`, so the meter shows there too — an earlier "desktop doesn't render it" note was wrong). **Restore-on-load follow-up** (2026-06-18, merge `d1a983c`): the meter was live-turn-only, so it started blank on a page refresh, a drawer switch, or a dev-simulator run (those land via DB reload, not a live SSE stream — this is why the simulator showed nothing). New `GET /api/checkpoint/meter` rehydrates the meter from the persisted extraction state on every conversation open, reusing `loadConversationContext` + `applyCheckpointGates` (the same readiness computation as the live path — no second formula). **Capture-progress reframe** (2026-06-18, merge `bb49c74`): the bar was a raw depth gauge, so it stayed ~full after a save (you can't capture again during the cooldown, but it didn't reset). Reframed so "full" means "capturable now": the fill is computed server-side (`reflectionMeterFill`) capped by the post-checkpoint cooldown — `turnsSinceCheckpoint` is 0 right after a save → fill 0 → it ramps back up over the cooldown, also capped by depth. The signal carries `{fill, ready}` (was `{depth}`); the client renders the server fill, and a latched-ready state always shows full so the bar and the bloom can't disagree. **Ready-visual fit-and-finish** (2026-06-26, merge `416471c`, from Jeff's review of the live flow): the bar and the ready state reworked. Bar 2→3px with a fill-glow that grows as it climbs, and a client-side **monotonic high-water mark** (`bumpReflectionFill` in `useChat`) so a shallower later turn no longer drags it backward — it only resets on the real events (save → 0; new-conversation/crisis → hidden), mirroring the readiness latch. Ready state rebuilt from centered loose text into a **distinct walnut card**: a fixed tee-up ("We've got enough here for a reflection worth keeping.") plus the one-sentence `current_thread` as the gist — plumbed as a new `preview` field on the `{fill, ready}` meter signal (live `call-persona` + restore `meter` route), reusing extraction's existing `current_thread` (**no new model call**; empty → tee-up alone). Interaction: the composer now **locks while the card is up** (the "chat stops" on ready) — "Not yet" flies the card up into the persistent top strip (new `reflectionFlyUp` keyframe; reduced-motion falls back to the instant swap) and re-opens the composer; "Build" opens the review overlay **immediately in a building state**, then fills in the composed entry in place (was: dead wait until compose returned). Still gated OFF; build + 1215 tests green. Naming watch unchanged ("reflection card" still pending the `rules.md` amendment). **Reliability fix (2026-06-30, merge `7d4c9be`):** after a user-pulled reflection was confirmed, the saved card + Jove's follow-up didn't reliably show in the live thread (thread relied on the SSE stream as sole source of truth; everything was already persisted). Fixed with a post-confirm `reconcileThread` DB re-read — see ship log.

Declined by founder (Complexity Gate): codifying the two-questions-pick-one exception (model already does it); an em-dash rule (overfit risk — watch item). **Soak iteration 2 shipped** (2026-06-10, merge `e934fe7`, from Jeff's review of the Kevin checkpoint): MECHANICS gains the depth bar (discovery-not-summary; walk one moment fully before proposing; scale claim to evidence — one instance = scoped read, not a trait) — the Tier-3 trim had cut the old checkpoint block's ripeness rules too deep, and a checkpoint fired off one partial instance as a trait-level summary. Returning-user opener now names the callback as a Manual entry by title (vague topic allusion read as confusion). Held (Complexity Gate): composer single-example softener edit — it under-fired once on thin material; trigger to revisit is an overclaiming headline on well-evidenced material. **Soak iteration 3 shipped** (2026-06-10, merge `788d588`): MECHANICS checkpoint section REWRITTEN (replace, not accrete) from the good-checkpoint standard — a 4-agent pass (applied-psychologist standard + transcript forensics on the three live exchanges, design, adversarial replay) defined the bar (a checkpoint = a reorganization the user did NOT arrive with; arc account→scene→mechanism→bind→self-recognition) and produced the walk-then-earn text: walking-not-harvesting, the never-said test before proposing (one operation carrying discovery vs summary + claim scaling + anchor-to-their-question), brought-to-file carve-out against over-withholding. Returning opener: callback is a doorway, not the agenda — new topics take priority. Adversarial replay: exchange 1 still fires at the same turn; 2 and 3 prevented/redirected at the failing turn. Composer still held, now on TWO triggers (overclaiming headline on good evidence; flat/hedged entry from a visible reorganization). New watch: composer sees only the last 8 messages — longer walks may outrun its window (cheap widen if entries cite only late material). **Soak iteration 4 shipped** (2026-06-10, merge `6344d51`): four revisions from the applied-psych evaluation of exchange 4, re-cut under the new **soak-governance rule** (zero-sum prompt: only recurring failures or red lines earn lines; revise before add; ~1,000-word tripwire then hard justification; taste gets tolerance, not rules — Jeff's correction after the prescription set started re-growing the pile). Dropped under governance: elicit-first paragraph, nouns ban, bind mandate, banned-list wiring (watch: therapy-register tics recurring — first leak 'sit with that' tolerated). Voice ~1,015 words — past the tripwire; next addition needs the justification test. **Soak iteration 5 shipped** (2026-06-10, merge `8429c31`): returning-user opener made concise per Jeff — general session ask by default; entry callback (by title) only when the last session confirmed an entry (the 'Manual is rich' condition fired the identical callback template three sessions running). Paragraph ~140→~80 words; also drops the audit-flagged 'Don't worry about where you start' reassurance fallback. Load-delay question investigated, no bug: opener path is one parallel DB batch → Opus; rebuilt prompt is ~6× smaller than legacy; felt delay = Opus 4.7 TTFT + turn-1 cache-create (pre-existing WATCH). Diagnostic: opener should be the fastest turn type — if consistently slower than mid-conversation turns, instrument. Future lever if latency matters: openers on Sonnet. **Soak iteration 6 shipped** (2026-06-10, merge `0e05da7`) — the task-agreement fix, the largest conceptual change since the rebuild: situation-NAVIGATION now has a home in the voice beside pattern-finding. Root cause (applied-psych workflow, Bordin task/goal agreement): intent.md ranks navigate-this-situation as value #1 and rules.md permits the advisory work, but the rebuilt voice described only extraction — every conversation funneled to a checkpoint, which is why entries read as summaries (exchanges 2-3: extraction ran against a different user goal). CHARACTER: people are always after something (understanding / a decision / readiness / to be heard); Jove reads the goal-shape out loud early, lets them correct it, names drift before following it (Jeff-abstracted wording — no situation-type enumeration; replay-after-the-fact leads as the common arrival); navigation stands on its own, doesn't have to end in the Manual. MECHANICS: a navigated conversation that files nothing is a FINISHED conversation; building toward an entry is said out loud ('here's what I'm seeing; before I'd put it down I want to know if it holds anywhere else'). Six-bucket intent taxonomy recorded as design lens (witness / reorganize / ambivalence / readiness / apply / extract) — deliberately NOT encoded in the prompt, no mode UI, no intent classifier (scaffolding test: the model discriminates intents; it lacked license + legibility). Net ~+108 words past the tripwire; justification: #1 value absent from prompt = mechanism red-line, recurring (exchanges 2+3). Soak watch: the goal-read must not become a per-turn check-in ritual (once early + on drift only). **Soak iteration 7 shipped** (2026-06-11, merge `5818549`) — the agreement clause, from the resolution-unfolded intent grid (11 buckets: core six + options/verdict/practical-domain/validation/stop-the-loop): when the want isn't givable (verdict, legal/medical/financial answers, a guarantee), say so in one line and name what you can do instead — closes the practical-domain red cell (domain-advice freelancing had NO boundary; referral block is clinical-only) and completes the task-agreement mechanism as a posture, not an intake procedure. Also: navigation verbs restored lean (the iteration-6 abstract rewrite had dropped the HOW), and option-generation licensed ('name possibilities they haven't seen — as questions, theirs to take or leave'; rules.md permitted it, the voice omitted it, the lab script-leak was the shadow). Parked with triggers: validation/phantom-refusal (Phase-5 ND layer), loop-naming (first unnamed live loop), duplicate-entry guard (first duplicate proposal). Onboarding scope-setting line flagged as a product surface (Jeff's, unscheduled). **Soak iteration 8 shipped** (2026-06-11, merge `6ac2612`) — bring-the-user-along: a proactive orientation clause in the navigation paragraph (name where you've gotten and where you're headed, in their material), closing the gap that the reactive 'name the shift' clause didn't cover. Own sentence (no 'early on' timing inheritance — runs throughout, incl. near a checkpoint). Guardrail against technique-narration carried by a contrast PAIR not an abstract rule: orient in their material ('we came in on the city; the live thing is the values question') vs never narrate the move ('I'm going to reflect that back'). Decided against re-weaving PROGRESS into conversation text — that was deliberately moved to UI modals (PatternFormingModal/ChatWindowModal, /api/modal-progress), the deterministic + enforceable home; conversation carries findings+goals (substance), UI carries trajectory (state). HIGHEST backfire risk of the soak — sits a hair from the deleted process-narration register; first line to cut if sessions read chatty/meta. **Soak iteration 9 shipped** (2026-06-12, merge `0b90871`) — Tier A of the deeper-pattern design (from exchange 5's 'thin, not resonant' verdict + Jeff's premature-closure insight: the aha is the unseen dimension, not the first abstraction that fits). All extraction/composer side, zero voice budget: A1 brief no longer tops out at situational mechanism (asks for the driver, warns against settling a type before instances+exception test it, prompts the same-engine Manual check); A2 contrasts no longer discarded at extraction.ts:209 (the exception is what PINS the type — Kelly's triadic logic; fixes the dropped 'there are exceptions'); A3 Manual-threading un-gated from gateReady (prior entries surface DURING exploration, capped at 2, framed as same-engine test — was deadlocked: thread arrived only after descent ended); composer ALTITUDE-vs-softener contradiction resolved (altitude earned by evidence span; one scene + no recurrence claim = scene-scoped title; the Kevin-title root cause). HELD pending Tier-A soak: Tier B voice edits (steer-consent with dimension left open, the not-knowing discipline/competing-reads restoration, threading voice line — each paid by named cuts; voice measured ~1,281w, over tripwire) and Tier C (mirror-language LIMITS line, ~25w UNPAID — needs explicit founder budget ratification; two strikes: 'heat', the bill-attribution). Soak tests: pin-the-type lab persona; watch over-descent/probing-past-value, forced Manual rhymes, over-scoped composer titles. **Soak iteration 10 shipped** (2026-06-12, merge `a054b41`) — the partnership arc, Jeff's four-beat 'bring the user along' ruling: (1) THE DEAL — first-message block now sets the expectation once, early ('when something true shows up, I'll flag it; if it holds, it can go in your Manual; you decide') — SUPERSEDES the deliberate surprise-mechanism rule ('Don't explain checkpoints, the Manual, or the five layers'); (2) flag-and-park + seam in MECHANICS — material-ready ≠ now; flag in passing ('that's one for your Manual, holding onto it'), propose only at a seam (fixes 'I get a checkpoint while I'm still talking' — the old text fused readiness with timing); (3) steer-consent shipped (held-B3, dimension left open). Paid by three cuts (clean-nod clause, no-third-option dup, no-performance disclaimers collapsed); net voice ~+30w. HELD on trust-the-LLM: B1 static method text (Tier A brief hints already carry the method per-turn). Soak watch: the deal lands once and plainly on first sessions; flags appear mid-flow without derailing; checkpoints arrive at exhales; consent fires only at real pattern-pivots.
- **Onboarding first-contact trust pass** (2026-06-17) — from the framework-teaching strategy work. Verdict: never front-load the five-layer taxonomy (every prior attempt was an anti-pattern), but DO front-load the proposition + provenance + control promise in plain language; the framework itself is learned as an OUTPUT (the section name already shows on every checkpoint → the accumulating Manual → one orienting line at first Manual view). **SHIPPED:** a just-in-time control line on the entry-card screen ("Nothing's saved unless you say so. You decide what goes in your Manual.") — the applied-psychologist pressure-test found the control/autonomy promise sat AFTER first disclosure (THE DEAL gates on the user's first message) when this audience most needs it before; THE DEAL's modeled-line clause-joining dash split to periods. **Also shipped (compliance cleared 2026-06-17):** the SeedScreen rewrite — state provenance affirmatively ("Jove works from what you actually say, in your own words"), dropping "surfaces patterns using psychological frameworks" + the no-diagnosis negation from the trust paragraph (negation primes the feared frame for a pathologization-wary audience; keep no-diagnosis quarantined in the consent-frame compliance paragraph). Compliance call (Jeff, 2026-06-17): removing "psychological frameworks" from user-facing copy does NOT weaken the self-help-exemption — rules.md §Self-Help Exemption left intact, since the exemption rests on the frameworks being used internally as scaffolding, not on the gate screen naming them. **Experiment named (Hypothesis, underpowered at ~10 beta users):** A/B/C on the entry surface — current vs affirmative-provenance + early-control-line vs same-with-negation — primary outcome first-message disclosure depth. Picks up iteration-7's flagged "onboarding scope-setting line." **Validation (#4):** not a formal A/B at ~10 users — a qualitative within-subjects read of the next first sessions (does the opening message arrive more concrete/disclosing with the control line present?). **Resolved (#2, 2026-06-17):** trimmed THE DEAL's redundant "you decide what goes in" clause. The control gate is already stated twice pre-disclosure (consent screen + entry-card line) and enforced by the confirm card, so THE DEAL keeps only its flag→Manual partnership arc. Applied-psych review: the third restatement risks reading as managed-not-granted for this literal audience, and the processing-fluency benefit saturates by the second mention; the trim doesn't weaken the guarantee (Tier-1 "nothing without confirmation" still governs). **Collapsed to one screen (2026-06-17):** realizing the mobile-behavior audit's P3 + the persona drop, onboarding went from info → persona → seed (3 full-screen gates before first value) to a single SeedScreen consent screen — InfoScreens prose merged in, the clinical disclaimer de-duped to one mention + crisis pointer, and the persona-mode onboarding screen deleted. The rebuilt voice doesn't render persona deltas (composeTier2 is legacy-path only), so the pick had no live effect; `persona_modes` defaults to `["general"]` in the pipeline and stays settable in Settings for the Phase-5 ND layer. `InfoScreens.tsx` + `PersonaModeScreen.tsx` removed; net −521 lines; persona deltas/data untouched (settled keep).
- **Conversation-authenticity audit — NEXT / LATER backlog** (2026-06-03). The NOW block shipped (see Deployed Features, merge `a30f5ec`). Not yet built. **NEXT:** the "exception question" (Jove asks "when did this NOT happen?" to actively surface strengths — today Layer 1 only catches them by accident); make the composer BIND conditional by layer (strengths get conditions-that-bring-it-out, not a manufactured cost); raise the first-checkpoint HARD depth floor from `feeling`→`mechanism` in `persona-pipeline.ts` (the NOW block only depth-gated the soft brief, not the gate); loosen the composer SECONDARY VERB rule to allow embodied verbs that are the user's own words; add specific-affirmation as a sanctioned warmth move; convert voice rule R-21's "available shapes" menu into a Socratic through-line; build the simplest **"Moment" field** — a verbatim, dated example captured at checkpoint-confirm and rendered under the entry body (`intent.md:153` defines it in the entry anatomy; the schema never had it). The Moment field is the real, buildable core of the founder's "example matrix / examples completed over time" idea. **LATER:** let a clean reflection stand as a whole turn (soften voice rule R-4); soften the 3-entry "your manual has a working first version" exit language so it doesn't read as done; "add another moment over time" (cross-entry example accumulation) once the single Moment field proves out.
- **Checkpoint-quality batch — status** (2026-06-03, merges `5d80d86` + `70d7215`). Most of the held queue shipped or was cut after founder decisions. **SHIPPED:** A — headline verb carve-out + ALTITUDE-FIRST rule (the user's own banned-verb phrase, e.g. "lose myself", is now allowed as a title when it's their verbatim word; `validateHeadline` exported + 9 tests, was zero); E — save-phrase salience (lead the checkpoint rules with the exact "in your Manual" contract, name the "put that down" slip; regex untouched); F — autistic body-default reworded to "take a moment. How does that show up for you, physically or mentally?" (was the clipped "where in your body?"); B — composer draws a prior-entry connection without making it the new entry's spine. **CUT (founder):** #7 two-question-marks (more questions get the user to type more — a goal; only the frame-smuggling either/or matters and it's already covered); #2 layer-by-lead ("the starting point may not be the pattern; pull the best content"). **D SHIPPED 2026-06-12** as checkpoint split delivery (see Deployed Features ship log, merge `44bf2e6`) — built exactly along the lines sketched here (lead-in as its own `message_complete`, typing indicator covers the compose gap, mid-stream indicator gate), browser-verified live, with the `composition_latency` log read first as required (10.45s on the verification run — compose confirmed as the dominant wait). **Also held: G** — the "Moment" field (founder wants to discuss; it's the buildable core of his "example matrix" idea — see the NEXT/LATER backlog bullet above). Field note: `COMPOSITION_MODEL` is Opus 4.6, not Sonnet (comments fixed this batch).
- **Sharper-mirror — watch + NEXT/LATER** (2026-06-04, NOW bucket shipped, merge `8c07c8d`). **WATCH:** the conversational turn now runs Opus 4.7 on EVERY message (`PERSONA_MODEL` in `config.ts`) — a real cost/latency increase. Decision pending after the founder tests: keep wide-open, or gate Opus to complex/synthesis turns only (Sonnet for simple turns). The flip is also the A/B for "how much of the claude.ai-vs-Jove gap was model vs craft." **NEXT (not built):** variable-isolation + numbered-decomposition ("two things are tangled — let's pull them apart," ending in a conclusion not a choice) as first-class voice moves; lower the commit-a-read threshold below the current 3-turn gate + add mid-conversation thread-naming (kills the early-interview feel); a scannable CAPS/numbered register wired to the persona deltas for users who signal a format preference. **LATER:** prompt Jove to actively use the compressed older Manual entries as pattern-testing starting points + latch a within-session working hypothesis (the extraction layer generates one each cycle but never carries it forward) — the cheap down-payment on "it knows me"; then full cross-session memory + a between-session synthesis pass (the big roadmap item; the bulk of the "it knows me" feeling).
- **Drop the dead `so_what` column** — `manual_entries.so_what` (migration `20260513100000`) is written by nothing in app code (`confirmCheckpoint` never passes `p_so_what`, so it is always NULL). Slated for a separate DROP migration + RPC-signature revert; held out of the 2026-06-03 NOW block because dropping a column on live beta data is destructive and deserves its own deliberate step (verify zero non-null values first).

- **Two-layer engine plan — paused as candidate overbuild (ADR-045)** ([docs/reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md](reference/two-layer-engine-evaluation.md), reserved ADR-044 draft at [docs/reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md](reference/two-layer-engine-adr-draft.md)). The six-phase plan to replace the single-overloaded Jove call with monitor + tracker + selector + stripped generator. **Phase 0 (shadow monitor) shipped 2026-05-21 and closed 2026-05-27 — verdict: promote** (see [docs/architecture/phase-0-closeout.md](architecture/phase-0-closeout.md)): the `direction` read validated as load-bearing, `rupture` validated with a one-turn recovery lag, `scope` observed-but-not-validated. **The monitor implementation was then removed in full 2026-06-04 under ADR-045** — `monitor.ts`, the `monitor_reads` table (drop migration `20260604000000`), the `MONITOR_ENABLED`/`MONITOR_MODEL` flags, and the `/replay-monitor` skill no longer exist (a sensor consumed by nothing; the removal is also recorded under Deployed Features). **The roadmap and the selector that would have read the monitor (ADR-043 Decision 1) are paused as candidate overbuild — not being pursued, and not to be rebuilt by default.** Re-entry condition is consumer-first: name the consumer before building any sensor, and prefer detecting the signal inside the main Jove call (which already reads the whole conversation under Opus) over a separate watcher + table + selector. ADR-043 Decisions 2 & 3 (Surface ripeness threshold; dropping the hard `distinct_contexts >= 2` gate) shipped as live logic in `validateMaterialQuality` / `deriveCheckpointApproaching` and remain in force, independent of the monitor; Lock 1 and its upstream `deriveCheckpointApproaching` gate are likewise unaffected.
- Dark-mode redesign is fully shipped — all 9 phases plus follow-ups. See the Deployed Features entry.
- Light-mode redesign is fully shipped — all 8 phases. See the Deployed Features entry.
- **Checkpoint hardening plan** ([docs/reference/checkpoint-hardening-plan.md](reference/checkpoint-hardening-plan.md)) — substantially shipped as of 2026-05-15. Track 1 (Supabase CLI migrations + drift detection): shipped, post-squash baseline at `20260417000000_squash_baseline.sql` plus per-feature migrations. Track 2 (idempotent confirm RPC + unique constraint + transactional writes): shipped via `confirm_checkpoint_write` + `manual_entries_user_source_msg_uniq` partial unique index. Track 3 (client-side resilience): shipped — 3-attempt retry with backoff, error taxonomy, stream-interruption recovery via `loadManual()`. Track 4 (structured logs + `confirm_failures` table + admin health panel): shipped, panel lives at `/admin?section=health` → ConfirmHealthPanel. Track 5 (E2E integration test + schema drift canary): shipped, `.github/workflows/supabase-e2e.yml` runs against a Dockerized Supabase on every PR. Track 6 (Upstash + enforced rate limits): code shipped, rate-limit module hard-fails in production if env vars missing; Upstash provisioned 2026-06-02 — rate limiting now fully enforced in prod. 2026-05-15 follow-up closed the three remaining gaps surfaced by the beta audit — reject/refine/defer idempotency, rate-limit prod hard-fail, stream outcome log timing. See Deployed Features for the audit + fixes entry.
- **Retire `beta_allowlist` table** — the 2026-06-02 beta-access unification (commit `4844375`) moved the auth gate onto `waitlist.status='invited'` and backfilled the table, but left `beta_allowlist` physically in place as a rollback net. Follow-up: once prod login is confirmed working on the new gate, land a migration dropping `public.beta_allowlist`. No code reads it anymore. (Optional pre-drop check the reviewer flagged: dry-run the unify migration against a prod snapshot to confirm no legacy allowlist row predates the lowercase/trim CHECK.)
